--- START OF FILE: README.md ---

# NewsHub - Admin Dashboard Documentation

The NewsHub Admin Dashboard is a React-based single-page application designed for centralized management of the NewsHub platform. It provides administrators, editors, and authors with tools to manage articles, categories, users, media content (images and videos), comments, and global site settings.

Core technologies used:
*   **React**: For building the user interface.
*   **Vite**: As the build tool and development server.
*   **Redux Toolkit (RTK) & RTK Query**: For state management and efficient data fetching/caching.
*   **React Router DOM**: For client-side routing.
*   **TailwindCSS**: For styling (based on `index.css` and typical modern React project structure).
*   **Lucide React**: For icons.

## 2. Getting Started & Logging In

### Prerequisites
*   Node.js (v18.x or higher recommended)
*   npm (or yarn)

### Installation
1.  Clone the `newshub-dashboard` repository.
2.  Navigate to the project directory: `cd newshub-dashboard`
3.  Install dependencies: `npm install`
4.  **Environment Configuration**:
    Create a `.env` file in the root of the project. This file is used to store environment-specific variables. Add the following variable:
    ```env
    VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:7000
    ```
    Replace `http://localhost:7000` with the actual base URL of your NewsHub backend API. This URL is sourced from `src/utils/ApiBaseUrl.js`.
5.  Start the development server: `npm run dev`
    The application will typically be available at `http://localhost:5173` (or another port if 5173 is busy).

### Authentication
The dashboard requires users to authenticate to access its features. The main authentication panel is `src/Pages/AuthPanel/AuthPanel.jsx`.
*   **Login (`src/components/Login/Login.jsx`)**: Users log in with their email and password. Successful authentication stores user information (including a JWT token and role) in local storage and Redux state via `authSlice.js` (`setCredentials` action).
*   **Signup (`src/components/Signup/Signup.jsx`)**: New users can register.
*   **OTP Verification (`src/Pages/VerifyOtp/VerifyOtp.jsx`)**: Used for verifying user actions, likely email verification after signup or during password reset.
*   **Password Reset**:
    *   `src/Pages/SendOtp/SendOtp.jsx`: Initiates the password reset process by sending an OTP to the user's email.
    *   `src/Pages/ResetPassword/ResetPassword.jsx`: Allows users to set a new password after verifying the OTP.
All authentication operations (login, register, OTP handling, password reset) are managed by RTK Query endpoints defined in `src/_core/Slices/userSlice.js` (e.g., `useLoginMutation`, `useRegisterMutation`, etc., which use the `/api/auth` base path).

## 3. Dashboard Core Concepts & Layout

### Main Layout
The main dashboard interface is structured by `src/Pages/Dashboard/Dashboard.jsx`. It consists of:
*   A collapsible **Sidebar (`src/components/Sidebar/Sidebar.jsx`)** for navigation.
*   A top **Header (`src/components/Header/Header.jsx`)** which includes a search bar (client-side, for UI only) and quick links to notifications (placeholder) and profile settings. It also contains the toggle button for the mobile sidebar.
*   A content area where different dashboard pages are rendered via `Outlet` from React Router.

![Dashboard Layout Placeholder](./docs/dashboard_layout.png)

### Navigation
*   Primary navigation is handled by the `Sidebar.jsx` component.
*   Sidebar links are dynamically generated based on `src/Data/sidebarData.jsx`, which also incorporates role-based visibility using the `hasPermission` helper (from `src/Helper/hasPermission.js`).
*   Client-side routing is managed by `react-router-dom`. Route definitions are centralized in `src/Data/routesData.jsx` and the routing logic (including lazy loading with `Suspense`) is in `src/routes.jsx`.

### State Management
*   **Redux Toolkit (RTK)** is used for global state management. The store is configured in `src/_core/store.js`.
*   **`authSlice.js` (`src/_core/Slices/authSlice.js`)**: Manages authentication state. It stores `userInfo` (which includes user details like role and the JWT token) upon successful login. This information is persisted in local storage to maintain session across browser refreshes and is used by `apiSlice` for token injection.
*   **RTK Query**: Handles all server state: data fetching, caching, and mutations.
    *   **`apiSlice.js` (`src/_core/Slices/apiSlice.js`)**: The base API slice. It's configured with `fetchBaseQuery` which points to `VITE_API_BASE_URL` and automatically prepares headers to include the JWT token from local storage. It defines common `tagTypes` for cache invalidation (e.g., 'User', 'Articles', 'Article', 'Category', etc.).
    *   **Feature Slices**: Specific data types (e.g., articles, users, categories) have their endpoints defined in separate slice files (e.g., `articleSlice.js`, `userSlice.js`, `categorySlice.js`) which inject into the main `apiSlice`. This keeps API definitions modular.

### Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)
The dashboard implements RBAC to control access to different pages and features.
*   **Page Access**: `src/Helper/ProtectedRoutes.jsx` component wraps routes defined in `routesData.jsx`. It checks if the logged-in user (whose role is retrieved from `authSlice` via local storage) has the `requiredRole` (or a higher role based on hierarchy) for a given route using `hasPermission.js`. If not, the user is redirected to a "Not Found" page.
*   **Feature/Button Visibility**: `src/Helper/hasPermission.js` provides a function that checks if a user's role has the necessary privileges. This helper defines a role hierarchy (`superadmin` > `admin` > `editor` > `author` > `reader`) to grant cumulative permissions. This function is used throughout various components to conditionally render UI elements.

The defined roles are:
*   `superadmin`: Full access, including global settings.
*   `admin`: User management, content management.
*   `editor`: Article, category, comment, gallery, and video management.
*   `author`: Own article creation and management.
*   `reader`: Basic dashboard access (e.g., view own profile, dashboard home).

## 4. Module-wise Feature Guide

This section details the functionalities available within each module of the admin dashboard.

### Dashboard Home (`/dashboard`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/DashboardHome/DashboardHome.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Provides a statistical overview and quick insights into content performance and user engagement.
*   **Features**:
    *   **Statistical Cards**: Displays key metrics like "Total Articles", "Total Views", "Active Users", and "Engagement" with trend indicators. (Currently uses static sample data).
    *   **Charts (using `recharts`)**:
        *   Articles by Category (Pie Chart).
        *   Monthly Performance (Bar Chart - Articles Published).
        *   Weekly Visitors (Area Chart).
        (All charts currently use static sample data).
    *   **Recent Articles Table**: Lists sample recent articles using the reusable `MuiTable` component.
*   **Data Source**: Currently uses static sample data. For production, data would be fetched via RTK Query from relevant API endpoints.
*   **Available To**: All authenticated users (`reader` role and above).

### Article Management
Manages news articles. API interactions are defined in `src/_core/Slices/articleSlice.js`.

*   **All Articles Page (`/dashboard/articles`)**
    *   **Component**: `src/Pages/ArticlesPage/ArticlesPage.jsx`.
    *   **Purpose**: View, search, filter, and manage all articles. Fetches data from `/api/articles/admin/all`.
    *   **Features**:
        *   Grid/List view toggle.
        *   Search by title, category, author.
        *   Filters: Category, Status (Published, Draft, Pending Review, Archived).
        *   Sorting: Created Date, Updated Date, Title, Views.
        *   Pagination.
        *   Actions: Edit (navigates to edit page), Delete (uses `useDeleteArticleMutation` with confirmation). RBAC applies.
        *   Bulk actions (select multiple, delete, export - delete/export are placeholders).
        *   "New Article" button navigates to `/dashboard/create-article`.
    *   **Available To**: `reader`+ (view access controlled by `requiredRole`), full management by `editor`+.

*   **Create Article Page (`/dashboard/create-article`)**
    *   **Component**: `src/Pages/NewsArticle/NewsArticle.jsx`.
    *   **Purpose**: Form for creating new articles.
    *   **Features**: Title (auto-generates slug), content editor (`WordEditor.jsx` - Tiptap based), featured image upload (with preview), category/subcategory selection, tags (select or custom), status selection.
    *   **Submission**: Uses `useCreateArticleMutation` from `articleSlice.js` (Note: `makeRequest` wrapper was observed in component, but `useCreateArticleMutation` should be the target. The `articleSlice.js` reviewed previously was missing `createArticle` and `updateArticle` mutations; these are assumed to be added for full functionality).
    *   **Available To**: `reader`+ (as per route, ideally `author`+).

*   **Edit Article Page (`/dashboard/edit-article/:articleId`)**
    *   **Component**: `src/Pages/EditArticle/EditArticle.jsx`.
    *   **Purpose**: Form to edit existing articles.
    *   **Features**:
        *   Fetches article data using `useGetArticleByIdQuery`.
        *   Form fields similar to Create Article page.
        *   **Update**: Uses `useUpdateArticleMutation` (assumed to be added to `articleSlice.js`, takes `FormData` if featured image changes).
        *   **Version History & Rollback**:
            *   "History" button (RBAC: editor+) opens a modal listing article versions (`useGetArticleHistoryQuery`).
            *   Allows reverting to a previous version (`useRevertArticleMutation` with confirmation). Reverted articles become 'pending-review'.
        *   **Article Image Gallery**:
            *   Upload multiple images to the article's gallery (`useAddArticleImagesMutation`).
            *   Display and delete existing gallery images (`useDeleteArticleImageMutation` with confirmation).
            *   (RBAC: author for own non-published, editor+ for all).
    *   **Available To**: `author`+.

### Category Management (`/dashboard/categories`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/Categories/Categories.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Allows users to manage content categories and their hierarchy. API via `categorySlice.js`.
*   **Features**:
    *   Fetches categories from `/api/categories/admin/all` (`useGetCategoriesQuery`).
    *   Hierarchical display in `MuiTable` with indentation.
    *   Create/Edit via `CategoryForm` in a `ModalWrapper`: Name, Description, Parent Category, IsActive toggle. Uses `useCreateCategoryMutation` and `useUpdateCategoryMutation`.
    *   Delete action uses `useDeleteCategoryMutation` with confirmation. Backend handles restrictions.
*   **Available To**: `editor`, `admin`, `superadmin`.

### User Management (`/dashboard/users`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/UserManagement/UserManagement.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Manage user accounts. API via `userSlice.js`.
*   **Features**:
    *   Lists users from `/api/users` (`useGetUsersQuery`) in `MuiTable`: Avatar, Name, Email, Role, Status, Joined Date. Includes pagination, search, role filter.
    *   **Create User**: `CreateUserForm` modal. RBAC for role assignment (superadmin can create admins). Uses `useCreateUserMutation`.
    *   **Edit User**: Navigates to `/dashboard/users/edit/:userId`.
    *   **Delete User**: Uses `useDeleteUserMutation` with confirmation. RBAC for deletions.
*   **Available To**: `admin`, `superadmin`.

*   **Edit User Page (`/dashboard/users/edit/:userId`)**
    *   **Component**: `src/Pages/EditUser/EditUser.jsx`
    *   **Purpose**: Edit existing user details.
    *   **Features**: Fetches user with `useGetUserByIdQuery`. Form for Full Name, Bio, Is Verified (admin/superadmin only), Role (superadmin only, cannot change other superadmins). Uses `useUpdateUserMutation`.
    *   **Available To**: `admin` (for lower roles), `superadmin`.

### Profile Settings (`/dashboard/profile-settings`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/ProfilePage/ProfilePage.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Allows users to manage their own profile.
    *   (Note: Component currently uses mock data/API. Needs integration with `authSlice` for user info and `userSlice.js` for mutations like `useUpdateProfileMutation` for profile data + avatar, and `useChangePasswordMutation` for password changes. These specific hooks might need to be added or adapted in `userSlice.js`.)
*   **Features (planned/mocked)**:
    *   Update: Full Name, Username, Bio. Avatar upload.
    *   Security: Change password.
*   **Available To**: All authenticated users (route is `admin`, but sidebar implies general access if `profile-settings` matches a general path).

### Comments Moderation (`/dashboard/comments`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/CommentModeration/CommentModeration.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Manage user-submitted comments. API via `commentSlice.js`.
*   **Features**:
    *   Lists comments from `/api/comments/all` (`useGetCommentsQuery`) with pagination.
    *   Filters: Status (All, Approved, Pending), Article ID, User ID.
    *   `MuiTable` displays: Comment Content (truncated), Author (link), Article (link), Status (chip), Reported (icon if reports > 0), Date.
    *   **Actions (RBAC: editor+)**: Approve/Unapprove (`useUpdateCommentStatusMutation`), Delete (`useDeleteCommentMutation` with confirmation).
*   **Available To**: `editor`, `admin`, `superadmin`.

### Image Gallery Module (`/dashboard/galleries`)
Manages image galleries. API via `gallerySlice.js`.
*   **List Page (`/dashboard/galleries`)**: `GalleryList.jsx`
    *   Lists galleries from `/api/galleries/admin/all` (`useGetGalleriesQuery`).
    *   `MuiTable`: Thumbnail, Title, Status, Author, Image Count, Created At.
    *   Actions: Edit (navigates), Delete (`useDeleteGalleryMutation` with confirmation).
    *   "Create Gallery" button.
*   **Create Page (`/dashboard/galleries/create`)**: `CreateGallery.jsx`
    *   Form: Title, Description, Status, Tags. Multi-image uploader.
    *   Submits `FormData` via `useCreateGalleryMutation`.
*   **Edit Page (`/dashboard/galleries/edit/:galleryId`)**: `EditGallery.jsx`
    *   Fetches gallery with `useGetGalleryByIdQuery`.
    *   Form to update metadata (`useUpdateGalleryMutation`).
    *   Manage images: Display existing (with captions, delete option via `useDeleteGalleryImageMutation`), Add new images (`useAddGalleryImagesMutation`).
*   **Available To**: `editor`, `admin`, `superadmin`.

### Video Module (`/dashboard/videos`)
Manages video content. API via `videoSlice.js`.
*   **List Page (`/dashboard/videos`)**: `VideoList.jsx`
    *   Lists videos from `/api/videos/admin/all` (`useGetVideosQuery`).
    *   `MuiTable`: Thumbnail, Title, Status, Uploader, Duration, Views, Created At.
    *   Actions: Edit (navigates), Delete (`useDeleteVideoMutation` with confirmation).
    *   "Upload Video" button.
*   **Upload Page (`/dashboard/videos/upload`)**: `UploadVideo.jsx`
    *   Form: Title, Description, Status, Tags. Single video file uploader.
    *   Submits `FormData` via `useUploadVideoMutation`.
*   **Edit Page (`/dashboard/videos/edit/:videoId`)**: `EditVideo.jsx`
    *   Fetches video with `useGetVideoByIdQuery`.
    *   Form to update metadata (`useUpdateVideoMetadataMutation`). Displays current thumbnail. (Video replacement is not part of this form).
*   **Available To**: `editor`, `admin`, `superadmin`.

### Global Settings (`/dashboard/settings/global`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/GlobalSettings/GlobalSettings.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Manage site-wide settings. API via `settingsSlice.js`.
*   **Features**:
    *   Fetches settings from `/api/settings/global` (`useGetGlobalSettingsQuery`).
    *   Form: Site Name, Site Logo (upload new, preview, remove), Social Media Links (Facebook, Twitter, Instagram, LinkedIn), Maintenance Mode (toggle & message).
    *   Submits `FormData` (for logo) via `useUpdateGlobalSettingsMutation`.
*   **Available To**: `superadmin` only.

## 5. Reusable Components Library

The dashboard utilizes a set of reusable UI components, primarily located in `src/components/ui/` and exported via `src/components/components.js`.

*   **`Button.jsx`**: Versatile button component with variants (e.g., `outline`, `ghost`, `destructive`), sizes, loading state, and icon support.
*   **`Card.jsx`**: Container for grouping content. Often used with `Card.Header`, `Card.Body`, `Card.Footer` (though these subcomponents are not explicitly defined as separate files, they are used conceptually or as part of the `Card`'s structure).
*   **`Input.jsx`**: General-purpose input field supporting various types (text, email, password, file), labels, icons, helper text, error states. Can also render as a `textarea` via `isTextArea` prop.
*   **`Select.jsx`**: Dropdown component for selecting values. Supports options array, placeholder, search functionality (seen in `EditArticle.jsx`), and multi-select.
*   **`Heading.jsx`**: Standardized component for rendering page and section titles (h1, h2, etc.) with optional descriptions and action buttons.
*   **`ModalWrapper.jsx`**: Reusable modal dialog component for confirmations or displaying forms. Manages open/close state and provides slots for title, body, and footer actions.
*   **`MuiTable.jsx`**: A component likely built around Material-UI Table (or a similar library) for displaying tabular data. Features include custom cell rendering, pagination support, and integration with data fetching.
*   **`WordEditor.jsx`**: A Tiptap-based rich text editor for creating and editing formatted article content.
*   **`CircularProgressBox.jsx`**: (Assumed from usage, e.g., `GlobalSettings.jsx`) A component for displaying a circular loading indicator, often with centered text.
*   **`ArticlesCard.jsx` / `ArticlesItem.jsx`**: Specialized components for displaying articles in grid and list views respectively on the `ArticlesPage.jsx`.
*   **`Textarea.jsx`**: Explicitly used in `CreateGallery.jsx`, `EditGallery.jsx`, `GlobalSettings.jsx`. Likely a styled multi-line text input, possibly a variation of the main `Input.jsx` or a distinct component.
*   **`Switch.jsx`**: (Assumed from usage in `GlobalSettings.jsx`) A toggle switch component, used for boolean settings like Maintenance Mode.
*   **`List.jsx` / `ListItem.jsx`**: (Assumed from usage in `EditArticle.jsx` for history) Components for rendering lists, likely simple wrappers or styled list elements.
*   **`Typography.jsx`**: (Assumed from Material-UI usage in various components) Wrapper for text display, allowing consistent typography.

This library promotes UI consistency and reusability across the dashboard.

--- START OF FILE: code.py ---

import os
import argparse

# --- Configuration ---
EXCLUDED_DIRS = {
    '.git',
    'node_modules',
    '__pycache__',
    'dist',
    'build',
    '.vscode',
    '.idea',
    'test-reports'
}

EXCLUDED_EXTENSIONS = {
    '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.gif', '.bmp', '.ico', '.svg',
    '.pdf', '.doc', '.docx', '.xls', '.xlsx',
    '.zip', '.rar', '.gz', '.tar',
    '.exe', '.dll', '.so', '.o', '.jar',
    '.mp4', '.mov', '.avi',
    '.mp3', '.wav',
    '.lock'
}

def process_project(root_dir, output_file_path):
    """
    Project ko process karta hai: pehle structure banata hai, phir content.
    Yeh debugging messages bhi print karega.
    """
    print("--- SCRIPT STARTING --- ")
    print(f"Project Directory: {os.path.abspath(root_dir)}")
    print(f"Excluded Directories: {EXCLUDED_DIRS}")

    # Pehle purani output file ko delete kar dein (agar maujood hai)
    if os.path.exists(output_file_path):
        os.remove(output_file_path)
        print(f"Deleted old output file: {output_file_path}")

    with open(output_file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as output_file:
        # Step 1: Directory Structure
        output_file.write("PROJECT STRUCTURE:\n====================\n\n")
        
        # Step 2: Content
        output_file.write("FILE CONTENTS:\n==============\n\n")

        # os.walk se sab folders aur files ko process karein
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root_dir, topdown=True):
            
            # <<< DEBUGGING LINE >>>
            print(f"\nCurrently in directory: {root}")
            
            # Directories ko filter karein
            original_dirs = list(dirs) # Original list ko save karein
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d.lower() not in EXCLUDED_DIRS]
            
            # <<< DEBUGGING LINES >>>
            # Batayein kon se folders skip kiye gaye
            skipped_dirs = set(original_dirs) - set(dirs)
            if skipped_dirs:
                for skipped in skipped_dirs:
                    print(f"!!! SKIPPING Directory: {skipped} !!!")
            
            # Process files in the current valid directory
            # Pehle structure likhein
            level = root.replace(root_dir, '').count(os.sep)
            if level > 0:
                 output_file.write(f"{' ' * 4 * (level-1)}└── {os.path.basename(root)}/\n")
            
            sub_indent = ' ' * 4 * (level + 1)
            
            for f in sorted(files):
                 if not any(f.lower().endswith(ext) for ext in EXCLUDED_EXTENSIONS):
                    output_file.write(f"{sub_indent}├── {f}\n")
            
            # Phir content likhein
            for filename in sorted(files):
                if any(filename.lower().endswith(ext) for ext in EXCLUDED_EXTENSIONS):
                    continue

                file_path = os.path.join(root, filename)
                relative_path = os.path.relpath(file_path, root_dir)

                try:
                    with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
                        content = f.read()
                    
                    output_file.write(f"\n--- START OF FILE: {relative_path} ---\n\n")
                    output_file.write(content)
                    output_file.write(f"\n\n--- END OF FILE: {relative_path} ---\n\n" + "="*80 + "\n\n")

                except Exception as e:
                    output_file.write(f"\n--- Could not read file: {relative_path} (Error: {e}) ---\n\n" + "="*80 + "\n\n")

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Combine project files into a single text file with debugging.")
    parser.add_argument("project_dir", type=str, help="Project ka path.")
    parser.add_argument("output_file", type=str, nargs='?', default="combined_project_code.txt", help="Output file ka naam.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not os.path.isdir(args.project_dir):
        print(f"Error: Directory not found at '{args.project_dir}'")
        return
    
    process_project(args.project_dir, args.output_file)
    print(f"\n--- SCRIPT FINISHED ---")
    print(f"Output saved to '{args.output_file}'")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# NewsHub - Admin Dashboard Documentation

The NewsHub Admin Dashboard is a React-based single-page application designed for centralized management of the NewsHub platform. It provides administrators, editors, and authors with tools to manage articles, categories, users, media content (images and videos), comments, and global site settings.

Core technologies used:
*   **React**: For building the user interface.
*   **Vite**: As the build tool and development server.
*   **Redux Toolkit (RTK) & RTK Query**: For state management and efficient data fetching/caching.
*   **React Router DOM**: For client-side routing.
*   **TailwindCSS**: For styling (based on `index.css` and typical modern React project structure).
*   **Lucide React**: For icons.

## 2. Getting Started & Logging In

### Prerequisites
*   Node.js (v18.x or higher recommended)
*   npm (or yarn)

### Installation
1.  Clone the `newshub-dashboard` repository.
2.  Navigate to the project directory: `cd newshub-dashboard`
3.  Install dependencies: `npm install`
4.  **Environment Configuration**:
    Create a `.env` file in the root of the project. This file is used to store environment-specific variables. Add the following variable:
    ```env
    VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:7000
    ```
    Replace `http://localhost:7000` with the actual base URL of your NewsHub backend API. This URL is sourced from `src/utils/ApiBaseUrl.js`.
5.  Start the development server: `npm run dev`
    The application will typically be available at `http://localhost:5173` (or another port if 5173 is busy).

### Authentication
The dashboard requires users to authenticate to access its features. The main authentication panel is `src/Pages/AuthPanel/AuthPanel.jsx`.
*   **Login (`src/components/Login/Login.jsx`)**: Users log in with their email and password. Successful authentication stores user information (including a JWT token and role) in local storage and Redux state via `authSlice.js` (`setCredentials` action).
*   **Signup (`src/components/Signup/Signup.jsx`)**: New users can register.
*   **OTP Verification (`src/Pages/VerifyOtp/VerifyOtp.jsx`)**: Used for verifying user actions, likely email verification after signup or during password reset.
*   **Password Reset**:
    *   `src/Pages/SendOtp/SendOtp.jsx`: Initiates the password reset process by sending an OTP to the user's email.
    *   `src/Pages/ResetPassword/ResetPassword.jsx`: Allows users to set a new password after verifying the OTP.
All authentication operations (login, register, OTP handling, password reset) are managed by RTK Query endpoints defined in `src/_core/Slices/userSlice.js` (e.g., `useLoginMutation`, `useRegisterMutation`, etc., which use the `/api/auth` base path).

## 3. Dashboard Core Concepts & Layout

### Main Layout
The main dashboard interface is structured by `src/Pages/Dashboard/Dashboard.jsx`. It consists of:
*   A collapsible **Sidebar (`src/components/Sidebar/Sidebar.jsx`)** for navigation.
*   A top **Header (`src/components/Header/Header.jsx`)** which includes a search bar (client-side, for UI only) and quick links to notifications (placeholder) and profile settings. It also contains the toggle button for the mobile sidebar.
*   A content area where different dashboard pages are rendered via `Outlet` from React Router.

![Dashboard Layout Placeholder](./docs/dashboard_layout.png)

### Navigation
*   Primary navigation is handled by the `Sidebar.jsx` component.
*   Sidebar links are dynamically generated based on `src/Data/sidebarData.jsx`, which also incorporates role-based visibility using the `hasPermission` helper (from `src/Helper/hasPermission.js`).
*   Client-side routing is managed by `react-router-dom`. Route definitions are centralized in `src/Data/routesData.jsx` and the routing logic (including lazy loading with `Suspense`) is in `src/routes.jsx`.

### State Management
*   **Redux Toolkit (RTK)** is used for global state management. The store is configured in `src/_core/store.js`.
*   **`authSlice.js` (`src/_core/Slices/authSlice.js`)**: Manages authentication state. It stores `userInfo` (which includes user details like role and the JWT token) upon successful login. This information is persisted in local storage to maintain session across browser refreshes and is used by `apiSlice` for token injection.
*   **RTK Query**: Handles all server state: data fetching, caching, and mutations.
    *   **`apiSlice.js` (`src/_core/Slices/apiSlice.js`)**: The base API slice. It's configured with `fetchBaseQuery` which points to `VITE_API_BASE_URL` and automatically prepares headers to include the JWT token from local storage. It defines common `tagTypes` for cache invalidation (e.g., 'User', 'Articles', 'Article', 'Category', etc.).
    *   **Feature Slices**: Specific data types (e.g., articles, users, categories) have their endpoints defined in separate slice files (e.g., `articleSlice.js`, `userSlice.js`, `categorySlice.js`) which inject into the main `apiSlice`. This keeps API definitions modular.

### Role-Based Access Control (RBAC)
The dashboard implements RBAC to control access to different pages and features.
*   **Page Access**: `src/Helper/ProtectedRoutes.jsx` component wraps routes defined in `routesData.jsx`. It checks if the logged-in user (whose role is retrieved from `authSlice` via local storage) has the `requiredRole` (or a higher role based on hierarchy) for a given route using `hasPermission.js`. If not, the user is redirected to a "Not Found" page.
*   **Feature/Button Visibility**: `src/Helper/hasPermission.js` provides a function that checks if a user's role has the necessary privileges. This helper defines a role hierarchy (`superadmin` > `admin` > `editor` > `author` > `reader`) to grant cumulative permissions. This function is used throughout various components to conditionally render UI elements.

The defined roles are:
*   `superadmin`: Full access, including global settings.
*   `admin`: User management, content management.
*   `editor`: Article, category, comment, gallery, and video management.
*   `author`: Own article creation and management.
*   `reader`: Basic dashboard access (e.g., view own profile, dashboard home).

## 4. Module-wise Feature Guide

This section details the functionalities available within each module of the admin dashboard.

### Dashboard Home (`/dashboard`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/DashboardHome/DashboardHome.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Provides a statistical overview and quick insights into content performance and user engagement.
*   **Features**:
    *   **Statistical Cards**: Displays key metrics like "Total Articles", "Total Views", "Active Users", and "Engagement" with trend indicators. (Currently uses static sample data).
    *   **Charts (using `recharts`)**:
        *   Articles by Category (Pie Chart).
        *   Monthly Performance (Bar Chart - Articles Published).
        *   Weekly Visitors (Area Chart).
        (All charts currently use static sample data).
    *   **Recent Articles Table**: Lists sample recent articles using the reusable `MuiTable` component.
*   **Data Source**: Currently uses static sample data. For production, data would be fetched via RTK Query from relevant API endpoints.
*   **Available To**: All authenticated users (`reader` role and above).

### Article Management
Manages news articles. API interactions are defined in `src/_core/Slices/articleSlice.js`.

*   **All Articles Page (`/dashboard/articles`)**
    *   **Component**: `src/Pages/ArticlesPage/ArticlesPage.jsx`.
    *   **Purpose**: View, search, filter, and manage all articles. Fetches data from `/api/articles/admin/all`.
    *   **Features**:
        *   Grid/List view toggle.
        *   Search by title, category, author.
        *   Filters: Category, Status (Published, Draft, Pending Review, Archived).
        *   Sorting: Created Date, Updated Date, Title, Views.
        *   Pagination.
        *   Actions: Edit (navigates to edit page), Delete (uses `useDeleteArticleMutation` with confirmation). RBAC applies.
        *   Bulk actions (select multiple, delete, export - delete/export are placeholders).
        *   "New Article" button navigates to `/dashboard/create-article`.
    *   **Available To**: `reader`+ (view access controlled by `requiredRole`), full management by `editor`+.

*   **Create Article Page (`/dashboard/create-article`)**
    *   **Component**: `src/Pages/NewsArticle/NewsArticle.jsx`.
    *   **Purpose**: Form for creating new articles.
    *   **Features**: Title (auto-generates slug), content editor (`WordEditor.jsx` - Tiptap based), featured image upload (with preview), category/subcategory selection, tags (select or custom), status selection.
    *   **Submission**: Uses `useCreateArticleMutation` from `articleSlice.js` (Note: `makeRequest` wrapper was observed in component, but `useCreateArticleMutation` should be the target. The `articleSlice.js` reviewed previously was missing `createArticle` and `updateArticle` mutations; these are assumed to be added for full functionality).
    *   **Available To**: `reader`+ (as per route, ideally `author`+).

*   **Edit Article Page (`/dashboard/edit-article/:articleId`)**
    *   **Component**: `src/Pages/EditArticle/EditArticle.jsx`.
    *   **Purpose**: Form to edit existing articles.
    *   **Features**:
        *   Fetches article data using `useGetArticleByIdQuery`.
        *   Form fields similar to Create Article page.
        *   **Update**: Uses `useUpdateArticleMutation` (assumed to be added to `articleSlice.js`, takes `FormData` if featured image changes).
        *   **Version History & Rollback**:
            *   "History" button (RBAC: editor+) opens a modal listing article versions (`useGetArticleHistoryQuery`).
            *   Allows reverting to a previous version (`useRevertArticleMutation` with confirmation). Reverted articles become 'pending-review'.
        *   **Article Image Gallery**:
            *   Upload multiple images to the article's gallery (`useAddArticleImagesMutation`).
            *   Display and delete existing gallery images (`useDeleteArticleImageMutation` with confirmation).
            *   (RBAC: author for own non-published, editor+ for all).
    *   **Available To**: `author`+.

### Category Management (`/dashboard/categories`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/Categories/Categories.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Allows users to manage content categories and their hierarchy. API via `categorySlice.js`.
*   **Features**:
    *   Fetches categories from `/api/categories/admin/all` (`useGetCategoriesQuery`).
    *   Hierarchical display in `MuiTable` with indentation.
    *   Create/Edit via `CategoryForm` in a `ModalWrapper`: Name, Description, Parent Category, IsActive toggle. Uses `useCreateCategoryMutation` and `useUpdateCategoryMutation`.
    *   Delete action uses `useDeleteCategoryMutation` with confirmation. Backend handles restrictions.
*   **Available To**: `editor`, `admin`, `superadmin`.

### User Management (`/dashboard/users`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/UserManagement/UserManagement.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Manage user accounts. API via `userSlice.js`.
*   **Features**:
    *   Lists users from `/api/users` (`useGetUsersQuery`) in `MuiTable`: Avatar, Name, Email, Role, Status, Joined Date. Includes pagination, search, role filter.
    *   **Create User**: `CreateUserForm` modal. RBAC for role assignment (superadmin can create admins). Uses `useCreateUserMutation`.
    *   **Edit User**: Navigates to `/dashboard/users/edit/:userId`.
    *   **Delete User**: Uses `useDeleteUserMutation` with confirmation. RBAC for deletions.
*   **Available To**: `admin`, `superadmin`.

*   **Edit User Page (`/dashboard/users/edit/:userId`)**
    *   **Component**: `src/Pages/EditUser/EditUser.jsx`
    *   **Purpose**: Edit existing user details.
    *   **Features**: Fetches user with `useGetUserByIdQuery`. Form for Full Name, Bio, Is Verified (admin/superadmin only), Role (superadmin only, cannot change other superadmins). Uses `useUpdateUserMutation`.
    *   **Available To**: `admin` (for lower roles), `superadmin`.

### Profile Settings (`/dashboard/profile-settings`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/ProfilePage/ProfilePage.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Allows users to manage their own profile.
    *   (Note: Component currently uses mock data/API. Needs integration with `authSlice` for user info and `userSlice.js` for mutations like `useUpdateProfileMutation` for profile data + avatar, and `useChangePasswordMutation` for password changes. These specific hooks might need to be added or adapted in `userSlice.js`.)
*   **Features (planned/mocked)**:
    *   Update: Full Name, Username, Bio. Avatar upload.
    *   Security: Change password.
*   **Available To**: All authenticated users (route is `admin`, but sidebar implies general access if `profile-settings` matches a general path).

### Comments Moderation (`/dashboard/comments`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/CommentModeration/CommentModeration.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Manage user-submitted comments. API via `commentSlice.js`.
*   **Features**:
    *   Lists comments from `/api/comments/all` (`useGetCommentsQuery`) with pagination.
    *   Filters: Status (All, Approved, Pending), Article ID, User ID.
    *   `MuiTable` displays: Comment Content (truncated), Author (link), Article (link), Status (chip), Reported (icon if reports > 0), Date.
    *   **Actions (RBAC: editor+)**: Approve/Unapprove (`useUpdateCommentStatusMutation`), Delete (`useDeleteCommentMutation` with confirmation).
*   **Available To**: `editor`, `admin`, `superadmin`.

### Image Gallery Module (`/dashboard/galleries`)
Manages image galleries. API via `gallerySlice.js`.
*   **List Page (`/dashboard/galleries`)**: `GalleryList.jsx`
    *   Lists galleries from `/api/galleries/admin/all` (`useGetGalleriesQuery`).
    *   `MuiTable`: Thumbnail, Title, Status, Author, Image Count, Created At.
    *   Actions: Edit (navigates), Delete (`useDeleteGalleryMutation` with confirmation).
    *   "Create Gallery" button.
*   **Create Page (`/dashboard/galleries/create`)**: `CreateGallery.jsx`
    *   Form: Title, Description, Status, Tags. Multi-image uploader.
    *   Submits `FormData` via `useCreateGalleryMutation`.
*   **Edit Page (`/dashboard/galleries/edit/:galleryId`)**: `EditGallery.jsx`
    *   Fetches gallery with `useGetGalleryByIdQuery`.
    *   Form to update metadata (`useUpdateGalleryMutation`).
    *   Manage images: Display existing (with captions, delete option via `useDeleteGalleryImageMutation`), Add new images (`useAddGalleryImagesMutation`).
*   **Available To**: `editor`, `admin`, `superadmin`.

### Video Module (`/dashboard/videos`)
Manages video content. API via `videoSlice.js`.
*   **List Page (`/dashboard/videos`)**: `VideoList.jsx`
    *   Lists videos from `/api/videos/admin/all` (`useGetVideosQuery`).
    *   `MuiTable`: Thumbnail, Title, Status, Uploader, Duration, Views, Created At.
    *   Actions: Edit (navigates), Delete (`useDeleteVideoMutation` with confirmation).
    *   "Upload Video" button.
*   **Upload Page (`/dashboard/videos/upload`)**: `UploadVideo.jsx`
    *   Form: Title, Description, Status, Tags. Single video file uploader.
    *   Submits `FormData` via `useUploadVideoMutation`.
*   **Edit Page (`/dashboard/videos/edit/:videoId`)**: `EditVideo.jsx`
    *   Fetches video with `useGetVideoByIdQuery`.
    *   Form to update metadata (`useUpdateVideoMetadataMutation`). Displays current thumbnail. (Video replacement is not part of this form).
*   **Available To**: `editor`, `admin`, `superadmin`.

### Global Settings (`/dashboard/settings/global`)
*   **Component**: `src/Pages/GlobalSettings/GlobalSettings.jsx`
*   **Purpose**: Manage site-wide settings. API via `settingsSlice.js`.
*   **Features**:
    *   Fetches settings from `/api/settings/global` (`useGetGlobalSettingsQuery`).
    *   Form: Site Name, Site Logo (upload new, preview, remove), Social Media Links (Facebook, Twitter, Instagram, LinkedIn), Maintenance Mode (toggle & message).
    *   Submits `FormData` (for logo) via `useUpdateGlobalSettingsMutation`.
*   **Available To**: `superadmin` only.

## 5. Reusable Components Library

The dashboard utilizes a set of reusable UI components, primarily located in `src/components/ui/` and exported via `src/components/components.js`.

*   **`Button.jsx`**: Versatile button component with variants (e.g., `outline`, `ghost`, `destructive`), sizes, loading state, and icon support.
*   **`Card.jsx`**: Container for grouping content. Often used with `Card.Header`, `Card.Body`, `Card.Footer` (though these subcomponents are not explicitly defined as separate files, they are used conceptually or as part of the `Card`'s structure).
*   **`Input.jsx`**: General-purpose input field supporting various types (text, email, password, file), labels, icons, helper text, error states. Can also render as a `textarea` via `isTextArea` prop.
*   **`Select.jsx`**: Dropdown component for selecting values. Supports options array, placeholder, search functionality (seen in `EditArticle.jsx`), and multi-select.
*   **`Heading.jsx`**: Standardized component for rendering page and section titles (h1, h2, etc.) with optional descriptions and action buttons.
*   **`ModalWrapper.jsx`**: Reusable modal dialog component for confirmations or displaying forms. Manages open/close state and provides slots for title, body, and footer actions.
*   **`MuiTable.jsx`**: A component likely built around Material-UI Table (or a similar library) for displaying tabular data. Features include custom cell rendering, pagination support, and integration with data fetching.
*   **`WordEditor.jsx`**: A Tiptap-based rich text editor for creating and editing formatted article content.
*   **`CircularProgressBox.jsx`**: (Assumed from usage, e.g., `GlobalSettings.jsx`) A component for displaying a circular loading indicator, often with centered text.
*   **`ArticlesCard.jsx` / `ArticlesItem.jsx`**: Specialized components for displaying articles in grid and list views respectively on the `ArticlesPage.jsx`.
*   **`Textarea.jsx`**: Explicitly used in `CreateGallery.jsx`, `EditGallery.jsx`, `GlobalSettings.jsx`. Likely a styled multi-line text input, possibly a variation of the main `Input.jsx` or a distinct component.
*   **`Switch.jsx`**: (Assumed from usage in `GlobalSettings.jsx`) A toggle switch component, used for boolean settings like Maintenance Mode.
*   **`List.jsx` / `ListItem.jsx`**: (Assumed from usage in `EditArticle.jsx` for history) Components for rendering lists, likely simple wrappers or styled list elements.
*   **`Typography.jsx`**: (Assumed from Material-UI usage in various components) Wrapper for text display, allowing consistent typography.

This library promotes UI consistency and reusability across the dashboard.

--- START OF FILE: src\routes.jsx ---

// --- START OF FILE: src/routes.jsx ---
import React, { Suspense } from "react";
import { Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";
import ProtectedRoutes from "./Helper/ProtectedRoutes";
import routesData from "./Data/routesData";

// Helper to render routes
const renderRoutes = (routes, parentPath = "") => {
    return routes.map((route, i) => {
        // Humne 'key' ko routeProps se alag kar liya hai.
        const { path, element, requiredRole, index, children } = route;
        const key = `${parentPath}-${path || i}`; // Make a more unique key

        const routeProps = {
            // key ab yahan nahi hai
            path: index ? undefined : path,
            index: index || false,
            element: requiredRole
                ? (
                    <ProtectedRoutes requiredRole={requiredRole}>
                        {element}
                    </ProtectedRoutes>
                )
                : element,
        };

        if (children) {
            return (
                // `key` ko seedha yahan pass kiya gaya hai
                <Route key={key} path={path} element={element}>
                    {renderRoutes(children, path)}
                </Route>
            );
        }

        // `key` ko seedha yahan pass kiya gaya hai
        return <Route key={key} {...routeProps} />;
    });
};

const AppRoutes = () => {
    const routes = routesData();

    return (
        <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-4">Loading...</div>}>
            <Routes>
                {renderRoutes(routes)}
            </Routes>
        </Suspense>
    );
};

export default AppRoutes;
// --- END OF FILE: src/routes.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\components\ArticlesCard\ArticlesCard.jsx ---

import React, { useState, Fragment } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";
import { toast } from "react-toastify";
import { Menu, Transition } from '@headlessui/react';

// --- Icon Imports ---
import {
    Calendar, Edit, ExternalLink, Eye, FileText, Pin, PinOff, MoreVertical,
    Star, Award, Zap, Trash2, Tag, User, Clock,
    LucideHighlighter,
    PenBox
} from "lucide-react";

// --- Component and Helper Imports ---
import { Card } from "../components";
import ConfirmModal from "../ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal";
import DropdownMenu from "../DropdownMenu/DropdownMenu";
import {
    useDeleteArticleMutation,
    usePinArticleMutation,
    useToggleFeaturedStatusMutation,
    useToggleTopHeadlineStatusMutation,
    useToggleEditorChoiceStatusMutation,
    useToggleHighlightStatusMutation,
    useToggleOpinionMutation,
} from "../../_core/Slices/articleSlice.js";
import { hasPermission } from "../../Helper/hasPermission.js";
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';

// --- Main ArticlesCard Component ---
const ArticlesCard = ({ article, onActionComplete }) => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    // --- State for Modals ---
    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);

    // --- RTK Query Mutation Hooks ---
    const [deleteArticle, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteArticleMutation();
    const [togglePin, { isLoading: isPinning }] = usePinArticleMutation();
    const [toggleFeatured, { isLoading: isFeaturing }] = useToggleFeaturedStatusMutation();
    const [toggleTopHeadline, { isLoading: isTogglingHeadline }] = useToggleTopHeadlineStatusMutation();
    const [toggleEditorChoice, { isLoading: isTogglingChoice }] = useToggleEditorChoiceStatusMutation();
    const [toggleHighlightStatus, { isLoading: isTogglingHighlight }] = useToggleHighlightStatusMutation()
    const [toggleOpinion, { isLoading: isTogglingOpinion }] = useToggleOpinionMutation()

    // --- Helper Functions ---
    const formatDate = (dateString) => new Date(dateString).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { year: "numeric", month: "short", day: "numeric" });
    const getStatusColor = (status) => {
        switch (status) { case "published": return "bg-green-100 text-green-800"; case "draft": return "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800"; case "pending-review": return "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800"; default: return "bg-gray-100 text-gray-800"; }
    };
    const getStatusIcon = (status) => { switch (status) { case "published": return <Eye size={14} />; case "draft": return <Edit size={14} />; case "pending-review": return <Clock size={14} />; default: return <FileText size={14} />; } };

    // --- API Handlers ---
    const handleToggle = (toggleFunc, articleId, actionName) => {
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: toggleFunc,
            data: articleId,
            toast: toast,

        });
    };

    const confirmDelete = () => {
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: deleteArticle,
            data: article._id,
            toast: toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
                if (onActionComplete) onActionComplete();
            },
            onError: () => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false),
        });
    };

    // --- Dropdown Menu Items ---
    const canManage = hasPermission("editor");
    const isAnyToggleLoading = isPinning || isFeaturing || isTogglingHeadline || isTogglingChoice || isTogglingHighlight;

    const dropdownItems = [
        // Group 1: Standard Actions
        [
            { label: 'Edit', icon: <Edit size={16} />, onClick: () => navigate(`/dashboard/edit-article/${article.slug}`) },
            { label: 'View Public', icon: <ExternalLink size={16} />, onClick: () => window.open(`/article/${article.slug}`, "_blank"), disabled: article.status !== 'published' },
        ],
    ];

    if (canManage) {
        dropdownItems.push(
            // Group 2: Toggles
            [
                { label: article.isPinned ? 'Unpin Article' : 'Pin Article', icon: <Pin size={16} />, onClick: () => handleToggle(togglePin, article._id, 'pin'), disabled: isAnyToggleLoading },
                { label: article.isFeatured ? 'Unset as Featured' : 'Set as Featured', icon: <Star size={16} />, onClick: () => handleToggle(toggleFeatured, article._id, 'featured'), disabled: isAnyToggleLoading },
                { label: article.isTopHeadline ? 'Remove from Headlines' : 'Set as Top Headline', icon: <Zap size={16} />, onClick: () => handleToggle(toggleTopHeadline, article._id, 'top headline'), disabled: isAnyToggleLoading },
                { label: article.isEditorChoice ? "Remove Editor's Choice" : "Set Editor's Choice", icon: <Award size={16} />, onClick: () => handleToggle(toggleEditorChoice, article._id, "editor's choice"), disabled: isAnyToggleLoading },
                { label: article.isHighlighted ? "Remove from Highlights" : "Set as Highlights", icon: <LucideHighlighter size={16} />, onClick: () => handleToggle(toggleHighlightStatus, article._id, "editor's choice"), disabled: isAnyToggleLoading },
                { label: article.isOpinion ? "Remove from Opinion" : "Set as Opinion", icon: <LucideHighlighter size={16} />, onClick: () => handleToggle(toggleOpinion, article._id, "editor's choice"), disabled: isAnyToggleLoading },
            ],
            // Group 3: Danger Zone
            [
                { label: 'Delete', icon: <Trash2 size={16} />, className: 'text-red-600', onClick: () => setIsDeleteModalOpen(true) },
            ]
        );
    }


    return (
        <>
            <Card padding='none' className="group hover:shadow-lg transition-shadow duration-300  flex flex-col">
                <div className="relative">
                    <Link to={`/article/${article.slug}`}>
                        <div className="w-full h-48 bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-t-md">
                            {article.featuredImage?.url ? (
                                <img src={article.featuredImage.url} alt={article.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300 rounded-t-md" />
                            ) : (
                                <FileText className="size-12 text-gray-400" />
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </Link>

                    <div className="absolute top-3 left-3">
                        <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded-full ${getStatusColor(article.status)}`}>
                            {getStatusIcon(article.status)}
                            <span className="ml-1.5">{article.status.replace("-", " ")}</span>
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Indicators for special flags */}
                    <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 flex flex-col items-end gap-2">
                        {article.isPinned && <div className="p-1.5 bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-full shadow-lg" title="Pinned"><Pin size={14} /></div>}
                        {article.isFeatured && <div className="p-1.5 bg-yellow-500 text-white rounded-full shadow-lg" title="Featured"><Star size={14} /></div>}
                        {article.isTopHeadline && <div className="p-1.5 bg-orange-500 text-white rounded-full shadow-lg" title="Top Headline"><Zap size={14} /></div>}
                        {article.isEditorChoice && <div className="p-1.5 bg-purple-500 text-white rounded-full shadow-lg" title="Editor's Choice"><Award size={14} /></div>}
                        {article.isHighlighted && <div className="p-1.5 bg-red-500 text-white rounded-full shadow-lg" title="Highlighted"><LucideHighlighter size={14} /></div>}
                        {article.isOpinion && <div className="p-1.5 bg-green-500 text-white rounded-full shadow-lg" title="Highlighted"><PenBox size={14} /></div>}
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-4 flex flex-col flex-grow">
                    <div className="mb-2">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center text-xs text-gray-500">
                            <Tag className="size-3 mr-1" />
                            {article.category?.name || 'Uncategorized'}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-2 line-clamp-2 group-hover:text-primary transition-colors flex-grow">
                        <Link to={`/article/${article.slug}`}>{article.title}</Link>
                    </h3>

                    <div className="flex items-center text-xs text-gray-500 mb-4">
                        <User className="size-3 mr-1" />
                        <Link to={`/dashboard/articles-by-author/${article.author?._id}`} className="mr-3 hover:underline">{article.author?.username || 'N/A'}</Link>
                        <Calendar className="size-3 mr-1" />
                        <span>{formatDate(article.createdAt)}</span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 mt-auto">
                        <div className="flex items-center text-xs text-gray-500"><Eye className="size-4 mr-1" /> {article.views || 0}</div>
                        <DropdownMenu items={dropdownItems} />
                    </div>
                </div>
            </Card>

            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                title="Confirm Delete Article"
                onConfirm={confirmDelete}
                isLoading={isDeleting}
                variant={"danger"}
            >
                Are you sure you want to permanently delete "<strong>{article?.title}</strong>"? This action cannot be undone.
            </ConfirmModal>
        </>
    );
};

export default ArticlesCard;

--- START OF FILE: src\components\ArticlesItem\ArticlesItem.jsx ---

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import {
    Calendar, Clock, Copy, Edit, ExternalLink, Eye, FileText, Share2, Tag, Trash2, User, Pin, PinOff
} from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card } from "../components";
import ConfirmModal from "../ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal";
// Using ConfirmModal
import { useDeleteArticleMutation, usePinArticleMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/articleSlice.js";
import { hasPermission } from "../../Helper/hasPermission.js";

const ArticlesItem = ({ article, onActionComplete }) => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { userInfo } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);

    // --- State for Modals ---
    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isPinModalOpen, setIsPinModalOpen] = useState(false);

    // --- RTK Query Mutation Hooks ---
    const [deleteArticle, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteArticleMutation();
    const [pinArticle, { isLoading: isPinning }] = usePinArticleMutation();

    // --- Helper Functions ---
    const formatDate = (dateString) => new Date(dateString).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { year: "numeric", month: "short", day: "numeric" });
    const getStatusColor = (status) => {
        switch (status) {
            case "published": return "bg-green-100 text-green-800";
            case "draft": return "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800";
            case "pending-review": return "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800";
            default: return "bg-gray-100 text-gray-800";
        }
    };
    const getStatusIcon = (status) => {
        switch (status) {
            case "published": return <Eye className="size-3" />;
            case "draft": return <Edit className="size-3" />;
            case "pending-review": return <Clock className="size-3" />;
            default: return <FileText className="size-3" />;
        }
    };

    // --- API Handlers ---
    const confirmDelete = async () => {
        try {
            await deleteArticle(article._id).unwrap();
            toast.success("Article deleted successfully!");
            if (onActionComplete) onActionComplete();
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || "Failed to delete article.");
        } finally {
            setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
        }
    };

    const confirmTogglePin = async () => {
        try {
            await pinArticle(article._id).unwrap();
            toast.success(`Article successfully ${article.isPinned ? 'unpinned' : 'pinned'}!`);
            if (onActionComplete) onActionComplete();
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || "Failed to update pin status.");
        } finally {
            setIsPinModalOpen(false);
        }
    };

    const handleShare = async () => {
        const shareUrl = `${window.location.origin}/article/${article.slug}`;
        try {
            await navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl);
            toast.info("Article link copied to clipboard!");
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error("Could not copy link.");
        }
    };

    const canManage = hasPermission('editor');

    return (
        <>
            <Card className="group hover:shadow-md transition-shadow duration-200 overflow-auto">
                <div className="p-4 flex items-start space-x-4">
                    {/* Image Placeholder */}
                    <Link to={`/dashboard/edit-article/${article.slug}`} className="flex-shrink-0">
                        <div className="w-24 h-24 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                            {article.featuredImage?.url ? (
                                <img src={article.featuredImage.url} alt={article.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                            ) : (
                                <FileText className="size-8 text-gray-400" />
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </Link>

                    {/* Main Content */}
                    <div className="lg:flex-1 w-full">
                        <div className="">
                            <div className="flex-1">
                                <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg mb-1 group-hover:text-primary transition-colors ">
                                    <Link to={`/dashboard/edit-article/${article.slug}`}>{article.title}</Link>
                                </h3>

                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">
                                    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${getStatusColor(article.status)}`}>
                                        {getStatusIcon(article.status)}
                                        <span className="ml-1">{article.status.replace("-", " ")}</span>
                                    </span>
                                    <span className="flex items-center"><Tag className="size-3 mr-1" />{article.category?.name || 'N/A'}</span>
                                    <span className="flex items-center"><User className="size-3 mr-1" />{article.author?.username || 'N/A'}</span>
                                    <span className="flex items-center"><Eye className="size-3 mr-1" />{article.views || 0} views</span>
                                </div>

                                <div className="flex items-center space-x-4 text-xs text-gray-500">
                                    <span className="flex items-center"><Calendar className="size-3 mr-1" />Created: {formatDate(article.createdAt)}</span>
                                    {article.publishedAt && <span className="flex items-center"><Clock className="size-3 mr-1" />Published: {formatDate(article.publishedAt)}</span>}
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            {/* Action Buttons */}
                            <div className="flex  mt-3 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                                <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => navigate(`/dashboard/edit-article/${article.slug}`)} title="Edit Article"><Edit className="size-4" /></Button>

                                {canManage && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setIsPinModalOpen(true)} title={article.isPinned ? "Unpin" : "Pin"}>{article.isPinned ? <PinOff className="size-4 text-orange-500" /> : <Pin className="size-4" />}</Button>}

                                {article.status === "published" && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" title="View Public Article" onClick={() => window.open(`/article/${article.slug}`, "_blank")}><ExternalLink className="size-4" /></Button>}

                                <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={handleShare} title="Copy Link"><Share2 className="size-4" /></Button>
                                {canManage && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" className="text-red-600" onClick={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(true)} title="Delete Article"><Trash2 className="size-4" /></Button>}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </Card>

            {/* Modals for Confirmation */}
            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={confirmDelete}
                isLoading={isDeleting}
                title="Delete Article"
            >
                Are you sure you want to permanently delete "<strong>{article.title}</strong>"?
            </ConfirmModal>

            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isPinModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => setIsPinModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={confirmTogglePin}
                isLoading={isPinning}
                title={article.isPinned ? "Unpin Article" : "Pin Article"}
            >
                Are you sure you want to <strong>{article.isPinned ? 'unpin' : 'pin'}</strong> this article?
            </ConfirmModal>
        </>
    );
};

export default ArticlesItem;

// src/components/ConfirmModal.jsx

import React from "react";
import Modal from "react-modal";
import { X, AlertTriangle, HelpCircle, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";

const ConfirmModal = ({
  isOpen,
  onRequestClose,
  onConfirm,
  title,
  children,
  confirmText = "Confirm",
  cancelText = "Cancel",
  isLoading = false,
  variant = "danger", // Defaulting to 'danger' as it's a common use-case
}) => {
  // A richer configuration for a more professional look
  const variantConfig = {
    primary: {
      IconComponent: HelpCircle,
      headerBg: "bg-blue-500",
      buttonClasses:
        "bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 focus-visible:ring-blue-500",
    },
    danger: {
      IconComponent: AlertTriangle,
      headerBg: "bg-red-800",
      buttonClasses: "bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 focus-visible:ring-red-500",
    },
    success: {
      IconComponent: CheckCircle2,
      headerBg: "bg-green-500",
      buttonClasses:
        "bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 focus-visible:ring-green-500",
    },
  };

  const selectedVariant = variantConfig[variant] || variantConfig.primary;
  const { IconComponent, headerBg, buttonClasses } = selectedVariant;

  const modalAnimation = `
        @keyframes fadeInScaleUp {
            from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(-20px) scale(0.98); }
            to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0) scale(1); }
        }
    `;

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onRequestClose={() => !isLoading && onRequestClose()}
      contentLabel={title}
      className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center p-4"
      overlayClassName="fixed inset-0 bg-gray-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm z-[9999] transition-opacity duration-300"
      ariaHideApp={false}
    >
      <style>{modalAnimation}</style>
      <div
        className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden flex flex-col"
        style={{
          animation:
            "fadeInScaleUp 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) forwards",
        }}
        role="alertdialog"
        aria-labelledby="modal-title"
        aria-describedby="modal-description"
      >
        {/* Close Button */}
        <button
          onClick={onRequestClose}
          disabled={isLoading}
          className="absolute top-3 right-3 p-1.5 rounded-full text-gray-200 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/20 transition-colors z-10"
          aria-label="Close modal"
        >
          <X size={20} />
        </button>

        {/* Header with Icon */}
        <div
          className={`relative h-20 flex items-center justify-center ${headerBg}`}
        >
          <IconComponent className="text-white" size={48} aria-hidden="true" />
        </div>

        {/* Body Content */}
        <div className="p-6 text-center">
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800" id="modal-title">
            {title}
          </h3>
          {children && (
            <div className="mt-2">
              <p className="text-base text-gray-600" id="modal-description">
                {children}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer with Action Buttons */}
        <div className="px-6 py-4 bg-gray-50 flex flex-col-reverse sm:flex-row sm:justify-end gap-3 border-t border-gray-200">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onRequestClose}
            disabled={isLoading}
            className="inline-flex w-full justify-center rounded-lg cursor-pointer bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-inset ring-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-100 disabled:opacity-50 sm:w-auto"
          >
            {cancelText}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onConfirm}
            disabled={isLoading}
            className={`inline-flex w-full justify-center cursor-pointer  items-center rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-sm transition-colors duration-150 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 disabled:bg-gray-400 disabled:cursor-not-allowed sm:w-auto ${buttonClasses}`}
          >
            {isLoading ? (
              <>
                <svg
                  className="animate-spin -ml-1 mr-3 h-5 w-5 text-white"
                  xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                  fill="none"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                >
                  <circle
                    className="opacity-25"
                    cx="12"
                    cy="12"
                    r="10"
                    stroke="currentColor"
                    strokeWidth="4"
                  ></circle>
                  <path
                    className="opacity-75"
                    fill="currentColor"
                    d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"
                  ></path>
                </svg>
                Processing...
              </>
            ) : (
              confirmText
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default ConfirmModal;

--- START OF FILE: src\components\CustomImage\CustomImage.jsx ---

import Image from '@tiptap/extension-image';
import { mergeAttributes, Node } from '@tiptap/core';
import { ReactNodeViewRenderer } from '@tiptap/react';

// A small React component to render the caption input inside the editor
const Caption = ({ node, updateAttributes }) => (
    <figcaption contentEditable suppressContentEditableWarning={true} onBlur={e => updateAttributes({ caption: e.target.textContent })}>
        {node.attrs.caption || 'Type a caption...'}
    </figcaption>
);

// The component that will render our image with its figure and caption
const ImageView = ({ node, updateAttributes, editor }) => {
    const { src, alt, title, 'data-align': align, caption } = node.attrs;

    return (
        <figure
            data-align={align}
            className={`image-figure ${align ? `align-${align}` : ''}`}
            style={{ float: align === 'center' ? 'none' : align }}
        >
            <img src={src} alt={alt} title={title} className="rounded-lg shadow-md" />
            {editor.isEditable && ( // Only show the caption input when editing
                <Caption node={node} updateAttributes={updateAttributes} />
            )}
        </figure>
    );
};

export const CustomImage = Image.extend({
    // Extend the attributes to include our custom ones
    addAttributes() {
        return {
            ...this.parent?.(),
            'data-align': {
                default: 'center', // Default to center alignment
            },
            caption: {
                default: null,
            },
            style: {
                default: null, // Allow inline styles for float
            }
        };
    },

    // This tells Tiptap to render the image using our custom React component
    addNodeView() {
        return ReactNodeViewRenderer(ImageView);
    },
});

export default CustomImage;

--- END OF FILE: src\components\CustomImage\CustomImage.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\components\CustomTooltip\CustomTooltip.jsx ---

import * as Tooltip from "@radix-ui/react-tooltip";

const CustomTooltip = ({ children, content }) => {
    if (!content) {
        return children;
    }
    return (
        <Tooltip.Provider>
            <Tooltip.Root>
                <Tooltip.Trigger asChild>
                    <div>{children}</div>
                </Tooltip.Trigger>
                <Tooltip.Portal>
                    <Tooltip.Content
                        className=" capitalize text-primary z-[999] relative bg-slate-100 border-primary border-[1px] shadow-lg px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-sm"
                        sideOffset={5}
                    >
                        {content}
                        <Tooltip.Arrow className="fill-primary" />
                    </Tooltip.Content>
                </Tooltip.Portal>
            </Tooltip.Root>
        </Tooltip.Provider>
    );
};

export default CustomTooltip;


--- END OF FILE: src\components\CustomTooltip\CustomTooltip.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\components\DropdownMenu\DropdownMenu.jsx ---

import React, { Fragment } from 'react';
import { Menu, Transition } from '@headlessui/react';
import { MoreVertical } from 'lucide-react';

const DropdownMenu = ({ buttonIcon, items }) => {
    return (
        <Menu as="div" className="relative inline-block text-left">
            <div>
                <Menu.Button className="inline-flex justify-center w-full rounded-md p-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-white focus-visible:ring-opacity-75">
                    {buttonIcon || <MoreVertical size={18} />}
                </Menu.Button>
            </div>
            <Transition
                as={Fragment}
                enter="transition ease-out duration-100"
                enterFrom="transform opacity-0 scale-95"
                enterTo="transform opacity-100 scale-100"
                leave="transition ease-in duration-75"
                leaveFrom="transform opacity-100 scale-100"
                leaveTo="transform opacity-0 scale-95"
            >
                <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-56 origin-top-right divide-y divide-gray-100 rounded-md bg-white shadow-lg ring-1 ring-black ring-opacity-5 focus:outline-none z-10">
                    <div className="px-1 py-1">
                        {items.map((group, groupIndex) => (
                            <div key={groupIndex} className="py-1">
                                {group.map((item) => (
                                    <Menu.Item key={item.label}>
                                        {({ active }) => (
                                            <button
                                                onClick={item.onClick}
                                                disabled={item.disabled}
                                                className={`${active ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'text-gray-900'
                                                    } group flex w-full items-center rounded-md px-2 py-2 text-sm disabled:opacity-50 ${item.className || ''}`}
                                            >
                                                {item.icon && <span className="mr-2 h-5 w-5">{item.icon}</span>}
                                                {item.label}
                                            </button>
                                        )}
                                    </Menu.Item>
                                ))}
                                {/* Add separator if not the last group */}
                                {groupIndex < items.length - 1 && <hr className="my-1" />}
                            </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </Transition>
        </Menu>
    );
};

export default DropdownMenu;

--- END OF FILE: src\components\DropdownMenu\DropdownMenu.jsx ---
