--- START OF FILE: src\Helper\hasPermission.js ---

// --- START OF FILE: src/helpers/hasPermission.js ---

// All roles in lowercase for consistent matching

import getRole from './getRole';
const ROLES = {
    superadmin: 4,
    admin: 3,
    editor: 2,
    author: 1,
    reader: 0
};

/**
 * Checks if a user's role meets the minimum required role.
 * @param {string} userRole - The role of the current user (e.g., 'superadmin').
 * @param {string} requiredRole - The minimum role required for access (e.g., 'editor').
 * @returns {boolean} - True if the user has sufficient permissions.
 */
export const hasPermission = (requiredRole) => {
    // **DEBUGGING LOG:** Check what values are being passed

    // If userRole is not a valid string, deny access immediately.
    const userRole = getRole()

    if (!userRole || typeof userRole !== 'string') {
        // console.log("Permission denied: userRole is invalid.");
        return false;
    }

    // If requiredRole is not a valid string, deny access.
    if (!requiredRole || typeof requiredRole !== 'string') {
        // console.log("Permission denied: requiredRole is invalid.");
        return false;
    }

    // **THE FIX:** Convert both roles to lowercase before comparison.
    const userLevel = ROLES[userRole.toLowerCase()] ?? -1;
    const requiredLevel = ROLES[requiredRole.toLowerCase()] ?? -1;

    // **DEBUGGING LOG:** Check the determined levels
    // console.log(`Levels: UserLevel=${userLevel}, RequiredLevel=${requiredLevel}`);

    // The user has permission if their level is greater than or equal to the required level.
    const result = userLevel >= requiredLevel;
    // console.log(`Permission granted: ${result}`);

    return result;
};

// File: src/hooks/useArticleFilters.js
import { useState, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';

export const useArticleFilters = (initialLimit = 10) => {
    const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    const [limit, setLimit] = useState(initialLimit);
    const [filters, setFilters] = useState("");
    const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState({ sortBy: "createdAt", sortOrder: "desc" });
    const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");

    const handleSearchChange = useCallback((e) => {
        setSearchQuery(e.target.value);
        setPage(1);
    }, []);

    const handleCategoryChange = useCallback((e) => {
        setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, category: e.target.value }));
        setPage(1);
    }, []);

    const handleStatusChange = useCallback((e) => {
        if (e.target.value == "") {
            setFilters("")
        } else {
            setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, status: e.target.value }));

        }

        setPage(1);
    }, []);

    const handleSortByChange = useCallback((e) => {
        setSortConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, sortBy: e.target.value }));
    }, []);

    const handleSortOrderToggle = useCallback(() => {
        setSortConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, sortOrder: prev.sortOrder === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc" }));
    }, []);

    const handlePageChange = useCallback((newPage) => {
        setPage(newPage);
    }, []);

    const handleLimitChange = useCallback((newLimit) => {
        setLimit(newLimit);
        setPage(1);
    }, []);

    const queryParams = useMemo(() => ({
        // page,
        // limit,
        // category: filters.category,
        status: filters.status,
        // sortBy: sortConfig.sortBy,
        // sortOrder: sortConfig.sortOrder,
        // searchQuery,
    }), [page, limit, filters, sortConfig, searchQuery]);

    return {
        page, setPage,
        limit, setLimit,
        filters, setFilters,
        sortConfig, setSortConfig,
        searchQuery, setSearchQuery,
        handlers: {
            handleSearchChange,
            handleCategoryChange,
            handleStatusChange,
            handleSortByChange,
            handleSortOrderToggle,
            handlePageChange,
            handleLimitChange,
        },
        queryParams,
    };
};

--- START OF FILE: src\hooks\useCommentFilters.js ---

// File: src/hooks/useCommentFilters.js
import { useState, useMemo, useCallback } from 'react';

export const useCommentFilters = (initialLimit = 10) => {
    const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    const [limit, setLimit] = useState(initialLimit);
    const [filters, setFilters] = useState({
        isApproved: '',
        articleId: '',
        userId: '',
    });
    const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState({ sortBy: 'createdAt', sortOrder: 'desc' });

    const handleFilterChange = useCallback((e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
        setPage(1);
    }, []);

    const handleStatusFilterChange = useCallback((e) => {
        setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, isApproved: e.target.value }));
        setPage(1);
    }, []);

    const handleMuiPageChange = useCallback((event, newPageZeroIndexed) => {
        setPage(newPageZeroIndexed + 1);
    }, []);

    const handleMuiLimitChange = useCallback((event) => {
        setLimit(parseInt(event.target.value, 10));
        setPage(1);
    }, []);

    const handleSortChange = useCallback((newSortBy) => {
        setSortConfig(prev => {
            const isAsc = prev.sortBy === newSortBy && prev.sortOrder === 'asc';
            return { sortBy: newSortBy, sortOrder: isAsc ? 'desc' : 'asc' };
        });
    }, []);

    const queryParams = useMemo(() => ({
        page,
        limit,
        articleId: filters.articleId,
        userId: filters.userId,
        isApproved: filters.isApproved === '' ? undefined : filters.isApproved === 'true',
        sortBy: sortConfig.sortBy,
        sortOrder: sortConfig.sortOrder,
    }), [page, limit, filters, sortConfig]);

    return {
        page,
        limit,
        filters, // Expose filters object for direct binding
        sortConfig,
        setSortConfig,
        handlers: {
            handleFilterChange,
            handleStatusFilterChange,
            handleMuiPageChange,
            handleMuiLimitChange,
            handleSortChange,
        },
        queryParams,
    };
};

--- START OF FILE: src\hooks\useUserFilters.js ---

// File: src/hooks/useUserFilters.js
import { useState, useMemo, useCallback } from "react";

export const useUserFilters = (initialLimit = 10) => {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1); // API uses 1-indexed page
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(initialLimit);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState("");
  const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState({
    sortBy: "createdAt",
    sortOrder: "desc",
  });

  const handleSearchChange = useCallback((e) => {
    setSearchTerm(e.target.value);
    setPage(1);
  }, []);

  const handleRoleChange = useCallback((e) => {
    setSelectedRole(e.target.value);
    setPage(1);
  }, []);

  const handleMuiPageChange = useCallback((event, newPageZeroIndexed) => {
    setPage(newPageZeroIndexed + 1);
  }, []);

  const handleMuiLimitChange = useCallback((event) => {
    setLimit(parseInt(event.target.value, 10));
    setPage(1);
  }, []);

  const handleSortChange = useCallback((newSortBy) => {
    setSortConfig((prev) => {
      const isAsc = prev.sortBy === newSortBy && prev.sortOrder === "asc";
      return { sortBy: newSortBy, sortOrder: isAsc ? "desc" : "asc" };
    });
  }, []);

  const queryParams = useMemo(() => {
    const params = {
      page,
      limit,
      sort: sortConfig.sortBy,
      sortOrder: sortConfig.sortOrder,
      searchQuery: searchTerm,
    };

    if (selectedRole) {
      params.role = selectedRole;
    }

    return params;
  }, [page, limit, selectedRole, sortConfig, searchTerm]);

  return {
    page,
    limit,
    searchTerm, // Expose searchTerm for direct binding if needed
    selectedRole, // Expose selectedRole for direct binding
    sortConfig,
    setSortConfig,
    handlers: {
      handleSearchChange,
      handleRoleChange,
      handleMuiPageChange,
      handleMuiLimitChange,
      handleSortChange,
    },
    queryParams,
  };
};


--- END OF FILE: src\hooks\useUserFilters.js ---

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\Adeverrtisement\AdFormModal.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Upload, Link as LinkIcon, X } from 'lucide-react';

// Import your standardized UI components
import { Input, Select, Button } from '../../components/components';
import ModalWrapper from '../../components/ModalWrapper/ModalWrapper';
// --- Helper Functions and Constants ---
const AD_DIMENSIONS = {
    header: { text: '1024 x 120px (Landscape)', type: 'landscape' },
    sidebar: { text: '300 x 600px (Portrait)', type: 'portrait' },
    footer: { text: '1024 x 120px (Landscape)', type: 'landscape' },
    'in-article': { text: '728 x 90px (Landscape)', type: 'landscape' },
};

const locationOptions = [
    { value: 'header', label: 'Header' },
    { value: 'sidebar', label: 'Sidebar' },
    { value: 'footer', label: 'Footer' },
    { value: 'in-article', label: 'In-Article' }
];

// --- Main Modal Component ---
const AdFormModal = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSave, adToEdit, isProcessing }) => {
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        title: '',
        link: '',
        location: 'sidebar',
        isActive: true,
        image: null,
        imageUrl: '',
    });
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});

    // --- NEW: State for image preview ---
    const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState(null);
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (adToEdit) {
            setFormData({
                title: adToEdit.title,
                link: adToEdit.link,
                location: adToEdit.location,
                isActive: adToEdit.isActive,
                image: null,
                imageUrl: adToEdit.image?.url || '',
            });
            // --- NEW: Set initial preview when editing ---
            setImagePreview(adToEdit.image?.url || null);
        } else {
            setFormData({
                title: '', link: '', location: 'sidebar', isActive: true, image: null, imageUrl: '',
            });
            // --- NEW: Clear preview for new ads ---
            setImagePreview(null);
        }
        setErrors({});
    }, [adToEdit, isOpen]);

    const validate = () => {
        // Validation logic remains the same...
        const newErrors = {};
        if (!formData.title) newErrors.title = 'Title is required.';
        if (!formData.link) newErrors.link = 'Link URL is required.';
        else if (!/^https?:\/\//.test(formData.link)) newErrors.link = 'Please enter a valid URL (e.g., https://...).';
        if (!formData.image && !formData.imageUrl && !imagePreview) newErrors.image = 'An image file or URL is required.';
        setErrors(newErrors);
        return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0;
    };

    const handleInputChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value, type, checked } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: type === 'checkbox' ? checked : value }));
    };

    const handleSelectChange = (e) => {
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, location: e.target.value }));
        setImagePreview(null)
    };

    // --- UPDATED: handleFileChange to show preview ---
    const handleFileChange = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;

        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (event) => {
            const img = new Image();
            img.onload = () => {
                // Dimension validation remains the same
                const requiredType = AD_DIMENSIONS[formData.location].type;
                const isLandscape = img.width > img.height;
                const isPortrait = img.height > img.width;

                if (requiredType === 'landscape' && !isLandscape) {
                    toast.warn(`Image for '${formData.location}' should be landscape (wider than tall).`);
                    return;
                }
                if (requiredType === 'portrait' && !isPortrait) {
                    toast.warn(`Image for '${formData.location}' should be portrait (taller than wide).`);
                    return;
                }

                // --- NEW: Set preview URL ---
                if (imagePreview && imagePreview.startsWith('blob:')) {
                    URL.revokeObjectURL(imagePreview); // Clean up previous blob URL
                }
                setImagePreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
                setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, image: file, imageUrl: '' }));
                setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, image: null }));
            };
            img.src = event.target.result;
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    // --- NEW: Function to remove the selected image ---
    const removeImage = () => {
        if (imagePreview && imagePreview.startsWith('blob:')) {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(imagePreview);
        }
        setImagePreview(null);
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, image: null, imageUrl: '' }));
        if (fileInputRef.current) {
            fileInputRef.current.value = "";
        }
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!validate()) return;

        const dataPayload = new FormData();
        dataPayload.append('title', formData.title);
        dataPayload.append('link', formData.link);
        dataPayload.append('location', formData.location);
        dataPayload.append('isActive', formData.isActive);

        if (formData.image) {
            dataPayload.append('image', formData.image);
        } else if (formData.imageUrl) {
            dataPayload.append('imageUrl', formData.imageUrl);
        }

        await onSave(dataPayload);
    };

    return (
        <ModalWrapper
            isOpen={isOpen}
            onRequestClose={onClose}
            title={adToEdit ? 'Edit Advertisement' : 'Create New Ad'}
            hideFooter={true}
            className="max-w-2xl"
        >
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                <Input label="Title" name="title" value={formData.title} onChange={handleInputChange} error={errors.title} required />
                <Input label="Link URL" name="link" type="url" placeholder="https://example.com" value={formData.link} onChange={handleInputChange} error={errors.link} leftIcon={<LinkIcon size={16} />} required />

                <div>
                    <Select label="Location" name="location" value={formData.location} onChange={handleSelectChange} options={locationOptions} error={errors.location} required />
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Recommended size: {AD_DIMENSIONS[formData.location].text}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="isActive" name="isActive" checked={formData.isActive} onChange={handleInputChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded" />
                    <label htmlFor="isActive" className="ml-2 text-sm text-gray-700">Ad is Active</label>
                </div>

                {/* --- NEW: Image Preview Section --- */}
                {imagePreview && (
                    <div className="relative w-full max-w-sm border border-gray-400 p-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-50">
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Image Preview:</p>
                        <img src={imagePreview} alt="Ad preview" className="rounded-md w-full h-auto object-contain max-h-48" />
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={removeImage}
                            className="absolute top-1 right-1 p-1 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-full hover:bg-red-700 transition-colors shadow-md"
                        >
                            <X size={16} />
                        </button>
                    </div>
                )}

                <div>
                    <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Image Source</label>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                        <label htmlFor="image-upload" className="cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 border rounded-md text-sm hover:bg-gray-50">
                            <Upload size={16} /> Upload File
                        </label>
                        <input type="file" id="image-upload" accept="image/*" onChange={handleFileChange} ref={fileInputRef} className="hidden" />
                        {formData.image && <span className="text-sm text-gray-600">{formData.image.name}</span>}
                    </div>
                    {errors.image && <p className="text-red-500 text-xs mt-1">{errors.image}</p>}
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <hr className="flex-grow" /> <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">OR</span> <hr className="flex-grow" />
                </div>

                {/* --- UPDATED: Input for URL to show preview --- */}
                <Input
                    label="Image from URL"
                    name="imageUrl"
                    type="url"
                    placeholder="https://example.com/image.png"
                    value={formData.imageUrl}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                        const url = e.target.value;
                        setFormData({ ...formData, imageUrl: url, image: null });
                        setImagePreview(url); // Update preview on URL change
                    }}
                    leftIcon={<LinkIcon size={16} />}
                />

                <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-gray-200 mt-6">
                    <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
                    <Button type="submit" isLoading={isProcessing} disabled={isProcessing}>
                        {isProcessing ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Ad'}
                    </Button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </ModalWrapper>
    );
};

export default AdFormModal;

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\Adeverrtisement\AdFormModal.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\Adeverrtisement\Advertisement.jsx ---

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useGetActiveAdsQuery, useCreateAdMutation, useUpdateAdMutation, useDeleteAdMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/advertisementSlice';
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Edit, Trash2, PlusCircle, Image as ImageIcon } from 'lucide-react';

// Import your standardized UI components
import { Heading, Button, Card } from '../../components/components';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';

// Import the refactored modal component
import AdFormModal from './AdFormModal'; // Adjust path if needed

const AdCard = ({ ad, onEdit, onDelete }) => {
    let imageClass = 'w-full object-cover';


    return (
        <Card className="flex flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden">
            <div>
                <div className="bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center">
                    {ad.image?.url ? (
                        <img src={ad.image.url} alt={ad.title} className={imageClass} />
                    ) : (
                        <div className={`flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 bg-gray-200 ${imageClass}`}>
                            <ImageIcon size={32} />
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
                <div className="p-4">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 line-clamp-2">{ad.title}</h3>
                        <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 text-xs rounded-full font-semibold ${ad.isActive ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-800'}`}>
                            {ad.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-mono bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded w-fit">Location: <span className='font-bold'>{ad.location}</span></p>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 flex justify-end space-x-2 border-t">
                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => onEdit(ad)} icon={<Edit size={14} />}>Edit</Button>
                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="text-red-600" onClick={() => onDelete(ad)} icon={<Trash2 size={14} />}>Delete</Button>
            </div>
        </Card>
    );
};

const Advertisement = () => {
    const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [adToEdit, setAdToEdit] = useState(null);
    const [adToDelete, setAdToDelete] = useState(null);

    const { data: ads = [], isLoading, refetch } = useGetActiveAdsQuery();
    const [createAd, { isLoading: isCreating }] = useCreateAdMutation();
    const [updateAd, { isLoading: isUpdating }] = useUpdateAdMutation();
    const [deleteAd, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteAdMutation();

    const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
        setAdToEdit(null);
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleOpenEditModal = (ad) => {
        setAdToEdit(ad);
        setIsModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleOpenDeleteModal = (ad) => {
        setAdToDelete(ad);
        setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
    };

    const handleSave = async (formData) => {
        const apiFunc = adToEdit ? updateAd : createAd;
        const data = adToEdit ? { id: adToEdit._id, adData: formData } : formData;

        await makeRequest({
            apiFunc,
            data,
            toast,
            toastText: "Ad updated sucessfully",
            onSuccess: () => {
                refetch();
                setIsModalOpen(false);
            },
        });
    };

    const confirmDelete = async () => {
        if (!adToDelete) return;
        await makeRequest({
            apiFunc: deleteAd,
            data: adToDelete._id,
            toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                refetch();
                setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
            },
            onError: () => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)
        });
    };

    if (isLoading) return <div>Loading...</div>;

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={confirmDelete}
                title="Confirm Deletion"
                isConfirmLoading={isDeleting}
                variant="danger"
            >
                Are you sure you want to permanently delete the ad: <strong>{adToDelete?.title}</strong>?
            </ConfirmModal>

            <AdFormModal
                isOpen={isModalOpen}
                isProcessing={isCreating || isUpdating}
                onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                onSave={handleSave}
                adToEdit={adToEdit}
            />

            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Advertisement Management"
                actions={
                    <Button onClick={handleOpenCreateModal} icon={<PlusCircle size={16} />}>
                        Create New Ad
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
                {ads.map((ad) => (
                    <AdCard
                        key={ad._id}
                        ad={ad}
                        onEdit={handleOpenEditModal}
                        onDelete={handleOpenDeleteModal}
                    />
                ))}
            </div>

            {ads.length === 0 && !isLoading && (
                <Card className="text-center py-16 col-span-full">
                    <p className="text-gray-500">No advertisements found. Click "Create New Ad" to get started.</p>
                </Card>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

export default Advertisement;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ArticlePage\ArticlePage.jsx ---

import React from 'react';
import { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'; // Use Link for navigation
import { useGetAdminArticlesBySlugQuery } from '../../_core/Slices/articleSlice'; // Adjust path
import { FaFacebook, FaLink } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { FaPinterest, FaXTwitter } from 'react-icons/fa6';
import { Calendar, User } from 'lucide-react';
import DOMPurify from 'dompurify'; // For safely rendering HTML
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';

// A small component to safely render HTML content
const ArticleBody = ({ htmlContent }) => {
  // Sanitize the HTML to prevent XSS attacks before rendering
  const sanitizedContent = DOMPurify.sanitize(htmlContent);
  return <div className="prose lg:prose-lg max-w-none" dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: sanitizedContent }} />;
};

const ArticlePage = () => {
  const { slug } = useParams(); // Get slug from URL, e.g., 'published-testing'
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const {
    data: article,
    isLoading,
    isError,
    error,
  } = useGetAdminArticlesBySlugQuery(slug);

  if (isLoading) {
    // You can replace this with a more sophisticated skeleton loader
    return <div className="h-screen w-full flex justify-center items-center">
      <CircularProgressBox />
    </div>;
  }

  if (isError || !article) {
    return (
      <div className="p-8 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-secondary">Article Not Found</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">{error?.data?.message || "The article you are looking for does not exist."}</p>

        <button onClick={() => { navigate(-1) }} className="mt-6 px-4 py-2 bg-black text-white rounded-lg">Go Back Home</button>

      </div>
    );
  }

  // Format date for display
  const formattedDate = new Date(article.publishedAt).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    day: 'numeric',
  });

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-8 mt-9">
      <div className="container mx-auto max-w-6xl">
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-12">

          {/* Main Article Content */}
          <main className="w-full lg:w-2/3">
            {/* Breadcrumbs */}
            <div className="mb-4 border-y-2 border-gray-200 py-2">
              <nav className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-sm font-bold uppercase text-gray-500">
                <Link to="/" className="hover:text-secondary">Home</Link>
                <span>/</span>
                {article.category && (
                  <>
                    <Link to={`/category/${article.category.slug}`} className="hover:text-secondary">
                      {article.category.name}
                    </Link>
                    <span>/</span>
                  </>
                )}
                <span className="text-gray-800 truncate">{article.title}</span>
              </nav>
            </div>

            <article className="space-y-6">
              {/* Header */}
              <header className="space-y-4">
                <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-gray-900">{article.title}</h1>
                {article.excerpt && <p className="text-lg text-gray-600">{article.excerpt}</p>}

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-2 text-sm text-gray-600">
                  {article.author && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <User size={16} />
                      <span>By <Link to={`/author/${article.author.slug}`} className="font-semibold hover:underline">{article.author.username}</Link></span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Calendar size={16} />
                    <span>{formattedDate}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </header>

              {/* Featured Image */}
              {article.featuredImage?.url && (
                <div className="my-6">
                  <img src={article.featuredImage.url} alt={article.title} className="w-full rounded-lg shadow-lg" />
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Article Body Content */}
              <div className="article-body-wrapper">
                <ArticleBody htmlContent={article.content} />
              </div>

              {/* Tags and Sharing */}
              <footer className="pt-6 border-t mt-8">
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center gap-4">
                  {article.tags?.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="font-bold">Tags:</span>
                      {article.tags.map((tag, index) => (
                        <span key={index} className="px-3 py-1 bg-gray-200 text-gray-800 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold">
                          {tag}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="flex items-center bg-white border border-gray-300 rounded-full shadow-sm gap-3 px-4 py-2">
                    <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Share:</span>
                    <a href="#" aria-label="Share on Facebook"><FaFacebook className="text-gray-600 hover:text-primary" /></a>
                    <a href="#" aria-label="Share on Twitter"><FaXTwitter className="text-gray-600 hover:text-black" /></a>
                    <a href="#" aria-label="Share on Pinterest"><FaPinterest className="text-gray-600 hover:text-secondary" /></a>
                    <a href="#" aria-label="Copy link"><FaLink className="text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900" /></a>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </footer>
            </article>
          </main>

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ArticlePage;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ArticlesPage\ArticlesPage.jsx ---

"use client"

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react"
import { Search, Plus, List, SortAsc, SortDesc, FileText, Grid } from "lucide-react"
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom"
import { toast } from "react-toastify"
import debounce from 'lodash.debounce'; // We'll need this for debouncing search

// RTK Query and other imports
import { useGetArticlesQuery } from "../../_core/Slices/articleSlice.js"
// NEW: Import hook to get all categories for the filter dropdown
import { useGetCategoriesQuery } from "../../_core/Slices/categorySlice.js"
import { ARTICLE_STATUS_OPTIONS, ARTICLE_SORT_OPTIONS } from '../../constants/articleConstants.js'
import { Select, Heading, Button, Card, Input } from "../../components/components.js"
import ArticlesItem from "../../components/ArticlesItem/ArticlesItem.jsx";
import ArticlesCard from "../../components/ArticlesCard/ArticlesCard.jsx"
import { Alert } from "@mui/material"
import Pagination from "../../components/Pagination/Pagination.jsx"; // Import the pagination component

const ARTICLES_PER_PAGE = 9; // This is now our 'limit'

const ArticlesPage = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const { authorId, categoryId, special } = useParams()

    // --- State for Filters, Sorting, and Pagination ---
    const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState("grid")
    const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("")
    const [debouncedSearchQuery, setDebouncedSearchQuery] = useState(""); // State for debounced search term
    const [filters, setFilters] = useState({ status: "", category: "" })
    const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState({ sortBy: "createdAt", sortOrder: "desc" })
    const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1)
    console.log(filters, "category")
    // --- Debounce search input to prevent API calls on every keystroke ---
    useEffect(() => {
        const handler = debounce(() => {
            setDebouncedSearchQuery(searchQuery);
            setCurrentPage(1); // Reset to page 1 on a new search
        }, 500); // 500ms delay

        handler();

        // Cleanup function to cancel the debounce on component unmount
        return () => {
            handler.cancel();
        };
    }, [searchQuery]);

    useEffect(() => {

        setCurrentPage(1);
    }, [authorId, categoryId, special])

    // --- RTK Query Hooks ---
    // The hook now receives ALL parameters for backend processing
    const {
        data: apiResponse,
        isLoading,
        isFetching, // Use isFetching to show loading state during refetches (e.g., page changes)
        isError,
        refetch,
        error,
    } = useGetArticlesQuery({
        page: currentPage,
        limit: ARTICLES_PER_PAGE,
        status: filters.status || undefined,
        category: filters.category || undefined,
        searchQuery: debouncedSearchQuery || undefined,
        sortBy: sortConfig.sortBy,
        sortOrder: sortConfig.sortOrder,
        // Params from URL
        author: authorId || undefined,
        category: categoryId || undefined,
        ...(special && { [special]: true })
    },
        {
            refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true
        });

    // NEW: Fetch all categories for the filter dropdown
    const { data: categoriesData } = useGetCategoriesQuery();

    // The backend now returns paginated data, so we extract it
    const articles = apiResponse?.articles || [];
    const totalPages = apiResponse?.totalPages || 1;

    // --- Handlers remain mostly the same, they just trigger refetches ---
    const handleFilterChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target
        setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }))
        setCurrentPage(1); // Reset to page 1 on filter change
    }
    const handleSortChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target
        setSortConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }))
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }
    const handleSortOrderToggle = () => {
        setSortConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, sortOrder: prev.sortOrder === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc' }))
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }
    const handleSearchChange = (e) => {
        setSearchQuery(e.target.value); // This updates the live search query
    }

    // --- Category options are now derived from their own API call ---
    const uniqueCategoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
        if (!categoriesData) return [];
        const options = categoriesData.map(cat => ({
            value: cat._id,
            label: cat.name,
        }));
        return [{ value: "", label: "All Categories" }, ...options];
    }, [categoriesData]);

    // --- Render Logic ---
    // if (isLoading || isFetching) { // Show initial loader only
    //     return (
    //         <div className="space-y-6">
    //             <div className="animate-pulse">
    //                 <div className="h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/4 mb-4"></div>
    //                 <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/2 mb-8"></div>
    //             </div>
    //             <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
    //                 {[...Array(6)].map((_, i) => (
    //                     <div key={i} className="animate-pulse bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm"><div className="bg-gray-200 h-40 rounded"></div><div className="h-4 mt-4 bg-gray-200 rounded"></div><div className="h-3 mt-2 w-3/4 bg-gray-200 rounded"></div></div>
    //                 ))}
    //             </div>
    //         </div>
    //     )
    // }
    if (isError) {
        return (
            <Alert severity="error" sx={{ m: 2 }}>
                Failed to load articles or there are too many requests. Refresh the page.  {error.data?.message || "Also please check your connection and try again."}
            </Alert>
        );
    }

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title={authorId && apiResponse?.articles[0]?.author?.fullName || categoryId && apiResponse?.articles[0]?.category?.name || "Articles"}
                description={`Manage all articles`}
                actions={
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                        <Button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard/create-article")} icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}>New Article</Button>
                    </div>
                }
            />

            <Card>
                <div className="p-4">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-4">
                        <div className="lg:col-span-2">
                            <Input placeholder="Search articles by title, author..." value={searchQuery} onChange={handleSearchChange} leftIcon={<Search className="size-4" />} />
                        </div>
                        <Select name="category" value={filters.category} onChange={handleFilterChange} options={uniqueCategoryOptions} placeholder="Category" />
                        <Select name="status" value={filters.status} onChange={handleFilterChange} options={ARTICLE_STATUS_OPTIONS} placeholder="Status" />
                        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 lg:col-span-2">
                            <Select name="sortBy" value={sortConfig.sortBy} onChange={handleSortChange} options={ARTICLE_SORT_OPTIONS} placeholder="Sort by" className="flex-1" />
                            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={handleSortOrderToggle} title={`Sort ${sortConfig.sortOrder === "asc" ? "Descending" : "Ascending"}`}>
                                {sortConfig.sortOrder === "asc" ? <SortAsc className="size-4" /> : <SortDesc className="size-4" />}
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </Card>

            {isFetching || isLoading ? <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="animate-pulse">
                    <div className="h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/4 mb-4"></div>
                    <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/2 mb-8"></div>
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                    {[...Array(6)].map((_, i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="animate-pulse bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm"><div className="bg-gray-200 h-40 rounded"></div><div className="h-4 mt-4 bg-gray-200 rounded"></div><div className="h-3 mt-2 w-3/4 bg-gray-200 rounded"></div></div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            </div> : articles.length === 0 ? (
                <Card>
                    <div className="text-center py-12">
                        <FileText className="size-12 text-gray-400 mx-auto mb-4" />
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-medium">No articles found</h3>
                        <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6">Try adjusting your filters or search terms, or create a new article.</p>
                        <Button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard/create-article")} icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}>Create New Article</Button>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            ) : (
                <div className={viewMode === "grid" ? "grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6" : "space-y-4"}>
                    {articles.map((article) =>
                        viewMode === "grid" ? (
                            <ArticlesCard key={article._id} article={article} />
                        ) : (
                            <ArticlesItem key={article._id} article={article} />
                        ),
                    )}
                </div>
            )}

            {apiResponse && apiResponse.totalArticles > ARTICLES_PER_PAGE && (
                <Card>
                    <div className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                            Showing {articles.length} of {apiResponse.totalArticles} articles
                        </span>
                        <Pagination
                            currentPage={apiResponse.currentPage}
                            totalPages={totalPages}
                            onPageChange={(page) => setCurrentPage(page)}
                            isFetching={isFetching}
                        />
                    </div>
                </Card>
            )}
        </div>
    )
}

export default ArticlesPage;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ArticleStatus\PublishedArticles.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import debounce from 'lodash.debounce';

// --- UI Component Imports ---
import { Heading, Button, MuiTable, Select, } from "../../components/components.js";
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
// Added ConfirmModal
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';

import { Box, Typography, Chip, Avatar, Tooltip, IconButton, Alert } from "@mui/material";
import { Search, Eye, Edit, Zap } from "lucide-react";

// --- RTK Query and Helper Imports ---
import { useGetArticlesQuery, useToggleTopHeadlineStatusMutation, useUpdateArticleStatusMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/articleSlice.js";
import { formatDate } from "../../utils/formatters.js";
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper.js';
import { CLIENT_URL } from '../../utils/ApiBaseUrl.js';

const PublishedArticles = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { articleStatus } = useParams();

    // --- State Management ---
    const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    const [limit, setLimit] = useState(10);
    const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
    const [debouncedSearchTerm, setDebouncedSearchTerm] = useState("");

    // **NEW**: State to manage the status change confirmation
    const [isStatusModalOpen, setIsStatusModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [statusChangeInfo, setStatusChangeInfo] = useState({ articleId: null, newStatus: '' });

    // --- RTK Query Hooks ---
    const {
        data: response,
        isLoading,
        isFetching,
        isError,
        error,
        refetch
    } = useGetArticlesQuery(
        { status: articleStatus, page, limit, q: debouncedSearchTerm || undefined },
        { refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true }
    );

    // Using the specific mutation hook from your slice
    const [toggleTopHeadlineStatus, { isLoading: isToggling }] = useToggleTopHeadlineStatusMutation();

    const [updateArticleStatus, { isLoading: isUpdatingStatus }] = useUpdateArticleStatusMutation();

    // --- Debounce Effect (Unchanged) ---
    useEffect(() => {
        const handler = debounce(() => { setDebouncedSearchTerm(searchTerm); setPage(1); }, 500);
        handler();
        return () => handler.cancel();
    }, [searchTerm]);

    // --- Handlers ---
    const handleMuiPageChange = (event, newPage) => setPage(newPage + 1);
    const handleMuiLimitChange = (event) => { setLimit(parseInt(event.target.value, 10)); setPage(1); };

    /**
     * **MODIFIED**: This handler now opens the confirmation modal instead of calling the API directly.
     */
    const handleStatusChangeRequest = (e, articleId) => {
        const newStatus = e.target.value;
        // Store the information needed for the confirmation
        setStatusChangeInfo({ articleId, newStatus });
        // Open the modal
        setIsStatusModalOpen(true);
    };


    

    /**
     * **NEW**: This function is called when the user confirms the change in the modal.
     */
    const confirmStatusChange = () => {
        const { articleId, newStatus } = statusChangeInfo;
        if (!articleId || !newStatus) return;

        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: updateArticleStatus,
            data: { articleId: articleId, status: { status: newStatus } },
            toast: toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                refetch(); // Refetch the list to show the updated status
            },
            // The makeRequest helper handles the error toast.
        });

        // Close the modal after initiating the request
        setIsStatusModalOpen(false);
    };

    const handleToggleHeadline = (row) => {
        toggleTopHeadlineStatus(row._id)
            .unwrap()
            .then(() => toast.success(`article headline status updated!`))
            .catch(err => toast.error(err.data?.message || "Failed to update status."));
    };
    // --- Column Definitions ---
    const statusOptions = [
        { value: "draft", label: "Draft" },
        { value: "published", label: "Published" },
        { value: "pending-review", label: "Pending Review" },
        { value: "archived", label: "Archived" },
        // { value: "scheduled", label: "Scheduled" },
        { value: "rejected", label: "Rejected" },
    ];

    const columns = [
        {
            header: "Article", field: "title", render: (title, row) => (
                <Box className={"flex min-w-64"} sx={{ alignItems: 'center', gap: 2 }}>
                    <a href={`${row.status == "published" ? CLIENT_URL : ""}/article/${row.slug}`} target='_blank'>
                        <Avatar variant="rounded" src={row.featuredImage?.url} />
                        <Box>
                            <Typography className='whitespace-normal' variant="body2" fontWeight="bold">{title}</Typography>
                            <Typography variant="caption" color="text.secondary">in "{row.category?.name}"</Typography>
                        </Box>
                    </a>
                </Box>)
        },
        {
            header: "Author", field: "author",
            render: (author) =>
                author ? <Tooltip title={`${author.fullName}'s articles`}>
                    <a className='hover:text-primary hover:underline' href={`https://newshub-demo.netlify.app/author/${author._id}`} target="_blank">{author?.username}</a>
                </Tooltip> : "NA"

        },
        {
            header: "Status", field: "status", align: "center",
            render: (status, row) => (
                <Select
                    className={"w-40"}
                    value={status}
                    options={statusOptions}
                    onChange={(e) => handleStatusChangeRequest(e, row._id)}
                    disabled={isUpdatingStatus} // Disable select while any status is updating
                />
            )
        },
        { header: "Views", field: "views", align: "center" },
        { header: "Published", field: "publishedAt", render: (date) => formatDate(date) },
        {
            header: "Actions", field: "actions", align: "right",
            render: (v, row) => (
                <Box className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => handleToggleHeadline(row)} disabled={isToggling}>
                        <Zap size={14} className={row?.isTopHeadline ? "text-orange-500" : "text-gray-500"} />
                    </Button>
                    <Tooltip title="View"><IconButton onClick={() => window.open(`/article/${row.slug}`, '_blank')} size="small"><Eye size={18} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                    <Tooltip title="Edit"><IconButton onClick={() => navigate(`/dashboard/edit-article/${row.slug}`)} size="small"><Edit size={18} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                </Box>
            )
        }
    ];

    if (isLoading) return <CircularProgressBox text={`Loading ${articleStatus} articles...`} />;

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title={`${articleStatus.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + articleStatus.slice(1)} Articles`}
                description="View and manage articles based on their status."
            />

            {/* **NEW**: Confirmation Modal for status change */}
            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isStatusModalOpen}
                variant='info'
                onRequestClose={() => setIsStatusModalOpen(false)}
                title="Confirm Status Change"
                onConfirm={confirmStatusChange}
                isLoading={isUpdatingStatus}
            >
                Are you sure you want to change this article's status to <strong>"{statusChangeInfo.newStatus}"</strong>?
            </ConfirmModal>

            <MuiTable
                title="Article List"
                columns={columns}
                rows={response?.articles || []}
                isLoading={isFetching || isUpdatingStatus} // Table should show loading state during update
                showSearch={false} // Hiding table's internal search to use our external one later if needed
                totalRows={response?.totalArticles || 0}
                page={page - 1}
                rowsPerPage={limit}
                onPageChange={handleMuiPageChange}
                onRowsPerPageChange={handleMuiLimitChange}
            />
            {isError && <Alert severity="error" sx={{ mt: 2 }}>{error?.data?.message || "Failed to load articles."}</Alert>}
        </div>
    );
};

export default PublishedArticles;

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\ArticleStatus\PublishedArticles.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\AuthPanel\AuthPanel.jsx ---


import { useState } from "react"
import Login from '../../components/Login/Login';
import Signup from '../../components/Signup/Signup';

const AuthPanel = () => {
    const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState("login") // "login" or "signup"

    // const handleLogin = (loginData) => {
    //     console.log("Login successful:", loginData)
    //     // Here you would typically validate credentials with your backend
    //     onAuthenticated && onAuthenticated(loginData)
    // }

    // const handleSignup = (signupData) => {
    //     console.log("Signup successful:", signupData)
    //     // Here you would typically create account with your backend
    //     onAuthenticated && onAuthenticated(signupData)
    // }
    const switchToSignup = () => setCurrentView("signup")
    const switchToLogin = () => setCurrentView("login")
    const authScreens = [
        {
            view: "login",
            element: <Login />
        },
        {
            view: "signup",
            element: <Signup />
        },
        {
            view: "foget-pass",
            element: <Login />
        },
    ]
    return (
        <>
            {currentView === "login" ? (
                <Login onSwitchToSignup={switchToSignup} />
            ) : (
                <Signup onSwitchToLogin={switchToLogin} />
            )}
        </>
    )
}

export default AuthPanel

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\Categories\Categories.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';

// --- UI Component Imports ---
import { Heading, Button, Card, Input, Select, Textarea, MuiTable } from "../../components/components.js";
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import ModalWrapper from '../../components/ModalWrapper/ModalWrapper';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';

import { Box, Typography, Chip, IconButton, Tooltip, Alert } from "@mui/material";
import { Edit, Trash2, Plus, BarChart, BookOpen } from "lucide-react";

// --- RTK Query and Helper Imports ---
import {
    useGetCategoriesQuery,
    useCreateCategoryMutation,
    useUpdateCategoryMutation,
    useDeleteCategoryMutation,
} from "../../_core/Slices/categorySlice";
import { hasPermission } from "../../Helper/hasPermission";
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import SubCategoryModal from './SubCategoryModal.jsx';
import CategoryForm from './CategoryForm.jsx';






// =================================================================================
// == Main Categories Page Component
// =================================================================================
const Categories = () => {
    const { userInfo } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);

    // --- State ---
    const [isFormModalOpen, setIsFormModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [currentCategory, setCurrentCategory] = useState(null); // For edit, delete, and sub-category view
    const [isDeleteConfirmOpen, setIsDeleteConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isSubCategoryModalOpen, setIsSubCategoryModalOpen] = useState(false);

    // --- RTK Hooks ---
    const { data: categoriesData, isLoading, isError, error, refetch } = useGetCategoriesQuery(undefined, { refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true });
    const [createCategory, { isLoading: isCreating }] = useCreateCategoryMutation();
    const [updateCategory, { isLoading: isUpdating }] = useUpdateCategoryMutation();
    const [deleteCategory, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteCategoryMutation();

    // Filter to get only top-level (root) categories for the main table display
    const rootCategories = useMemo(() => {
        if (!categoriesData) return [];
        return categoriesData.filter(cat => !cat.parentCategory);
    }, [categoriesData]);

    // --- Handlers ---
    const handleOpenCreateModal = () => { setCurrentCategory(null); setIsFormModalOpen(true); };
    const handleOpenEditModal = (category) => { setCurrentCategory(category); setIsFormModalOpen(true); };
    const handleOpenSubCategoriesModal = (category) => { setCurrentCategory(category); setIsSubCategoryModalOpen(true); };
    const handleOpenDeleteConfirm = (category) => { setCurrentCategory(category); setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(true); };

    const handleFormSubmit = (formData) => {
        const apiFunc = currentCategory ? updateCategory : createCategory;
        const data = currentCategory ? { id: currentCategory._id, data: formData } : formData;
        makeRequest({ apiFunc, data, toast, toastText: "Article Saved Successfully", onSuccess: () => setIsFormModalOpen(false) });
    };

    const handleDeleteConfirm = () => {
        makeRequest({ apiFunc: deleteCategory, data: currentCategory?._id, toast, onSuccess: () => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false) });
    };

    const userCanManage = hasPermission('editor');

    const columns = [
        { field: "name", header: "Name" }, { field: "slug", header: "Slug" },
        { field: "description", header: "Description", render: (desc) => <span className="line-clamp-2 max-w-xs">{desc}</span> },
        { field: "subcategories", header: "Topics", align: "center", render: (subcategories) => <Chip label={subcategories?.length || 0} size="small" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); /* Optionally handle click */ }} /> },
        {
            field: "actions", header: "Actions", align: "right", render: (value, row) => userCanManage && (
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 0.5 }}>
                    <Tooltip title="View articles">
                        <Link to={`/dashboard/articles-by-category/${row._id}`}>
                            <IconButton size="small">
                                <BookOpen size={16} />
                            </IconButton>
                        </Link>
                    </Tooltip>
                    <Tooltip title="View & Manage topics"><IconButton onClick={() => handleOpenSubCategoriesModal(row)} size="small"><BarChart size={16} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                    <Tooltip title="Edit Category"><IconButton onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(row)} size="small"><Edit size={16} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                    <Tooltip title="Delete Category"><IconButton onClick={() => handleOpenDeleteConfirm(row)} size="small" color="error"><Trash2 size={16} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                </Box>
            )
        },
    ];

    if (isLoading) return <CircularProgressBox text="Loading Categories..." />;

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <ModalWrapper isOpen={isFormModalOpen} onRequestClose={() => setIsFormModalOpen(false)} title={currentCategory ? "Edit Category" : "Create New Root Category"}>
                <CategoryForm initialData={currentCategory} onSubmit={handleFormSubmit} onCancel={() => setIsFormModalOpen(false)} isLoading={isCreating || isUpdating} allCategories={categoriesData || []} />
            </ModalWrapper>

            {currentCategory && (
                <SubCategoryModal isOpen={isSubCategoryModalOpen} onClose={() => setIsSubCategoryModalOpen(false)}
                    parentCategory={(categoriesData.filter(cat => cat._id === currentCategory._id)[0] || null)}
                    allCategories={categoriesData || []} onActionComplete={refetch} />
            )}

            <ConfirmModal isOpen={isDeleteConfirmOpen} onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false)} title="Confirm Delete" onConfirm={handleDeleteConfirm} isLoading={isDeleting} confirmButtonVariant="danger">
                <p>Are you sure you want to delete the category: <strong>{currentCategory?.name}</strong>?</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">Note: Sub-categories will be promoted to root level. This action cannot be undone.</p>
            </ConfirmModal>

            <Heading level={1} title="Manage Categories" description="Organize your content by creating and editing root categories and their sub-categories." actions={userCanManage && <Button onClick={handleOpenCreateModal} icon={<Plus size={16} />}>New Root Category</Button>} />

            <Card>
                {isError && <Alert severity="error">{error.data?.message || "An error occurred."}</Alert>}
                {!isError && (
                    <MuiTable title="Root Categories" subtitle={`${rootCategories.length} top-level categories found`} columns={columns} rows={rootCategories} />
                )}
            </Card>
        </div>
    );
};

export default Categories;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\Categories\CategoryForm.jsx ---

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Button, Input, Select, Textarea } from '../../components/components';
import { Box } from '@mui/material';

const CategoryForm = ({ initialData, onSubmit, onCancel, isLoading, allCategories = [], defaultParentId = null }) => {
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ name: "", description: "" });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (initialData) {
            // Editing an existing category
            setFormData({
                name: initialData.name || "",
                description: initialData.description || "",
                // parentCategory: initialData.parentCategory?._id || initialData.parentCategory || null,
            });
        } else {
            // Creating a new category, potentially with a pre-selected parent
            setFormData({
                name: "",
                description: "",
                // parentCategory: defaultParentId,
            });
        }
    }, [initialData, defaultParentId]);

    const handleChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    };

    // const handleSelectChange = (e) => {
    //     setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, parentCategory: e.target.value || null }));
    // };

    const handleSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!formData.name.trim()) {
            toast.error("Category name is required.");
            return;
        }
        onSubmit(formData);
    };

    // Create a list of potential parents, excluding the category itself and its descendants
    const parentCategoryOptions = [
        { value: "", label: "None (Root Category)" },
        ...allCategories
            .filter(cat => cat._id !== initialData?._id) // Prevent self-parenting
            .map(cat => ({ value: cat._id, label: cat.name }))
    ];

    return (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-4">
            <Input name="name" label="Name" value={formData.name} onChange={handleChange} required placeholder="e.g., Technology" />
            <Textarea name="description" label="Description" value={formData.description} onChange={handleChange} placeholder="A short description of the category." rows={3} />
            {/* <Select
                name="parentCategory"
                label="Parent Category"
                value={formData.parentCategory || ""}
                onChange={handleSelectChange}
                options={parentCategoryOptions}
                disabled={!!defaultParentId} // Disable if creating a sub-category
                helperText={defaultParentId ? "Parent is pre-selected and cannot be changed." : ""}
            /> */}
            <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'flex-end', gap: 2, pt: 2, borderTop: 1, borderColor: 'divider' }}>
                <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={onCancel} disabled={isLoading}>Cancel</Button>
                <Button type="submit" isLoading={isLoading} disabled={isLoading}>{initialData ? "Update Category" : "Create"}</Button>
            </Box>
        </form>
    );
};
export default CategoryForm

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\Categories\CategoryForm.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\Categories\SubCategoryModal.jsx ---

import React, { useState } from 'react'
import { useCreateCategoryMutation, useDeleteCategoryMutation, useUpdateCategoryMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/categorySlice';
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import ModalWrapper from '../../components/ModalWrapper/ModalWrapper';
import { IconButton, Typography } from '@mui/material';
import { Button, Card } from '../../components/components';
import CategoryForm from './CategoryForm';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
import { BookOpen, Edit, Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Link } from 'react-router';

// =================================================================================
// == Sub-categories Management Modal Component
// =================================================================================
const SubCategoryModal = ({ setIsOpen, isOpen, onClose, parentCategory, allCategories, onActionComplete }) => {
    // These hooks are used for actions *within* this modal
    const [createCategory, { isLoading: isCreating }] = useCreateCategoryMutation();
    const [updateCategory, { isLoading: isUpdating }] = useUpdateCategoryMutation();
    const [deleteCategory, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteCategoryMutation();

    const [isFormVisible, setIsFormVisible] = useState(false);
    const [editingSubCategory, setEditingSubCategory] = useState(null);
    const [isDeleteConfirmOpen, setIsDeleteConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
    const [subCategoryToDelete, setSubCategoryToDelete] = useState(null);

    if (!parentCategory) return null;

    const subCategories = parentCategory.subcategories || [];

    const handleFormSubmit = (formData) => {
        const apiFunc = editingSubCategory ? updateCategory : createCategory;
        const data = editingSubCategory ? { id: editingSubCategory._id, data: formData } : { ...formData, parentCategory: parentCategory._id };

        makeRequest({
            apiFunc,
            data,
            toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setIsFormVisible(false);
                setEditingSubCategory(null);
                onActionComplete(); // Tell the parent page to refetch all categories
            }
        });
    };

    const handleDeleteClick = (subCategory) => {
        setSubCategoryToDelete(subCategory);
        setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(true);
    };

    const confirmDelete = () => {
        if (!subCategoryToDelete) return;
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: deleteCategory,
            data: subCategoryToDelete._id,
            toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false);
                setSubCategoryToDelete(null);
                onActionComplete();
            },
            onError: () => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false),
        });
    };

    return (
        <>
            <ModalWrapper isOpen={isOpen} onRequestClose={onClose} title={`Manage Sub-categories for "${parentCategory.name}"`}>
                <div className="space-y-4 p-4">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                        <Typography variant="h6">Topics List</Typography>
                        <Button icon={<Plus size={16} />} onClick={() => { setEditingSubCategory(null); setIsFormVisible(true); }}>
                            New Topic
                        </Button>
                    </div>

                    {isFormVisible && (
                        <Card variant="outlined" className="p-4 bg-gray-50">
                            <CategoryForm
                                initialData={editingSubCategory}
                                onSubmit={handleFormSubmit}
                                onCancel={() => setIsFormVisible(false)}
                                isLoading={isCreating || isUpdating}
                                allCategories={allCategories}
                                defaultParentId={parentCategory._id}
                            />
                        </Card>
                    )}

                    {subCategories.length > 0 ? (
                        <ul className="space-y-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto p-1">
                            {subCategories.map(sub => (
                                <li key={sub._id} className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-200">
                                    <span className="font-medium">{sub.name}</span>
                                    <div className="space-x-2">
                                        <IconButton size="small" title="Articles">
                                            <Link to={`/dashboard/articles-by-category/${sub._id}`} >
                                                <BookOpen size={16} />
                                            </Link>
                                        </IconButton>

                                        <IconButton onClick={() => { setEditingSubCategory(sub); setIsFormVisible(true); }} size="small" title="Edit"><Edit size={16} /></IconButton>
                                        <IconButton onClick={() => handleDeleteClick(sub)} size="small" title="Delete" color="error"><Trash2 size={16} /></IconButton>
                                    </div>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    ) : (
                        !isFormVisible && <p className="text-gray-500 text-center py-4">This category has no sub-categories yet.</p>
                    )}
                </div>
            </ModalWrapper>

            <ConfirmModal isOpen={isDeleteConfirmOpen} onClose={() => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false)} title="Confirm Delete" onConfirm={confirmDelete} isLoading={isDeleting} confirmButtonVariant="danger">
                <p>Are you sure you want to delete the sub-category: <strong>{subCategoryToDelete?.name}</strong>?</p>
            </ConfirmModal>
        </>
    );
};

export default SubCategoryModal

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ChangePass\ChangePass.jsx ---

// File: src/Pages/ChangePass/ChangePass.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Card, Heading, Input, Button } from '../../components/components'; // Assuming components.js exports these
import { useChangePasswordMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/userSlice.js'; // Corrected path
import { Lock, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';

const ChangePass = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        oldPassword: '',
        newPassword: '',
        confirmNewPassword: '',
    });
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});

    const [changePassword, { isLoading }] = useChangePasswordMutation();

    const handleChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
        if (errors[name]) {
            setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: null }));
        }
        if (name === "newPassword" || name === "confirmNewPassword") {
            if (name === "confirmNewPassword" && formData.newPassword && value !== formData.newPassword) {
                setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, confirmNewPassword: "New passwords do not match." }));
            } else if (name === "newPassword" && formData.confirmNewPassword && value !== formData.confirmNewPassword) {
                setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, confirmNewPassword: "New passwords do not match." }));
            }
            else {
                setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, confirmNewPassword: null }));
            }
        }
    };

    const validateForm = () => {
        const newErrors = {};
        if (!formData.oldPassword) newErrors.oldPassword = "Current password is required.";
        if (!formData.newPassword) newErrors.newPassword = "New password is required.";
        else if (formData.newPassword.length < 6) newErrors.newPassword = "New password must be at least 6 characters."; // Basic length validation
        if (!formData.confirmNewPassword) newErrors.confirmNewPassword = "Please confirm your new password.";
        else if (formData.newPassword !== formData.confirmNewPassword) newErrors.confirmNewPassword = "New passwords do not match.";

        setErrors(newErrors);
        return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0;
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!validateForm()) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            const payload = {
                oldPassword: formData.oldPassword,
                newPassword: formData.newPassword,
            };
            const res = await changePassword(payload).unwrap();
            toast.success(res?.message || 'Password changed successfully!');
            setFormData({ oldPassword: '', newPassword: '', confirmNewPassword: '' }); // Clear form
            // Optionally navigate away, e.g., to profile page or dashboard
            // navigate('/dashboard/profile');
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || err.message || 'Failed to change password. Please check your current password.');
            if (err.data?.errors) { // Example: if backend sends field-specific errors
                setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, ...err.data.errors }));
            }
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            <Button
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() => navigate(-1)} // Go back to previous page
                className="mb-6 flex items-center"
                size="sm"
            >
                <ArrowLeft size={16} className="mr-2" />
                Back
            </Button>

            <Card>
                <div className="p-6">
                    <Heading level={2} title="Change Your Password" className="mb-6 text-center" />
                    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
                        <Input
                            type="password"
                            label="Current Password"
                            name="oldPassword"
                            value={formData.oldPassword}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            placeholder="Enter your current password"
                            leftIcon={<Lock size={18} />}
                            required
                            error={errors.oldPassword}
                        />
                        <Input
                            type="password"
                            label="New Password"
                            name="newPassword"
                            value={formData.newPassword}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            placeholder="Enter your new password"
                            leftIcon={<Lock size={18} />}
                            required
                            error={errors.newPassword}
                        />
                        <Input
                            type="password"
                            label="Confirm New Password"
                            name="confirmNewPassword"
                            value={formData.confirmNewPassword}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            placeholder="Confirm your new password"
                            leftIcon={<Lock size={18} />}
                            required
                            error={errors.confirmNewPassword}
                        />
                        <div className="pt-2">
                            <Button type="submit" fullWidth isLoading={isLoading} disabled={isLoading}>
                                {isLoading ? 'Updating Password...' : 'Change Password'}
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </Card>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ChangePass;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\CommentModeration\CommentModeration.jsx ---

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useGetCommentsQuery } from '../../_core/Slices/commentSlice';
import { Heading, Button, Card, Select } from '../../components/components';
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';

import CommentRow from './CommentRow'; // Adjust path if needed
import { MessageSquare, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';

const CommentModeration = () => {
    // --- State for Filtering and Pagination ---
    const [filter, setFilter] = useState({
        isApproved: false, // Default to the most actionable filter
        page: 1,
        limit: 10,
    });

    // --- RTK Query Hook ---
    // The query will automatically refetch when `filter` state changes
    const {
        data: response,
        isLoading,
        isFetching,
        isError,
        error,
    } = useGetCommentsQuery(filter);

    const comments = response?.comments || [];
    const totalPages = response?.totalPages || 1;

    // --- Event Handlers ---
    const handleFilterChange = (e) => {
        if (e.target.value == "all") {
            setFilter("")
        } else {
            setFilter(prev => ({ ...prev, isApproved: e.target.value, page: 1 })); // Reset to page 1 on filter change

        }
    };

    const handlePageChange = (newPage) => {
        setFilter(prev => ({ ...prev, page: newPage }));
    };

    const statusOptions = [
        { value: false, label: 'Pending Review' },
        { value: true, label: 'Approved' },
        { value: 'all', label: 'All Comments' }, // Assuming backend supports fetching all
    ];

    // --- Render Logic ---
    let content;
    if (isLoading) {
        content = <div className="h-screen w-full flex justify-center items-center">
            <CircularProgressBox />
        </div>;;
    } else if (isError) {
        content = <p className="text-center text-red-500 p-4">Error: {error.data?.message || 'Could not fetch comments.'}</p>;
    } else if (comments.length === 0) {
        content = (
            <div className="text-center py-12">
                <MessageSquare className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-gray-400" />
                <h3 className="mt-2 text-lg font-medium text-gray-900">No Comments Found</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">There are no comments matching the selected filter.</p>
            </div>
        );
    } else {
        content = (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="min-w-full bg-white text-sm">
                    <thead className="bg-gray-100 border-b">
                        <tr>
                            <th scope="col" className="text-left font-semibold text-gray-600 p-4">Comment & Author</th>
                            <th scope="col" className="text-left font-semibold text-gray-600 p-4">In Response To</th>
                            <th scope="col" className="text-center font-semibold text-gray-600 p-4">Status</th>
                            <th scope="col" className="text-center font-semibold text-gray-600 p-4">Actions</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        {comments.map(comment => (
                            <CommentRow key={comment._id} comment={comment} />
                        ))}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        );
    }

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Comment Moderation"
                description="Approve, edit, or delete user comments."
            />

            <Card>
                <div className="p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
                    <div className="w-full sm:w-auto">
                        <Select
                            label="Filter by status"
                            placeholder="All Comments"
                            options={statusOptions}
                            value={filter.isApproved}
                            onChange={handleFilterChange}
                            name="status"
                        />
                    </div>
                    {isFetching && <RefreshCw className="animate-spin text-gray-400" />}
                </div>

                {content}

                {/* Pagination Controls */}
                {!isLoading && !isError && comments.length > 0 && totalPages > 1 && (
                    <div className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between border-t">
                        <span className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                            Page {filter.page} of {totalPages}
                        </span>
                        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                            <Button
                                variant="outline" size="sm"
                                onClick={() => handlePageChange(filter.page - 1)}
                                disabled={filter.page === 1 || isFetching}
                            >
                                Previous
                            </Button>
                            <Button
                                variant="outline" size="sm"
                                onClick={() => handlePageChange(filter.page + 1)}
                                disabled={filter.page === totalPages || isFetching}
                            >
                                Next
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                )}
            </Card>
        </div>
    );
};

export default CommentModeration;
