--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\CommentModeration\CommentRow.jsx ---

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useUpdateCommentStatusMutation, useDeleteCommentMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/commentSlice';
import { CheckCircle, XCircle, Trash2, Edit } from 'lucide-react';
import { Avatar, Tooltip } from '@mui/material';
import { Button } from '../../components/components';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';

const CommentRow = ({ comment }) => {
    const { _id, content, user, article, isApproved, createdAt } = comment;

    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);

    // Mutation hooks for this specific row's actions
    const [updateStatus, { isLoading: isUpdating }] = useUpdateCommentStatusMutation();
    const [deleteComment, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteCommentMutation();

    const handleUpdateStatus = async (newStatus) => {
        try {
            await updateStatus({ commentId: _id, isApproved: newStatus }).unwrap();
            toast.success(`Comment ${newStatus ? 'approved' : 'unapproved'}.`);
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || 'Failed to update status.');
        }
    };

    const handleDelete = async () => {
        try {
            await deleteComment(_id).unwrap();
            toast.success('Comment deleted successfully.');
            // No need to close modal manually if the component unmounts on list refetch
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || 'Failed to delete comment.');
        } finally {
            setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
        }
    };

    return (
        <>
            <tr className="border-b hover:bg-gray-50">
                <td className="p-4 align-top">
                    <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                        <Avatar src={user?.avatar?.url} alt={user?.username} />
                        <div>
                            <p className="font-semibold text-sm">{user?.username || 'Anonymous'}</p>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{new Date(createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>
                            <p className="mt-2 text-gray-700 text-sm">{content}</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </td>
                <td className="p-4 align-top text-sm">
                    {article ? (
                        <Link to={`/article/${article.slug}`} className="text-blue-600 hover:underline">
                            {article.title}
                        </Link>
                    ) : (
                        'N/A'
                    )}
                </td>
                <td className="p-4 align-top text-center">
                    <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium ${isApproved ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800'}`}>
                        {isApproved ? 'Approved' : 'Pending'}
                    </span>
                </td>
                <td className="p-4 align-top text-center">
                    <div className="flex justify-center items-center space-x-2">
                        {isApproved ? (
                            <Tooltip title="Unapprove">
                                <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" onClick={() => handleUpdateStatus(false)} disabled={isUpdating}>
                                    <XCircle className="text-orange-500" size={20} />
                                </Button>
                            </Tooltip>
                        ) : (
                            <Tooltip title="Approve">
                                <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" onClick={() => handleUpdateStatus(true)} disabled={isUpdating}>
                                    <CheckCircle className="text-green-500" size={20} />
                                </Button>
                            </Tooltip>
                        )}
                        <Tooltip title="Delete">
                            <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(true)} disabled={isDeleting}>
                                <Trash2 className="text-red-500" size={20} />
                            </Button>
                        </Tooltip>
                    </div>
                </td>
            </tr>

            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={handleDelete}
                title="Delete Comment"
                isLoading={isDeleting}
            >
                Are you sure you want to permanently delete this comment? This action cannot be undone.
            </ConfirmModal>
        </>
    );
};

export default CommentRow;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\CreateGallery\CreateGallery.jsx ---

"use client"

import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { useCreateGalleryMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/gallerySlice';
import {
    Heading,
    Button,
    Card,
    Input,
    Select,
    Textarea,
} from '../../components/components';
import { ArrowLeft, Trash2, UploadCloud, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission';

// Basic hasPermission utility (should be centralized)


const CreateGallery = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { userInfo } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
    const [createGallery, { isLoading: isCreatingGallery }] = useCreateGalleryMutation();

    // --- State Management ---
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        title: '',
        description: '',
        status: 'published',
        tags: [], // **CHANGED**: Now an array
    });
    const [currentTag, setCurrentTag] = useState(''); // State for the tag input field
    const [galleryImages, setGalleryImages] = useState([]); // Array of File objects
    const [imagePreviews, setImagePreviews] = useState([]);
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

    // --- Event Handlers ---
    const handleInputChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    };

    const handleFileChange = (e) => {
        const files = Array.from(e.target.files);
        if (files.length > 20) {
            toast.error("You can upload a maximum of 20 images at a time.");
            return;
        }

        const validFiles = files.filter(file => {
            if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
                toast.error(`${file.name} is not a valid image.`);
                return false;
            }
            if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) { // 5MB limit
                toast.error(`${file.name} exceeds the 5MB size limit.`);
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        });

        setGalleryImages(prev => [...prev, ...validFiles]);
        const previews = validFiles.map(file => URL.createObjectURL(file));
        setImagePreviews(prev => [...prev, ...previews]);
    };

    const removeImage = (indexToRemove) => {
        // Revoke the object URL to prevent memory leaks
        URL.revokeObjectURL(imagePreviews[indexToRemove]);

        setGalleryImages(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== indexToRemove));
        setImagePreviews(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== indexToRemove));

        // This part is tricky and often not reliable across browsers.
        // It's usually better to just re-select files if needed.
        // For simplicity, we just clear the input visually.
        if (fileInputRef.current) {
            fileInputRef.current.value = "";
        }
    };

    // **NEW**: Handler for adding a tag
    const handleTagKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key !== 'Enter' || !currentTag.trim()) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        const newTag = currentTag.trim();
        if (newTag && !formData.tags.includes(newTag)) {
            setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, tags: [...prev.tags, newTag] }));
        }
        setCurrentTag(''); // Reset the input field
    };

    // **NEW**: Handler for removing a tag
    const removeTag = (tagToRemove) => {
        setFormData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            tags: prev.tags.filter(tag => tag !== tagToRemove),
        }));
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (galleryImages.length === 0) {
            toast.error("Please select at least one image for the gallery.");
            return;
        }
        if (!formData.title.trim()) {
            toast.error("Title is required.");
            return;
        }

        const data = new FormData();
        data.append('title', formData.title);
        data.append('description', formData.description);
        data.append('status', formData.status);

        // **CHANGED**: Append each tag to the FormData object
        // The `[]` in 'tags[]' tells backend frameworks like Express (with multer/busboy)
        // to parse this into an array.
        formData.tags.forEach(tag => {
            data.append('tags[]', tag);
        });

        galleryImages.forEach(file => {
            data.append('galleryImages', file);
        });

        try {
            await createGallery(data).unwrap();
            toast.success('Gallery created successfully!');
            navigate('/dashboard/galleries');
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || 'Failed to create gallery.');
        }
    };

    // --- Render Logic ---
    const userCanCreate = hasPermission('editor');

    if (!userCanCreate) {
        return (
            <div className="p-6 text-center">
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-red-600">Access Denied</h1>
                <p>You do not have permission to create image galleries.</p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    const statusOptions = [
        { value: 'draft', label: 'Draft' },
        { value: 'published', label: 'Published' },
    ];

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Create New Image Gallery"
                actions={
                    <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/galleries')} icon={<ArrowLeft className="size-4" />}>
                        Back to Galleries
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            <Card className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">
                    <Input name="title" label="Gallery Title" value={formData.title} onChange={handleInputChange} placeholder="e.g., Summer Vacation 2024" required />
                    <Textarea name="description" label="Description (Optional)" value={formData.description} onChange={handleInputChange} placeholder="A short description of the gallery..." rows={3} />

                    {/* **NEW**: Updated Tag Input Section */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Tags</label>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg">
                            {formData.tags.map((tag, index) => (
                                <span key={index} className="flex items-center bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 text-sm font-medium pl-2.5 pr-1 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                                    {tag}
                                    <button type="button" onClick={() => removeTag(tag)} className="ml-1.5 p-0.5 rounded-full hover:bg-blue-200">
                                        <X className="size-3" />
                                    </button>
                                </span>
                            ))}
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={currentTag}
                                onChange={(e) => setCurrentTag(e.target.value)}
                                onKeyDown={handleTagKeyDown}
                                className="flex-1 min-w-[120px] p-1 outline-none border-none bg-transparent placeholder-gray-500 text-sm"
                                placeholder={formData.tags.length === 0 ? "Type a tag and press Enter" : "Add another tag..."}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* <Select name="status" label="Status" value={formData.status} onChange={handleInputChange} options={statusOptions} /> */}

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Gallery Images (Max 20)</label>
                        <div className="mt-1 flex justify-center px-6 pt-5 pb-6 border-2 border-gray-300 border-dashed rounded-md">
                            <div className="space-y-1 text-center">
                                <UploadCloud className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-gray-400" />
                                <div className="flex text-sm text-gray-600">
                                    <label htmlFor="file-upload" className="relative cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-md font-medium text-primary hover:text-primary-focus focus-within:outline-none focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-offset-2 focus-within:ring-primary">
                                        <span>Upload files</span>
                                        <input id="file-upload" name="galleryImages" type="file" className="sr-only" multiple onChange={handleFileChange} ref={fileInputRef} accept="image/*" />
                                    </label>
                                    <p className="pl-1">or drag and drop</p>
                                </div>
                                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">PNG, JPG, GIF up to 5MB each</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {imagePreviews.length > 0 && (
                            <div className="mt-4">
                                <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Selected Images:</h3>
                                <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 md:grid-cols-6 gap-4">
                                    {imagePreviews.map((previewUrl, index) => (
                                        <div key={index} className="relative group aspect-square">
                                            <img src={previewUrl} alt={`Preview ${index + 1}`} className="h-full w-full object-cover rounded-md shadow-md" />
                                            <div className="absolute inset-0 group-hover:bg-opacity-50 transition-all flex items-start justify-end">
                                                <Button
                                                    size="icon"
                                                    variant="destructive"
                                                    className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 p-2"
                                                    onClick={() => removeImage(index)}
                                                    title="Remove image"
                                                >
                                                    <Trash2 size={20} className='shadow-2xl' color='red' />
                                                </Button>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    ))}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end pt-4 border-t">
                        <Button type="submit" disabled={isCreatingGallery} isLoading={isCreatingGallery}>
                            {isCreatingGallery ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Gallery'}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </Card>
        </div>
    );
};

export default CreateGallery;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\Dashboard\Dashboard.jsx ---

import React, { useState } from 'react'
import { Outlet } from 'react-router'
import Header from '../../components/Header/Header';
import Sidebar from '../../components/Sidebar/Sidebar';

export default function Dashboard() {
  const [isSidebarCollapsed, setIsSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false)
  const [isMobileSidebarOpen, setIsMobileSidebarOpen] = useState(false)

  const toggleSidebar = () => {
    if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
      setIsMobileSidebarOpen(!isMobileSidebarOpen)
    } else {
      setIsSidebarCollapsed(!isSidebarCollapsed)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-[96vh] bg-gray-50 font-inter overflow-hidden">
      <Sidebar
        setIsColapsed={setIsSidebarCollapsed}
        isCollapsed={isSidebarCollapsed}
        isMobileOpen={isMobileSidebarOpen}
        toggleSidebar={toggleSidebar}
      />

      <div className={`flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden `}>
        <Header toggleSidebar={toggleSidebar} />

        {/* Main Content Area */}
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 bg-gray-50">
          <Outlet />
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

"use client"

import { TrendingUp, Users, FileText, Edit, Eye, ArrowUpRight, ArrowDownRight, MoreHorizontal, Download, MoreHorizontalIcon, Trash2 } from "lucide-react"
import {
    PieChart,
    Pie,
    Cell,
    BarChart,
    Bar,
    XAxis,
    YAxis,
    CartesianGrid,
    Tooltip,
    ResponsiveContainer,
    Area,
    AreaChart,
} from "recharts"
import { Heading, Card, Button, MuiTable } from "../../components/components"
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';

import { Alert, Chip, IconButton } from "@mui/material"
import { useGetDashboardStatsQuery } from "../../_core/Slices/dashboardSlice";
import { useDeleteArticleMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/articleSlice";
import { formatDate } from "../../utils/formatters.js";
import { useNavigate, Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useState } from "react"
import { toast } from "react-toastify";

import DropdownMenu from '../../components/DropdownMenu/DropdownMenu';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
import getRole from "../../Helper/getRole.js";

const Dashboard = () => {

    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [articleToDelete, setArticleToDelete] = useState(null);
    const { data: apiResponse, isLoading, isError, error, refetch } = useGetDashboardStatsQuery(undefined, {
        refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true
    }); // refetch ko yahan se nikal lein
    const [deleteArticle, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteArticleMutation();
    const dashboardStats = apiResponse?.data;


    const handleDeleteClick = (article) => {
        setArticleToDelete(article);
        setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
    };

    const confirmDelete = async () => {
        if (!articleToDelete) return;

        try {
            await deleteArticle(articleToDelete._id).unwrap();
            toast.success(`Article "${articleToDelete.title}" deleted successfully!`);
            setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
            setArticleToDelete(null);
            refetch();
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || "Failed to delete the article.");
            setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
        }
    };
    if (isLoading) {
        return <CircularProgressBox text="Loading Dashboard Data..." />;
    }

    if (isError) {
        const role = getRole()
        return (
            <div>
                <Alert severity="error" sx={{ m: 2 }}>
                    Failed to load dashboard data: {error.data?.message || "Please check your connection and try again."}
                </Alert>
                {role == "reader" ? <Button onClick={() => { navigate('/dashboard/profile-settings') }}>Your Profile</Button> :
                    <Button onClick={() => { navigate('/dashboard/create-article') }}>Create Aricles</Button>}
            </div>

        );
    }


    const columns = [
        {
            field: "title",
            header: "Article",
            render: (value, row) => (
                <span
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/dashboard/edit-article/${row.slug}`)}
                    className="font-semibold block whitespace-normal cursor-pointer hover:text-primary transition-colors w-xs "
                    title={value}
                >
                    {value}
                </span>
            )
        },
        {
            field: "author",
            header: "Author",
            render: (author) => author?.fullName || author?.username || "N/A"
        },
        {
            field: "category",
            header: "Category",
            render: (category) => <Chip label={category?.name || "Uncategorized"} variant="outlined" size="small" />
        },
        {
            field: "views",
            header: "Views",
            align: "center"
        },
        {
            field: "status",
            header: "Status",
            align: "center",
            render: (value) => (
                <Chip
                    label={value}
                    size="small"
                    color={value === "published" ? "success" : "warning"}
                    className="capitalize"
                />
            ),
        },
        {
            field: "createdAt",
            header: "Date",
            render: (date) => formatDate(date)
        },
        {
            field: "action",
            header: "Action",
            align: "center",
            render: (value, row) => {
                const dropdownItems = [
                    [
                        {
                            label: 'Edit',
                            icon: <Edit size={16} />,
                            onClick: () => navigate(`/dashboard/edit-article/${row.slug}`)
                        },
                        {
                            label: 'View Public',
                            icon: <Eye size={16} />,
                            onClick: () => window.open(`/article/${row.slug}`, "_blank"),
                            disabled: row.status !== 'published'
                        },
                    ],
                    [
                        {
                            label: 'Delete',
                            icon: <Trash2 size={16} />,
                            className: 'text-red-600',
                            onClick: () => handleDeleteClick(row)
                        }
                    ]
                ];

                return <DropdownMenu items={dropdownItems} />;
            }
        },
    ];

    // Pie chart ke liye colors (dynamic)
    const PIE_COLORS = ["#3D72FA", "#10B981", "#F59E0B", "#EF4444", "#8B5CF6", "#6366F1", "#14B8A6"];

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">

            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={confirmDelete}
                title="Confirm Article Deletion"
                isLoading={isDeleting}
                variant={"danger"}
            >
                Are you sure you want to permanently delete the article:
                <br />
                <strong>"{articleToDelete?.title}"</strong>?
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-500">This action cannot be undone.</p>
            </ConfirmModal>
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Dashboard Overview"
                description="Monitor your content performance and user engagement metrics"
            />

            {/* Stats Cards */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
                {dashboardStats?.cards?.map((stat) => (
                    <Card key={stat.title}>
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
                            <div>
                                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600">{stat.title}</p>
                                <p className="text-2xl font-semibold text-text-dark mt-1">{stat.value}</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                ))}
            </div>

            {/* Charts Section */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                {/* Pie Chart */}
                <Card>
                    <Card.Header title="Articles by Category" />
                    <Card.Body>
                        <div className="h-64">
                            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                                <PieChart>
                                    <Pie data={dashboardStats?.articlesByCategory} dataKey="value" nameKey="name" cx="50%" cy="50%" innerRadius={60} outerRadius={100} paddingAngle={2}>
                                        {dashboardStats?.articlesByCategory?.map((entry, index) => (
                                            <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={PIE_COLORS[index % PIE_COLORS.length]} />
                                        ))}
                                    </Pie>
                                    <Tooltip />
                                </PieChart>
                            </ResponsiveContainer>
                        </div>
                    </Card.Body>
                </Card>

                {/* Bar Chart */}
                <Card>
                    <Card.Header title="Monthly Performance" />
                    <Card.Body>
                        <div className="h-64">
                            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                                <BarChart data={dashboardStats?.monthlyPerformance}>
                                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} />
                                    <XAxis dataKey="name" fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                                    <YAxis fontSize={12} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} />
                                    <Tooltip />
                                    <Bar dataKey="articles" fill="#3D72FA" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                                </BarChart>
                            </ResponsiveContainer>
                        </div>
                    </Card.Body>
                </Card>
            </div>

            {/* Recent Articles Table */}
            <MuiTable
                title="Recent Articles"
                subtitle="Latest published and draft articles"
                columns={columns}
                rows={dashboardStats?.recentArticles || []}
                showPagination={false}
            />
        </div>
    );
};

export default Dashboard;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\EditArticle\EditArticle.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useRef, useMemo, useEffect } from "react"
import { Plus, Eye, Globe, Tag, FileText, Save, Upload, X, Star, Zap, Award, ChevronDown, Clock, Image, Pin, LucideHighlighter, PenBox } from "lucide-react"
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom"
import { ARTICLE_STATUS_OPTIONS, ARTICLE_LAYOUT_OPTIONS } from "../../constants/articleConstants.js";
// Custom UI components
import { Button, Heading, Input, Card, Select, Textarea } from "../../components/components";
import WordEditor from "../../components/WordEditor/WordEditor";
import CircularProgressBox from "../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox";

// RTK Query Hooks
import {
    useGetAdminArticlesBySlugQuery,
    useUpdateArticleMutation
} from "../../_core/Slices/articleSlice"
import { useGetCategoriesQuery } from "../../_core/Slices/categorySlice"
import { useSelector } from "react-redux"
import { hasPermission } from "../../Helper/hasPermission"
import { toast } from "react-toastify"

const EditArticle = () => {
    const { slug } = useParams()
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    // --- Component State ---
        const [factBoxContent, setFactBoxContent] = useState("");

    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        title: "",
        content: "",
        category: "",
        excerpt: "",
        parentCategory: null,
        imageCaption: "",
        tags: [],
        slug: "",
        status: "draft",
        featuredImage: null,
        existingImage: null,
        publishedAt: "",
        isPinned: false,         // <-- NEW
        isHighlighted: false,
        isFeatured: false,
        isOpinion: false,
        isTopHeadline: false,
        isEditorChoice: false,
        metaTitle: "",
        metaDescription: ""
    })
    const [currentTag, setCurrentTag] = useState("")
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({})
    const [rootCategory, setRootCategory] = useState(null)
    const [isSeoOpen, setIsSeoOpen] = useState(false)
    const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState(null)
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null)

    // --- RTK Query Hooks ---
    const {
        data: articleData,
        isLoading: isLoadingArticle,
        isError: isArticleError,
        error: articleError,
        refetch
    } = useGetAdminArticlesBySlugQuery(slug)

    const [updateArticle, { isLoading: isSubmitting }] = useUpdateArticleMutation()
    const { data: categoriesData, isLoading: isLoadingCategories, isError: isCategoryError, error: categoryFetchError } = useGetCategoriesQuery()


    // --- Memoized Data Transformation ---
    const categoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
        if (!categoriesData) return []
        return categoriesData?.map(cat => ({
            value: cat._id,
            label: cat.name,
            description: cat.description,
        }))
    }, [categoriesData])

    const subCategoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
        if (!rootCategory) return []
        const parentCategory = categoriesData?.find((cat) => cat._id === rootCategory)
        return parentCategory?.subcategories?.map(cat => ({
            value: cat._id,
            label: cat.name,
            description: cat.description,
        })) || []
    }, [categoriesData, rootCategory])

    // --- Static Options ---
    const statusOptions = [
        { value: "draft", label: "Draft" },
        { value: "published", label: "Published" },
        { value: "scheduled", label: "Schedule" },
        { value: "archived", label: "Archived" },
    ]


    useEffect(() => {
        if (articleData && categoriesData) {
            const article = articleData;


            setFormData({
                title: article.title || "",
                content: article.content || "",
                excerpt: article.excerpt || "",
                imageCaption: article.imageCaption || "",
                tags: article.tags || [],
                slug: article.slug || "",
                status: article.status || "draft",
                existingImage: article.featuredImage,
                isOpinion: article.isOpinion || false,
                publishedAt: article.publishedAt ? new Date(article.publishedAt).toISOString().slice(0, 16) : "",
                isFeatured: article.isFeatured || false,
                isTopHeadline: article.isTopHeadline || false,
                isEditorChoice: article.isEditorChoice || false,
                isPinned: article.isPinned || false,             // <-- NEW
                isHighlighted: article.isHighlighted || false,
                layoutType: article.layoutType || "normal",
                metaTitle: article.metaTitle || "",
                metaDescription: article.metaDescription || ""
            });
            setFactBoxContent(article.factBoxContent || "");


            if (article.category) {
                const savedCategory = article.category;


                if (savedCategory.parentCategory && savedCategory.parentCategory._id) {

                    setRootCategory(savedCategory.parentCategory._id);

                    setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, category: savedCategory._id }));


                } else {

                    setRootCategory(savedCategory._id);

                    setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, category: "" }));
                }
            } else {

                setRootCategory(null);
                setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, category: "" }));
            }


            if (article.featuredImage?.url) {
                setImagePreview(article.featuredImage.url);
            }
        }
    }, [articleData, categoriesData]);

    // --- Helper & Event Handler Functions ---
    const generateSlug = (title) => title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 -]/g, "").replace(/\s+/g, "-").replace(/-+/g, "-").trim("-")

    const handleChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value, type, checked } = e.target;
        const newValue = type === 'checkbox' ? checked : value;
        setFormData((prev) => {
            const newData = { ...prev, [name]: newValue }
            if (name === "title") newData.slug = generateSlug(value)
            return newData
        })
        if (errors[name]) setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: undefined }))
    }

    const handleEditorChange = (html) => {
        setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, content: html }))
        if (errors.content) setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, content: undefined }))
    }
    const handleFactBoxEditorChange = (html) => {
    setFactBoxContent(html);
};

    const handleFileUpload = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0]
        if (!file) return
        if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
            setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: "Please select a valid image file." }))
            return
        }
        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
            setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: "Image size cannot exceed 5MB." }))
            return
        }
        setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: file }))
        setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: undefined }))
        const reader = new FileReader()
        reader.onloadend = () => setImagePreview(reader.result)
        reader.readAsDataURL(file)
    }

    const removeFeaturedImage = () => {
        setFormData((prev) => ({
            ...prev,
            featuredImage: null,
            existingImage: null
        }))
        setImagePreview(null)
        if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = ""
    }

    const handleTagKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key !== "Enter" || !currentTag.trim()) return
        e.preventDefault()
        const newTag = currentTag.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\b\w/g, char => char.toUpperCase())
        if (!formData.tags.includes(newTag)) {
            setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, tags: [...prev.tags, newTag] }))
        }
        setCurrentTag("")
    }

    const removeTag = (tagToRemove) => setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, tags: prev.tags.filter((tag) => tag !== tagToRemove) }))

    const validateForm = () => {
        const newErrors = {}
        if (!formData.title.trim()) newErrors.title = "Title is required"
        if (!formData.content.trim()) newErrors.content = "Content is required"
        if (!rootCategory) newErrors.category = "Category is required"
        if (formData.status === 'scheduled' && !formData.publishedAt) {
            newErrors.publishedAt = "Please select a publish date and time for scheduling."
        }
        setErrors(newErrors)
        return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0
    }

    const handleSubmit = async (e, submissionStatus = formData.status) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!validateForm()) return

        const data = new FormData()
        data.append("title", formData.title)
        data.append("content", formData.content)
        // data.append("category", formData.category !== rootCategory ? rootCategory : formData.category)
        data.append("excerpt", formData.excerpt)
        data.append("imageCaption", formData.imageCaption || "")
            data.append("factBoxContent", factBoxContent);

        data.append("status", submissionStatus)
        data.append("isFeatured", formData.isFeatured)
        data.append("isTopHeadline", formData.isTopHeadline)
        data.append("isPinned", formData.isPinned)
        data.append("isHighlighted", formData.isHighlighted)
        data.append("isEditorChoice", formData.isEditorChoice)
        data.append("layoutType", formData.layoutType);
        data.append("metaDescription", formData.metaDescription)
        data.append("metaTitle", formData.metaTitle)
        data.append("isOpinion", formData.isOpinion)

        const finalCategoryId = formData.category || rootCategory;
        data.append("category", finalCategoryId);
        if (submissionStatus === 'scheduled' && formData.publishedAt) {
            data.append("publishedAt", new Date(formData.publishedAt).toISOString())
        }

        if (formData.tags.length > 0) {
            formData.tags.forEach(tag => data.append('tags[]', tag))
        }

        if (formData.featuredImage) {
            data.append("featuredImage", formData.featuredImage)
        } else if (!formData.existingImage) {
            data.append("removeFeaturedImage", "true")
        }

        try {
            await updateArticle({ id: articleData?._id, data }).unwrap()
            toast.success(`Article successfully updated as ${submissionStatus}!`)
            refetch()
            navigate(-1)
        } catch (err) {
            console.error("Failed to update article:", err)
            toast.error(`Error: ${err.data?.message || "An unexpected error occurred."}`)
        }
    }

    const handleSaveAsDraft = (e) => handleSubmit(e, 'draft')
    const handleSaveAsPreview = (e) => handleSubmit(e, 'pending-review')

    const handlePreview = () => {
        const previewWindow = window.open("", "_blank")
        if (!previewWindow) {
            alert("Please allow popups to preview the article.")
            return
        }

        const previewStyles = `
            body { 
                font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif; 
                line-height: 1.6; 
                color: #333; 
                max-width: 800px; 
                margin: 40px auto; 
                padding: 20px; 
            }
            h1 { font-size: 2.5em; }
            .meta { color: #666; }
            .featured-image { max-width: 100%; height: auto; border-radius: 8px; margin: 1em 0; }
            .tag { display: inline-block; background-color: #f0f0f0; padding: 5px 10px; margin-right: 5px; border-radius: 15px; font-size: 0.8em; }
            .article-content {
                white-space: pre-wrap; 
                word-wrap: break-word; 
            }
        `

        const categoryLabel = categoryOptions.find(c => c.value === formData.category)?.label ||
            subCategoryOptions.find(c => c.value === formData.category)?.label ||
            'N/A'
        const tagsHtml = formData.tags.length > 0 ?
            `<div><strong>Tags:</strong> ${formData.tags.map(tag => `<span class="tag">${tag}</span>`).join(" ")}</div>` :
            ''

        previewWindow.document.write(`
            <html>
            <head>
                <title>Preview: ${formData.title}</title>
                <style>${previewStyles}</style>
            </head>
            <body>
                <h1>${formData.title}</h1>
                <div class="meta">
                    <span>Category: ${categoryLabel}</span> | <span>Status: ${formData.status}</span>
                </div>
                ${imagePreview ? `<img src="${imagePreview}" alt="Featured" class="featured-image" />` : ""}
                <div class="article-content">${formData.content}</div>
                ${tagsHtml}
            </body>
            </html>
        `)
        previewWindow.document.close()
    }

    // --- Conditional Rendering ---
    if (isLoadingArticle || isLoadingCategories) {
        return (
            <div className="h-screen w-full flex justify-center items-center">
                <CircularProgressBox />
            </div>
        )
    }

    if (isArticleError) {
        return (
            <div className="p-6 text-red-600">
                Error loading article: {articleError.data?.message || 'Please try again later.'}
            </div>
        )
    }

    if (isCategoryError) {
        return (
            <div className="p-6 text-red-600">
                Error loading categories: {categoryFetchError.data?.message || 'Please try again later.'}
            </div>
        )
    }

    const canPublish = hasPermission('editor')

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={2}
                title="Edit Article"
                description="Update and refine your content for your audience"
                actions={
                    <Button variant="outline" type="button" onClick={handlePreview} icon={<Eye className="size-4" />}>
                        Preview Article
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            <>
                <form onSubmit={(e) => handleSubmit(e, canPublish ? formData.status : 'pending-review')} className="space-y-6">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                        <Input
                            id="title"
                            name="title"
                            label="Article Title"
                            value={formData.title}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            required
                            placeholder="Enter an engaging article title"
                            error={errors.title}
                            leftIcon={<FileText className="size-4" />}
                        />

                        <Input
                            id="slug"
                            name="slug"
                            label="URL Slug"
                            disabled
                            value={formData.slug}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            placeholder="url-friendly-slug"
                            readOnly
                            leftIcon={<Globe className="size-4" />}
                            helperText="Auto-generated from title."
                        />
                    </div>

                    <Input
                        inputClassname={"w-full"}
                        id="excerpt"
                        name="excerpt"
                        label="Article Excerpt"
                        value={formData.excerpt}
                        onChange={handleChange}
                        placeholder="Enter a short description of article"
                        error={errors.title}
                        leftIcon={<FileText className="size-4" />}
                    />

                    {/* Featured Image Upload */}
                    <div className="">

                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2 mt-3 ">Featured Image</label>
                        <div className="space-y-3">
                            {imagePreview ? (
                                <div className="relative inline-block">
                                    <img src={imagePreview} alt="Featured image preview" className="w-full max-w-md h-48 object-cover rounded-lg border" />
                                    <button type="button" onClick={removeFeaturedImage} className="absolute top-2 right-2 p-1 bg-red-500 text-white rounded-full">
                                        <X className="size-4" />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            ) : (
                                <div onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} className="border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg p-6 text-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <Upload className="size-8 text-gray-400 mx-auto mb-2" />
                                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Click to upload</p>
                                </div>
                            )}
                            <input
                                ref={fileInputRef}
                                type="file"
                                accept="image/*"
                                onChange={handleFileUpload}
                                className="hidden"
                            />
                            {errors.featuredImage && <p className="text-xs text-red-500">{errors.featuredImage}</p>}
                        </div>
                        <Input
                            inputClassname={"w-full my-3"}
                            id="imageCaption"
                            name="imageCaption"
                            label="Image Caption"
                            value={formData.imageCaption}
                            onChange={handleChange} placeholder="Enter caption of image" leftIcon={<Image className="size-4" />} />
                    </div>

                    {/* Rich Text Editor Section */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Article Content <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                        <WordEditor content={formData.content} onChange={handleEditorChange} />
                        {errors.content && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-red-500">{errors.content}</p>}
                    </div>
                            <Card className="mt-6">
                <Card.Header title="Fact Box / Summary (Optional)" />
                <Card.Body className="p-4">
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-2">
                        Content added here will be displayed in a special styled box within the article.
                    </p>
                    <WordEditor
                        content={factBoxContent}
                        onChange={handleFactBoxEditorChange}
                        placeholder="Add key takeaways, a summary, or an address here..."
                    />
                </Card.Body>
            </Card>
            
                    {/* Category and Status Section */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                        <Select
                            label="Category"
                            value={rootCategory}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                                setRootCategory(e.target.value);

                                setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, category: "" }));
                            }}
                            options={categoryOptions}
                            placeholder="Select a category"
                            required
                            error={errors.category}
                        />
                        <Select
                            disabled={!rootCategory}
                            id="category"
                            name="category"
                            label="Topic"
                            value={formData.category}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            options={subCategoryOptions}
                            placeholder="Select a category"
                        />
                        <Select
                            id="layoutType"
                            name="layoutType"
                            label="Article Layout"
                            value={formData.layoutType}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            options={ARTICLE_LAYOUT_OPTIONS}
                            required
                        />
                        {canPublish && (
                            <Select
                                id="status"
                                name="status"
                                label="Status"
                                value={formData.status}
                                onChange={handleChange}
                                options={statusOptions}
                                required
                            />
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {canPublish && formData.status === 'scheduled' && (
                        <div className="p-4 bg-blue-50 border-l-4 border-blue-400 rounded-r-lg">
                            <Input
                                label="Publish Date and Time"
                                name="publishedAt"
                                type="datetime-local"
                                value={formData.publishedAt}
                                onChange={handleChange}
                                required={formData.status === 'scheduled'}
                                leftIcon={<Clock className="size-4" />}
                                helperText="The article will be automatically published at this time."
                                error={errors.publishedAt}
                            />
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Tags Section */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Tags</label>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2 py-3 border border-gray-300 bg-white shadow rounded-md">
                            {formData.tags.map((tag) => (
                                <span key={tag} className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-blue-100 text-blue-800">
                                    {tag} <X onClick={() => removeTag(tag)} className="ml-1.5 size-3 cursor-pointer" />
                                </span>
                            ))}
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={currentTag}
                                onChange={(e) => setCurrentTag(e.target.value)}
                                onKeyDown={handleTagKeyDown}
                                className="flex-1 outline-none border-none bg-transparent text-sm"
                                placeholder="Add a tag and press Enter..."
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="pt-6">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setIsSeoOpen(!isSeoOpen)}
                            className="w-full flex justify-between items-center font-semibold text-lg text-gray-700"
                        >
                            <span>Advanced: SEO Settings</span>
                            <ChevronDown className={`transform transition-transform duration-200 ${isSeoOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
                        </button>
                        {isSeoOpen && (
                            <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
                                <Input
                                    name="metaTitle"
                                    label="Meta Title"
                                    value={formData.metaTitle}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    placeholder="Custom title for search engine results"
                                    helperText="If empty, the article title will be used."
                                />
                                <Textarea
                                    name="metaDescription"
                                    label="Meta Description"
                                    value={formData.metaDescription}
                                    onChange={handleChange}
                                    placeholder="Custom description for search engines"
                                    rows={3}
                                    helperText="A good meta description is around 155-160 characters."
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Article Flags Section */}
                    {canPublish && (
                        <div>
                            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Article Flags</label>
                            <div className="p-4 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg space-y-4">
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        name="isFeatured"
                                        checked={formData.isFeatured}
                                        onChange={handleChange}
                                        className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary"
                                    />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Mark as Featured</span>
                                    <Star className="ml-2 size-4 text-yellow-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        name="isTopHeadline"
                                        checked={formData.isTopHeadline}
                                        onChange={handleChange}
                                        className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary"
                                    />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Set as Top Headline</span>
                                    <Zap className="ml-2 size-4 text-orange-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        name="isEditorChoice"
                                        checked={formData.isEditorChoice}
                                        onChange={handleChange}
                                        className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary"
                                    />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Editor's Choice</span>
                                    <Award className="ml-2 size-4 text-blue-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="checkbox" name="isPinned" checked={formData.isPinned} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Pin Article</span>
                                    <Pin className="ml-2 size-4 text-indigo-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="checkbox" name="isHighlighted" checked={formData.isHighlighted} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Highlight Article</span>
                                    <LucideHighlighter className="ml-2 size-4 text-pink-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="checkbox" name="isOpinion" checked={formData.isOpinion} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Set as Opinion</span>
                                    <PenBox className="ml-2 size-4 text-green-500" />
                                </label>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Form Actions */}
                    <Card.Footer>
                        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-end gap-3">
                            {canPublish ? (
                                <>
                                    <Button
                                        variant="outline"
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={handleSaveAsDraft}
                                        disabled={isSubmitting}
                                        icon={<Save className="size-4" />}
                                    >
                                        Save as Draft
                                    </Button>
                                    <Button
                                        type="submit"
                                        disabled={isSubmitting}
                                        isLoading={isSubmitting}
                                        icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}
                                    >
                                        {isSubmitting ? "Updating..." : "Update Article"}
                                    </Button>
                                </>
                            ) : (
                                <Button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={handleSaveAsPreview}
                                    disabled={isSubmitting}
                                    isLoading={isSubmitting}
                                    icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}
                                >
                                    {isSubmitting ? "Submitting..." : "Submit for Review"}
                                </Button>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </Card.Footer>
                </form>
            </>
        </div>
    )
}

export default EditArticle

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\EditGallery\EditGallery.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import {
    useGetGalleryByIdQuery,
    useUpdateGalleryMutation,
    useAddImageToGalleryMutation,
    useDeleteGalleryImageMutation,
} from '../../_core/Slices/gallerySlice';
import {
    Heading,
    Button,
    Card,
    Input,
    Select,
    Textarea
} from '../../components/components';

import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import { ArrowLeft, Trash2, X, Image as ImageIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import {
    Box, Typography, Tooltip, IconButton,
} from '@mui/material';

const EditGallery = () => {
    const { slug } = useParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { userInfo } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);

    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        title: '',
        description: '',
        status: 'draft',
        tags: [], // **CHANGED**: Now an array instead of a string
    });
    const [currentTag, setCurrentTag] = useState(''); // **NEW**: State for the tag input
    const [existingImages, setExistingImages] = useState([]);
    const newImagesInputRef = useRef(null);

    // Modal states
    const [isDeleteImageModalOpen, setIsDeleteImageModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [imageToDelete, setImageToDelete] = useState(null);

    // RTK Query hooks
    const {
        data: galleryData,
        isLoading: isLoadingGallery,
        isError: isGetGalleryError,
        error: getGalleryError,
        refetch: refetchGallery,
    } = useGetGalleryByIdQuery(slug);

    const [updateGalleryMetadata, { isLoading: isUpdatingMetadata }] = useUpdateGalleryMutation();
    const [addImageToGallery, { isLoading: isAddingImages }] = useAddImageToGalleryMutation();
    const [deleteGalleryImage, { isLoading: isDeletingImage }] = useDeleteGalleryImageMutation();

    useEffect(() => {
        if (galleryData) {
            const { title, description, status, tags, images } = galleryData;
            setFormData({
                title: title || '',
                description: description || '',
                status: status || 'draft',
                tags: Array.isArray(tags) ? tags : [], // **CHANGED**: Set as an array directly
            });
            setExistingImages(images || []);
        }
    }, [galleryData]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isGetGalleryError) {
            toast.error(getGalleryError?.data?.message || 'Failed to load gallery data.');
            navigate('/dashboard/galleries');
        }
    }, [isGetGalleryError, getGalleryError, navigate]);

    const handleInputChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    };

    // **NEW**: Handler for adding a tag
    const handleTagKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key !== 'Enter' || !currentTag.trim()) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        const newTag = currentTag.trim();
        if (newTag && !formData.tags.includes(newTag)) {
            setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, tags: [...prev.tags, newTag] }));
        }
        setCurrentTag(''); // Reset the input field
    };

    // **NEW**: Handler for removing a tag
    const removeTag = (tagToRemove) => {
        setFormData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            tags: prev.tags.filter(tag => tag !== tagToRemove),
        }));
    };


    // --- API Logic is kept exactly as you provided ---

    const handleMetadataSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!formData.title.trim()) {
            toast.error("Title is required.");
            return;
        }
        try {
            // **CHANGED**: The payload is now sent directly, as formData.tags is already the correct array format.
            const payload = { ...formData };
            await updateGalleryMetadata({ id: galleryData._id, data: payload }).unwrap();
            toast.success('Gallery metadata updated successfully!');
            refetchGallery(); // Refetch to ensure data consistency
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || 'Failed to update gallery metadata.');
        }
    };

    const handleAddNewImages = async (e) => {
        const files = e.target.files;
        if (!files || files.length === 0) return;

        const newImageData = new FormData();
        for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
            newImageData.append('galleryImages', files[i]);
        }

        try {
            await addImageToGallery({ id: galleryData._id, data: newImageData }).unwrap();
            toast.success(`${files.length} image(s) added successfully!`);
            if (newImagesInputRef.current) newImagesInputRef.current.value = "";
            refetchGallery();
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || `Failed to add image(s).`);
        }
    };

    const openDeleteImageModal = (image) => {
        setImageToDelete(image)
        setIsDeleteImageModalOpen(true);
    };

    const confirmDeleteImage = async () => {
        if (!imageToDelete || !galleryData._id) return;
        try {
            await deleteGalleryImage({ galleryId: galleryData._id, imageId: imageToDelete._id }).unwrap();
            toast.success(`Image deleted successfully!`);
            setIsDeleteImageModalOpen(false);
            setImageToDelete(null);
            refetchGallery();
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || `Failed to delete image.`);
            setIsDeleteImageModalOpen(false);
        }
    };

    if (isLoadingGallery) {
        return <div className="h-screen w-full flex justify-center items-center">
            <CircularProgressBox />
        </div>;;
    }

    const globalLoading = isUpdatingMetadata || isAddingImages || isDeletingImage;
    const statusOptions = [{ value: 'draft', label: 'Draft' }, { value: 'published', label: 'Published' }];

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title={`Edit Gallery: ${galleryData?.title || '...'}`}
                actions={<Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/galleries')} icon={<ArrowLeft className="size-4" />}>Back to Galleries</Button>}
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                <div className="lg:col-span-1 space-y-6">
                    <Card>
                        <form onSubmit={handleMetadataSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">
                            <Heading level={3} title="Gallery Details" />
                            <Input name="title" label="Gallery Title" value={formData.title} onChange={handleInputChange} required disabled={globalLoading} />
                            <Textarea name="description" label="Description" value={formData.description} onChange={handleInputChange} rows={4} disabled={globalLoading} />

                            {/* **CHANGED**: Tag Input UI */}
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Tags</label>
                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg min-h-[44px]">
                                    {formData.tags.map((tag, index) => (
                                        <span key={index} className="flex items-center bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 text-sm font-medium pl-2.5 pr-1 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                                            {tag}
                                            <button type="button" onClick={() => removeTag(tag)} className="ml-1.5 p-0.5 rounded-full hover:bg-blue-200" disabled={globalLoading}>
                                                <X className="size-3" />
                                            </button>
                                        </span>
                                    ))}
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={currentTag}
                                        onChange={(e) => setCurrentTag(e.target.value)}
                                        onKeyDown={handleTagKeyDown}
                                        className="flex-1 min-w-[100px] p-1 outline-none border-none bg-transparent text-sm"
                                        placeholder="Add tag..."
                                        disabled={globalLoading}
                                    />
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <Select name="status" label="Status" value={formData.status} onChange={handleInputChange} options={statusOptions} disabled={globalLoading} />
                            <div className="flex justify-end pt-4 border-t">
                                <Button type="submit" disabled={globalLoading} isLoading={isUpdatingMetadata}>
                                    {isUpdatingMetadata ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Details'}
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </Card>
                </div>
                <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
                    <Card>
                        <div className="p-6 space-y-4">
                            <Heading level={3} title="Manage Images" description={`Currently ${existingImages.length} image(s)`} />
                            <div>
                                <label htmlFor="add-new-images" className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Add New Images</label>
                                <Input id="add-new-images" type="file" multiple ref={newImagesInputRef} onChange={handleAddNewImages} accept="image/*" disabled={globalLoading} />
                                {isAddingImages && <p className="text-sm text-blue-500 mt-2">Uploading...</p>}
                            </div>
                            {existingImages.length > 0 ? (
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 pt-4 border-t">
                                    {existingImages.map((image) => (
                                        <Box key={image._id} className="relative group aspect-square">
                                            <img src={image.url} alt={image.caption || 'Gallery image'} className="w-full h-full object-cover rounded-md shadow" />
                                            <div className="absolute inset-0  group-hover:bg-opacity-60 transition-all flex items-center justify-center">
                                                <Tooltip title="Delete Image">
                                                    <IconButton size="small" onClick={() => openDeleteImageModal(image)} disabled={globalLoading} sx={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,0,0,0.7)', '&:hover': { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,0,0,0.9)' }, color: 'white', p: 1 }}>
                                                        <Trash2 size={16} />
                                                    </IconButton>
                                                </Tooltip>
                                            </div>
                                        </Box>
                                    ))}
                                </div>
                            ) : (
                                <div className="text-center py-8 border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg">
                                    <ImageIcon className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-gray-400" />
                                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">This gallery has no images yet.</p>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </div>
            </div>

            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteImageModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteImageModalOpen(false)}
                title="Confirm Delete Image"
                onConfirm={confirmDeleteImage}
                confirmText="Delete"
                cancelText="Cancel"
                isLoading={isDeletingImage}
            >
                <p>Are you sure you want to permanently delete this image? This action cannot be undone.</p>
                {imageToDelete?.url && <img src={imageToDelete.url} alt="Preview" className="mt-4 max-h-40 mx-auto rounded-md" />}
            </ConfirmModal>
        </div>
    );
};

export default EditGallery;

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\EditGallery\EditGallery.jsx ---
