        └── EditUser/
                ├── EditUser.jsx

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\EditUser\EditUser.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';

// RTK Query Hooks for User API
import { useGetUserByIdQuery, useUpdateUserMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/userSlice';

// Helper Function for API calls
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission';

// UI Components
// Common UI components from barrel file
import {
    Heading,
    Button,
    Card,
    Select,
    Textarea,
    Input
} from '../../components/components';

// Specialized components with explicit paths
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import { ArrowLeft, Save, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import { Avatar } from '@mui/material';

const EditUser = () => {
    const { userId } = useParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { userInfo: loggedInUser } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);

    // --- State Initialization ---
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        fullName: '',
        username: '',
        bio: '',
        isVerified: true, // Assuming a field to (de)activate users
        role: '',
    });
    const [originalData, setOriginalData] = useState(null);
    const [hasUnsavedChanges, setHasUnsavedChanges] = useState(false);

    // --- RTK Query Hooks ---
    const {
        data: userData, // The direct user object from the API
        isLoading: isLoadingUser,
        isError: isGetUserError,
        error: getUserError,
        refetch: refetchUser,
    } = useGetUserByIdQuery(userId, { refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true });

    const [updateUser, { isLoading: isUpdatingUser }] = useUpdateUserMutation();

    // --- Effects ---
    useEffect(() => {
        if (userData) {
            const populatedData = {
                fullName: userData.fullName || '',
                username: userData.username || '',
                bio: userData.bio || '',
                isVerified: userData.isVerified !== undefined ? userData.isVerified : true,
                role: userData.role || '',
            };
            setFormData(populatedData);
            setOriginalData(populatedData);
            setHasUnsavedChanges(false);
        }
    }, [userData]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (isGetUserError) {
            toast.error(getUserError?.data?.message || 'Failed to load user data.');
            navigate('/dashboard/users');
        }
    }, [isGetUserError, getUserError, navigate]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (originalData && !isLoadingUser) {
            setHasUnsavedChanges(JSON.stringify(formData) !== JSON.stringify(originalData));
        }
    }, [formData, originalData, isLoadingUser]);

    // --- Event Handlers ---
    const handleChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value, type, checked } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: type === 'checkbox' ? checked : value }));
    };

    // --- API Handlers ---
    const handleSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        // Construct the payload with only the fields that can be updated
        const dataToUpdate = {
            fullName: formData.fullName,
            username: formData.username,
            bio: formData.bio,
            isVerified: formData.isVerified,
            role: formData.role, // The backend will validate if the logged-in user can change this
        };

        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: updateUser,
            data: { id: userId, data: dataToUpdate },
            toast: toast,
            toastText: "User has been updated",
            onSuccess: () => {
                refetchUser(); // Refreshes data and resets hasUnsavedChanges via useEffect
            },
        });
    };

    // --- Permission Checks ---
    const canEditRole = hasPermission('admin');
    const canDeactivateUser = hasPermission('admin');

    // --- Render Logic ---
    if (isLoadingUser) {
        return <div className="h-screen w-full flex justify-center items-center">
            <CircularProgressBox />
        </div>;
    }
    if (!userData) {
        return (
            <div className="p-6 text-center">
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-red-600">User Not Found</h1>
                <p>The requested user could not be loaded.</p>
                <Button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/users')} className="mt-4">Back to List</Button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    // Admins can change roles up to 'editor'. Superadmin can change up to 'admin'.
    const roleOptions = [
        { value: 'reader', label: 'Reader' },
        { value: 'author', label: 'Author' },
        { value: 'editor', label: 'Editor' },
    ];
    if (hasPermission("superadmin")) {
        roleOptions.push({ value: 'admin', label: 'Admin' });
    }
    // Prevent a user from setting a role higher than their own or editing a superadmin
    const isEditingHigherRole = !hasPermission("admin");

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Edit User Profile"
                description={`Modifying details for @${userData.username}`}
                actions={
                    <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/users')} icon={<ArrowLeft className="size-4" />}>Back to User List</Button>
                        <Button onClick={handleSubmit} icon={<Save className="size-4" />} disabled={isUpdatingUser || !hasUnsavedChanges || isEditingHigherRole} isLoading={isUpdatingUser}>
                            Save Changes
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                }
            />

            <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                <div className="lg:col-span-1">
                    <Card className="p-6 text-center">
                        <Avatar
                            src={userData.avatar?.url}
                            alt={userData.fullName}
                            sx={{ width: 120, height: 120, margin: '0 auto', fontSize: '3rem' }}
                        />
                        <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-bold">{userData.fullName}</h2>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">@{userData.username}</p>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">{userData.email}</p>

                        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
                            <span className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full ${userData.role === 'superadmin' ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800' : userData.role === 'admin' ? 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800' : 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800'}`}>
                                {userData.role}
                            </span>
                            {userData.isVerified && (
                                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-green-100 text-green-800 text-xs font-medium px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                                    <ShieldCheck size={14} /> Verified
                                </span>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </div>
                <div className="lg:col-span-2">
                    <Card>
                        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">
                            <Heading level={3} title="User Information" />
                            <Input name="fullName" label="Full Name" value={formData.fullName} onChange={handleChange} required disabled={isUpdatingUser || isEditingHigherRole} />
                            <Input name="username" label="Username" value={formData.username} onChange={handleChange} required disabled={isUpdatingUser || isEditingHigherRole} />
                            <Textarea name="bio" label="Biography" value={formData.bio} onChange={handleChange} rows={4} placeholder="A short bio about the user..." disabled={isUpdatingUser || isEditingHigherRole} />

                            <Heading level={3} title="Permissions & Status" className="pt-4 border-t" />
                            <Select
                                label="Role"
                                name="role"
                                value={formData.role}
                                onChange={handleChange}
                                options={roleOptions}
                                disabled={!canEditRole || isEditingHigherRole}
                                helperText={!canEditRole ? "Only a Superadmin can change roles." : isEditingHigherRole ? "Cannot edit a Superadmin's role." : ""}
                            />

                            {canDeactivateUser && (
                                <div className="flex items-center">
                                    <input
                                        id="isVerified"
                                        name="isVerified"
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        checked={formData.isVerified}
                                        onChange={handleChange}
                                        className="h-4 w-4 text-primary border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-primary"
                                        disabled={isUpdatingUser || isEditingHigherRole || userData._id === loggedInUser?.user?._id}
                                    />
                                    <label htmlFor="isVerified" className="ml-2 block text-sm font-medium">
                                        Account Active
                                    </label>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </form>
                    </Card>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default EditUser;

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\EditUser\EditUser.jsx ---

        └── EditVideo/
                ├── EditVideo.jsx

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\EditVideo\EditVideo.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';

// RTK Query & Helpers
import { useGetVideoByIdQuery, useUpdateVideoMutation, useDeleteVideoMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/videoSlice';
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';

// UI Components
// Basic UI components (from barrel file)
import {
    Heading,
    Button,
    Card,
    Input,
    Select,
    Textarea
} from '../../components/components';

// Complex components (explicit paths)
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import { ArrowLeft, Trash2, X, Save, Upload, Link as LinkIcon, Youtube } from 'lucide-react';

// Helper to parse YouTube IDs
const getYouTubeVideoId = (url) => {
    if (!url) return null;
    const regExp = /^.*(youtu.be\/|v\/|u\/\w\/|embed\/|watch\?v=|&v=)([^#\&\?]*).*/;
    const match = url.match(regExp);
    return (match && match[2].length === 11) ? match[2] : null;
};

const EditVideo = () => {
    const { videoSlug } = useParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    // --- State Management ---
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ title: '', description: '', status: 'private', tags: [] });
    const [sourceType, setSourceType] = useState('upload'); // 'upload' or 'url'
    const [videoUrl, setVideoUrl] = useState(''); // For URL input
    const [newVideoFile, setNewVideoFile] = useState(null); // For new file upload

    const [originalData, setOriginalData] = useState(null);
    const [currentTag, setCurrentTag] = useState('');
    const [hasUnsavedChanges, setHasUnsavedChanges] = useState(false);
    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const videoFileInputRef = useRef(null);

    // --- RTK Query Hooks ---
    const { data: videoData, isLoading: isLoadingVideo, refetch } = useGetVideoByIdQuery(videoSlug, { refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true });
    const [updateVideo, { isLoading: isUpdating }] = useUpdateVideoMutation();
    const [deleteVideo, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteVideoMutation();

    // --- Effects ---
    useEffect(() => {
        if (videoData) {
            const populatedData = {
                title: videoData.title || '',
                description: videoData.description || '',
                status: videoData.status || 'private',
                tags: Array.isArray(videoData.tags) ? videoData.tags : [],
            };
            setFormData(populatedData);
            setSourceType(videoData.sourceType || 'upload');
            setVideoUrl(videoData.videoUrl || '');
            setOriginalData({ ...populatedData, sourceType: videoData.sourceType, videoUrl: videoData.videoUrl });
            setHasUnsavedChanges(false);
        }
    }, [videoData]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (originalData) {
            const dataChanged = JSON.stringify(formData) !== JSON.stringify(originalData) || sourceType !== originalData.sourceType || videoUrl !== originalData.videoUrl;
            setHasUnsavedChanges(dataChanged || !!newVideoFile);
        }
    }, [formData, sourceType, videoUrl, originalData, newVideoFile]);

    // --- Handlers ---
    const handleInputChange = (e) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value }));
    const handleTagKeyDown = (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && currentTag.trim()) { e.preventDefault(); const newTag = currentTag.trim(); if (!formData.tags.includes(newTag)) { setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, tags: [...prev.tags, newTag] })); } setCurrentTag(''); } };
    const removeTag = (tag) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, tags: prev.tags.filter(t => t !== tag) }));
    const handleVideoFileChange = (e) => { const file = e.target.files?.[0]; if (file) setNewVideoFile(file); };



    const handleFileChange = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (file) {
            setNewVideoFile(file);
            toast.info(`Selected new file: ${file.name}`);
        }
    };

    // --- API Handlers ---
    const handleSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!formData.title.trim()) {
            toast.error("Title is required.");
            return;
        }

        const payload = new FormData();

        // 1. Metadata ko append karein
        payload.append('title', formData.title);
        payload.append('description', formData.description);
        payload.append('status', formData.status);
        payload.append('sourceType', sourceType); // sourceType state se lein

        // 2. Tags ko sahi tareeqe se append karein
        if (formData.tags && formData.tags.length > 0) {
            formData.tags.forEach(tag => {
                payload.append('tags[]', tag); // '[]' backend ko batata hai ke yeh ek array hai
            });
        }

        // 3. Source ke hisab se file ya URL append karein
        if (sourceType === 'upload') {
            if (newVideoFile) {
                // Agar nayi file hai to usay attach karein
                payload.append('videoFile', newVideoFile);
            }
            // Agar nayi file nahi hai, to kuch na karein, backend purani file ko barkarar rakhega
        } else if (sourceType === 'url') {
            payload.append('videoUrl', videoUrl);
        }

        // 4. API call karein
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: updateVideo,
            data: { id: videoData._id, data: payload },
            toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setNewVideoFile(null);
                if (videoFileInputRef.current) videoFileInputRef.current.value = "";
                refetch();
                toast.success("Video updated successfully!");
            }
        });
    };

    const confirmDelete = () => makeRequest({ apiFunc: deleteVideo, data: videoData._id, toast, navigate, redirectTo: '/dashboard/videos', onSuccess: () => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false), onError: () => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false) });

    // --- Smart Video Player for Preview ---
    const VideoPlayer = () => {
        // Preview the new file if one has been selected
        if (newVideoFile) {
            return <video src={URL.createObjectURL(newVideoFile)} controls className="w-full aspect-video rounded-lg bg-black" />;
        }
        // If editing a URL video, show the embed
        if (videoData?.sourceType === 'url') {
            const videoId = getYouTubeVideoId(videoData.videoUrl);
            return videoId ? <iframe src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${videoId}`} className="w-full aspect-video rounded-lg" allowFullScreen></iframe> : <p>Invalid YouTube URL</p>;
        }
        // Otherwise, show the existing uploaded video
        return <video src={videoData?.videoFile?.url} poster={videoData?.thumbnail?.url} controls className="w-full aspect-video rounded-lg bg-black" />;
    };

    if (isLoadingVideo) return <CircularProgressBox text="Loading video..." />;

    const globalLoading = isUpdating || isDeleting;
    const statusOptions = [{ value: 'private', label: 'Private' }, { value: 'published', label: 'Published' }];

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading level={1} title="Edit Video" description={videoData?.title} actions={
                <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
                    <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/videos')}><ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back</Button>
                    <Button onClick={handleSubmit} icon={<Save size={16} />} disabled={globalLoading || !hasUnsavedChanges} isLoading={isUpdating}>Save Changes</Button>
                </div>
            } />

            <div className=" gap-6">
                <div className=" space-y-6 mb-4">
                    <Card><VideoPlayer /></Card>
                </div>
                <div className=" space-y-6">
                    <Card>
                        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">
                            <Heading level={3} title="Video Details" />
                            <Input name="title" label="Title" value={formData.title} onChange={handleInputChange} required disabled={globalLoading} />
                            <Textarea name="description" label="Description" value={formData.description} onChange={handleInputChange} rows={4} disabled={globalLoading} />
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">Tags</label>
                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2  min-h-[44px]">
                                    {formData.tags.map((tag) => (<span key={tag} className="flex items-center text-xs bg-blue-100 px-2 py-1 rounded-full">{tag}<button type="button" onClick={() => removeTag(tag)} className="ml-1.5"><X size={14} /></button></span>))}
                                    <Input type="text" value={currentTag} onChange={(e) => setCurrentTag(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={handleTagKeyDown} className="flex-1 p-1" placeholder="Add tag..." disabled={globalLoading} />
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <Select name="status" label="Status" value={formData.status} onChange={handleInputChange} options={statusOptions} disabled={globalLoading} />

                            <div className="pt-4 border-t">
                                <Heading level={4} title="Video Source" className="mb-2" />
                                <div className="flex rounded-md shadow-sm">
                                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setSourceType('upload')} className={`flex-1 p-2 rounded-l-md ${sourceType === 'upload' ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-gray-200'}`}>Upload File</button>
                                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setSourceType('url')} className={`flex-1 p-2 rounded-r-md ${sourceType === 'url' ? 'bg-primary text-white' : 'bg-gray-200'}`}>From URL</button>
                                </div>
                                {sourceType === 'upload' ? (
                                    <div className="mt-4"><Input type="file" name="videoFile" accept="video/*" ref={videoFileInputRef} onChange={handleVideoFileChange} helperText="Uploading a new file will replace the old one." disabled={globalLoading} /></div>
                                ) : (
                                    <div className="mt-4"><Input name="videoUrl" label="Video URL" value={videoUrl} onChange={(e) => setVideoUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g., https://youtube.com/watch?v=..." leftIcon={<LinkIcon size={16} />} disabled={globalLoading} /></div>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </Card>
                    <Card>
                        <div className="p-6">
                            <Heading level={3} title="Danger Zone" />
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 my-2">Permanently delete this video.</p>
                            <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(true)} disabled={globalLoading}>Delete Video</Button>
                        </div>
                    </Card>

                </div>
            </div>

            <ConfirmModal isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen} onClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)} title="Confirm Delete" onConfirm={confirmDelete} isLoading={isDeleting} confirmButtonVariant="danger">
                <p>Are you sure you want to permanently delete "<strong>{videoData?.title}</strong>"?</p>
            </ConfirmModal>
        </div>
    );
};

export default EditVideo;

                ├── ForgotPassVerifyOtp.jsx

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ForgotPassVerifyOtp\ForgotPassVerifyOtp.jsx ---

"use client"

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { ArrowRight, ArrowLeft, RefreshCw, Shield, Mail } from 'lucide-react';
import { useSendOtpMutation, useVerifyOtpMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/authApiSlice.js";
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import { Button } from '../../components/components';

const ForgotPassVerifyOtp = () => {
    const [otp, setOtp] = useState(["", "", "", "", "", ""]);
    const [error, setError] = useState("");
    const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(60);
    const [canResend, setCanResend] = useState(false);

    const inputRefs = useRef([]);
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { email } = useParams();

    // RTK Query Hooks
    const [sendOtp, { isLoading: isResendingOtp }] = useSendOtpMutation();
    const [verifyOtp, { isLoading: isVerifyingOtp }] = useVerifyOtpMutation();
    const isLoading = isResendingOtp || isVerifyingOtp;

    // Timer countdown effect
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!canResend) {
            const timer = setTimeout(() => {
                if (timeLeft > 0) {
                    setTimeLeft(t => t - 1);
                } else {
                    setCanResend(true);
                }
            }, 1000);
            return () => clearTimeout(timer);
        }
    }, [timeLeft, canResend]);

    // Auto-focus the first input on component mount
    useEffect(() => {
        inputRefs.current[0]?.focus();
    }, []);

    const formatTime = (seconds) => {
        const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
        const secs = seconds % 60;
        return `${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    };

    const handleOtpChange = (index, value) => {
        // Allow only digits
        if (!/^\d*$/.test(value)) return;

        const newOtp = [...otp];
        newOtp[index] = value;
        setOtp(newOtp);
        setError(""); // Clear previous errors on new input

        // Move to next input if a digit is entered
        if (value && index < 5) {
            inputRefs.current[index + 1]?.focus();
        }

        // If all digits are filled, auto-submit for verification
        const completeOtp = newOtp.join("");
        if (completeOtp.length === 6) {
            handleVerify(completeOtp);
        }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (index, e) => {
        if (e.key === "Backspace" && !otp[index] && index > 0) {
            inputRefs.current[index - 1]?.focus();
        }
    };

    const handlePaste = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const pastedData = e.clipboardData.getData("text").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 6);
        if (pastedData.length === 6) {
            setOtp(pastedData.split(""));
            handleVerify(pastedData);
        }
    };

    const handleVerify = async (otpCode = otp.join("")) => {
        if (otpCode.length !== 6) {
            setError("Please enter all 6 digits.");
            return;
        }
        navigate(`/forgot-password`, { state: { email, otp: otpCode } });


        // makeRequest({
        //     apiFunc: verifyOtp,
        //     data: { email, otp: otpCode },
        //     toast: toast,
        //     onSuccess: (res) => {
        //         toast.success("OTP Verified! Please set your new password.");
        //         // Navigate to the reset password page, passing the verified data securely
        //         navigate(`/forgot-password`, { state: { email, otp: otpCode } });
        //     },
        //     onError: (err) => {
        //         setError(err.data?.message || "Invalid or expired OTP. Please try again.");
        //         setOtp(["", "", "", "", "", ""]); // Clear OTP fields on error
        //         inputRefs.current[0]?.focus();
        //     }
        // });
    };

    const handleResendOtp = async () => {
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: sendOtp,
            data: { email },
            toast: toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setTimeLeft(0);
                setCanResend(false);
                setOtp(["", "", "", "", "", ""]);
                inputRefs.current[0]?.focus();
            }
        });
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-50 to-gray-100 py-12 px-4">
            <div className="max-w-md w-full space-y-8">
                <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-8">
                    <div className="text-center mb-8">
                        <div className="mx-auto h-16 w-16 rounded-xl bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                            <Shield className="h-8 w-8 text-primary" />
                        </div>
                        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">Enter Verification Code</h2>
                        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">We've sent a 6-digit code to your email:</p>
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center mt-2">
                            <Mail className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-400 mr-2" />
                            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">{email}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mb-6">
                        <div className="flex justify-center space-x-2 sm:space-x-3" onPaste={handlePaste}>
                            {otp.map((digit, index) => (
                                <input
                                    key={index}
                                    ref={(el) => (inputRefs.current[index] = el)}
                                    type="text"
                                    inputMode="numeric"
                                    maxLength="1"
                                    value={digit}
                                    onChange={(e) => handleOtpChange(index, e.target.value)}
                                    onKeyDown={(e) => handleKeyDown(index, e)}
                                    className={`w-12 h-14 sm:w-14 sm:h-16 text-center text-xl font-semibold border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary transition-all duration-200 ${error ? "border-red-500 ring-red-500" : "border-gray-300"} ${digit ? "border-primary bg-primary/5" : ""}`}
                                    disabled={isLoading}
                                />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                        {error && <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-red-600 text-center">{error}</p>}
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-center mb-6">
                        {/* {!canResend ? (
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Resend code in <span className="font-medium text-primary">{formatTime(timeLeft)}</span></p>
                        ) : ( */}
                        <Button variant="link" onClick={handleResendOtp} disabled={isResendingOtp} icon={isResendingOtp ? <RefreshCw className="size-4 animate-spin" /> : <RefreshCw className="size-4" />}>
                            {isResendingOtp ? "Sending..." : "Resend Code"}
                        </Button>
                        {/* )} */}
                    </div>

                    <div className="space-y-4">
                        <Button onClick={() => handleVerify()} disabled={isVerifyingOtp || otp.join('').length !== 6} isLoading={isVerifyingOtp} fullWidth icon={<ArrowRight className="size-4" />} iconPosition="right">
                            Verify Code
                        </Button>
                        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => navigate("/login")} fullWidth icon={<ArrowLeft className="size-4" />} disabled={isLoading}>
                            Back to Login
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ForgotPassVerifyOtp;

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\ForgotPassVerifyOtp\ForgotPassVerifyOtp.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ForgotPassword\ForgotPassword.jsx ---

"use client"

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Eye, EyeOff, KeyRound, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';

// Import the correct mutation hook
import { useForgotPasswordMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/authApiSlice.js';
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import { Heading, Input, Button, Card } from '../../components/components';

const ForgotPassword = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const location = useLocation();

    // --- Form State ---
    const [passwords, setPasswords] = useState({ newPassword: '', confirmPassword: '' });
    const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});

    // --- Get email and OTP from navigation state ---
    const { email, otp } = location.state || {};

    // --- RTK Query Hook ---
    const [forgotPassword, { isLoading }] = useForgotPasswordMutation();

    // Effect to redirect if email or OTP are missing
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!email || !otp) {
            toast.error("Verification data missing. Please start over.");
            navigate('/send-otp'); // Redirect to the initial forgot password page
        }
    }, [email, otp, navigate]);

    const handleChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setPasswords(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
        if (errors[name]) setErrors(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: '' }));
    };

    const validate = () => {
        const newErrors = {};
        if (!passwords.newPassword) {
            newErrors.newPassword = 'New password is required.';
        } else if (passwords.newPassword.length < 6) {
            newErrors.newPassword = 'Password must be at least 6 characters.';
        }
        if (passwords.newPassword !== passwords.confirmPassword) {
            newErrors.confirmPassword = 'Passwords do not match.';
        }
        setErrors(newErrors);
        return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0;
    };

    const handleSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!validate()) return;

        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: forgotPassword,
            data: {
                email: email,
                otp: otp,
                newPassword: passwords.newPassword,
            },
            toast: toast,
            navigate: navigate,
            redirectTo: '/', // Redirect to login page on success

        });
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-50 p-4">
            <Card className="max-w-md w-full">
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-8 space-y-6">
                    <div className="text-center">
                        <div className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 rounded-full bg-primary/10 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                            <KeyRound className="h-6 w-6 text-primary" />
                        </div>
                        <Heading level={2} title="Set New Password" />
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">Create a new, strong password for your account.</p>
                    </div>

                    <Input
                        type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                        name="newPassword"
                        label="New Password"
                        value={passwords.newPassword}
                        onChange={handleChange}
                        error={errors.newPassword}
                        required
                        showPasswordToggle
                    />


                    <Input
                        type="password"
                        name="confirmPassword"
                        label="Confirm New Password"
                        value={passwords.confirmPassword}
                        onChange={handleChange}
                        error={errors.confirmPassword}
                        required
                        showPasswordToggle
                    />
                    <Link to={-1}>
                        <Button type="button" fullWidth isLoading={isLoading} disabled={isLoading}>

                            {'Verify Otp'}
                        </Button>
                    </Link>

                    <Button type="submit" className={"mt-3"} fullWidth isLoading={isLoading} disabled={isLoading}>
                        {isLoading ? 'Resetting...' : 'Reset Password'}
                    </Button>
                    <Button type="button" variant="ghost" fullWidth onClick={() => navigate('/')} icon={<ArrowLeft size={16} />}>
                        Back to Login
                    </Button>
                </form>
            </Card>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ForgotPassword;

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';


import { useGetGalleriesQuery, useDeleteGalleryMutation, useToggleGalleryHeadlineMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/gallerySlice';
import { useGalleryFilters } from '../../hooks/useGalleryFilters.js'; // Import the custom hook
// Keep these from the barrel file
import {
    Heading,
    Button,
    MuiTable,
    Card
} from '../../components/components';

// Explicit imports for other components
import ModalWrapper from '../../components/ModalWrapper/ModalWrapper';
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
import { IconButton, Tooltip, Chip, Box, Typography } from '@mui/material';
import { Edit, Trash2, Plus, Image as ImageIcon, Zap } from 'lucide-react';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission.js'; // Import centralized hasPermission
import { formatDate } from '../../utils/formatters.js'; // Import formatDate

// Removed local hasPermission function, using imported version

const GalleryList = () => {
    // Use the custom hook for pagination and sorting
    const {
        page, // 1-indexed page from hook
        limit, // from hook
        // sortConfig, // from queryParams or hook state if needed directly
        handlers,
        queryParams, // fully constructed query params for RTK Query
    } = useGalleryFilters(10);
    const {
        data: galleriesData,
        isLoading: isLoadingGalleries,
        isError,
        error,
        refetch
    } = useGetGalleriesQuery(queryParams
        , // First argument: your query parameters
        {
            // Second argument: the options object
            refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true,
        }
    );
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { userInfo } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);

    const [toggleHeadline, { isLoading: isToggling }] = useToggleGalleryHeadlineMutation();

    const handleToggleHeadline = (galleryId) => {
        toggleHeadline(galleryId)
            .unwrap()
            .then(() => {
                refetch(); // Refetch galleries after toggling headline
                toast.success("Gallery headline status updated!")
            })
            .catch(err => toast.error(err.data?.message || "Failed to update status."));
    };
    // Default limit 10

    // Delete Modal State
    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [galleryToDelete, setGalleryToDelete] = useState(null); // Will store the whole gallery object for name in modal

    // Use queryParams from the hook

    const [deleteGallery, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteGalleryMutation();

    // Removed local handlers: handlePageChange, handleLimitChange
    // These are now provided by the useGalleryFilters hook's `handlers` object.

    const userCanManage = hasPermission('editor');

    const columns = [
        {
            header: "Title",
            field: "title",
            render: (value, row) => (
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                    {row.images && row.images.length > 0 ? (
                        <img src={row.images[0].thumbnailUrl || row.images[0].url} alt={value} style={{ width: 50, height: 50, objectFit: 'cover', marginRight: 10, borderRadius: '4px' }} />
                    ) : (
                        <Box sx={{ width: 50, height: 50, marginRight: 10, borderRadius: '4px', backgroundColor: '#f0f0f0', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                            <ImageIcon size={24} color="#999" />
                        </Box>
                    )}
                    <Typography variant="body2" fontWeight="medium">{value}</Typography>
                </Box>
            )
        },
        {
            header: "Status",
            field: "status",
            align: "center",
            render: (status) => (
                <Chip
                    label={status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + status.slice(1)}
                    color={status === "published" ? "success" : "default"}
                    size="small"
                />
            )
        },
        {
            header: "Author",
            field: "authorId",
            render: (authorId) => authorId?.fullName || authorId?.username || 'N/A'
        },
        {
            header: "Image Count",
            field: "images",
            align: "center",
            render: (images) => images?.length || 0
        },
        {
            header: "Created At",
            field: "createdAt",
            render: (date) => formatDate(date) // Use formatDate
        },
        {
            header: "Actions",
            field: "actions",
            align: "center",
            render: (value, row) => userCanManage && (
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', gap: 0.5 }}>
                    <Tooltip title={row.isTopHeadline ? "Remove from Headlines" : "Set as Top Headline"}>
                        <IconButton
                            size="small"
                            onClick={() => handleToggleHeadline(row._id)}
                            disabled={isToggling}
                        >
                            <Zap
                                size={16}
                                color={row.isTopHeadline ? '#f97316' : 'currentColor'} // Orange color when active
                            />
                        </IconButton>
                    </Tooltip>
                    <Tooltip title="Edit Gallery">
                        <IconButton
                            size="small"
                            onClick={() => navigate(`/dashboard/galleries/edit/${row.slug}`)}
                        >
                            <Edit size={18} />
                        </IconButton>
                    </Tooltip>
                    <Tooltip title="Delete Gallery">
                        <IconButton
                            size="small"
                            color="error"
                            onClick={() => {
                                setGalleryToDelete(row);
                                setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
                            }}
                        >
                            <Trash2 size={18} />
                        </IconButton>
                    </Tooltip>
                </Box>
            ),
        },

    ];

    if (isLoadingGalleries) {
        return <div className="h-screen w-full flex justify-center items-center">
            <CircularProgressBox />
        </div>;;
    }

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Image Galleries"
                actions={userCanManage && (
                    <Button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/galleries/create')} icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}>
                        Create Gallery
                    </Button>
                )}
            />

            <Card>
                {isError && (
                    <Box sx={{ p: 2, textAlign: 'center', color: 'red' }}>
                        <Typography>{error?.data?.message || error?.message || "An error occurred."}</Typography>
                        <Button onClick={() => refetchGalleries()} className="mt-2">Try Again</Button>
                    </Box>
                )}
                {!isError && (
                    <MuiTable
                        title="All Galleries"
                        columns={columns}
                        rows={galleriesData?.galleries || []}
                        pagination
                        page={page - 1} // MuiTable is 0-indexed, hook's page is 1-indexed
                        rowsPerPage={limit} // limit from hook
                        totalRows={galleriesData?.totalGalleries || 0}
                        onPageChange={handlers.handleMuiPageChange} // Use Mui specific handler from hook
                        onRowsPerPageChange={handlers.handleMuiLimitChange} // Use Mui specific handler from hook
                    // Sorting can be added to MuiTable if it supports server-side sort model
                    // and if handlers.handleSortChange is wired up to table's onSortModelChange or similar.
                    />
                )}
            </Card>

            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => {
                    setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
                    setGalleryToDelete(null);
                }}
                title="Confirm Delete Gallery"
                onConfirm={async () => {
                    if (!galleryToDelete) return;
                    try {
                        await deleteGallery(galleryToDelete._id).unwrap();
                        toast.success(`Gallery "${galleryToDelete.title}" deleted successfully!`);
                        setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
                        setGalleryToDelete(null);
                        // The list will refetch due to tag invalidation
                    } catch (err) {
                        toast.error(err.data?.message || `Failed to delete gallery.`);
                        setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
                    }
                }}
                confirmText="Delete"
                cancelText="Cancel"
                isConfirmLoading={isDeleting}
                variant={"danger"}
            >
                <p>Are you sure you want to delete the gallery "<strong>{galleryToDelete?.title}</strong>"?</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-red-500 mt-2">This action cannot be undone and will delete all associated images.</p>
            </ConfirmModal>
        </div>
    );
};

export default GalleryList;

// File: src/Pages/GlobalSettings/GlobalSettings.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
// Keep these from the barrel file
import {
    Card,
    Heading,
    Input,
    Button
} from '../../components/components';

// Explicit imports for other components
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal'; import { useGetGlobalSettingsQuery, useUpdateGlobalSettingsMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/settingsSlice.js';
import { UploadCloud, Image as ImageIconPlaceholder, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';
import { Switch, FormControlLabel, Box, Typography } from '@mui/material'; // For Maintenance Mode Toggle

const GlobalSettings = () => {
    const [siteTitle, setSiteTitle] = useState('');
    const [removeModal, setRemoveModal] = useState(false)
    const [siteLogoFile, setSiteLogoFile] = useState(null);
    const [siteLogoPreview, setSiteLogoPreview] = useState('');
    const [maintenanceMode, setMaintenanceMode] = useState({
        isActive: false, message: ""
    });
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

    const { data: globalSettingsData, isLoading: isLoadingSettings, isError, error, refetch } = useGetGlobalSettingsQuery();
    const [updateGlobalSettings, { isLoading: isUpdatingSettings }] = useUpdateGlobalSettingsMutation();
    useEffect(() => {
        if (globalSettingsData) {
            const settings = globalSettingsData;
            setSiteTitle(settings.siteName || '');
            const maintenence = settings.maintenanceMode || maintenanceMode;
            setMaintenanceMode(maintenence);
            if (settings.siteLogo?.url) {
                setSiteLogoPreview(settings.siteLogo.url);
            }
        }
    }, [globalSettingsData]);
    console.log(siteLogoFile, "siteLogoFile")
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isError && error) {
            toast.error(error.data?.message || error.message || "Failed to load global settings.");
        }
    }, [isError, error]);

    const handleLogoChange = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (file) {
            if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
                toast.error("Please select a valid image file (PNG, JPG, GIF, WEBP, SVG).");
                if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = ""; // Reset file input
                return;
            }
            if (file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) { // 2MB limit example
                toast.error("Image size should be less than 2MB.");
                if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = "";
                return;
            }
            setSiteLogoFile(file);
            setSiteLogoPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
        }
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('siteName', siteTitle);
        formData.append('maintenanceMode', JSON.stringify(maintenanceMode));
        formData.append('siteLogo', siteLogoFile || "");
        if (siteLogoPreview === '' && globalSettingsData?.settings?.siteLogo?.url) {
            // If preview is cleared and there was an existing logo, signal removal
            formData.append('removeSiteLogo', 'true');
        }


        try {
            const res = await updateGlobalSettings(formData).unwrap();
            toast.success(res?.message || 'Global settings updated successfully!');
            if (res?.data?.settings?.siteLogo?.url) { // If backend returns new logo URL
                setSiteLogoPreview(res.data.settings.siteLogo.url);
                setSiteLogoFile(null); // Clear file input after successful upload
                if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = "";
            } else if (siteLogoPreview === '' && siteLogoFile === null) { // If logo was removed
                setSiteLogoPreview(''); // Ensure preview is cleared if backend confirms removal by not sending a new URL
            }
            refetch(); // Refetch settings to ensure form is up-to-date with any backend transformations
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || err.message || 'Failed to update settings.');
        }
    };

    const handleRemoveLogo = () => {
        setSiteLogoFile(null);
        setSiteLogoPreview(''); // This will signal backend to remove logo if submitted
        if (fileInputRef.current) {
            fileInputRef.current.value = ''; // Clear the file input
        }
        setRemoveModal(false)
        // No API call here, removal happens on submit if preview is empty & there was an old logo
        toast.info("Current logo will be removed on save.");
    };


    if (isLoadingSettings) {
        return <div className='flex justify-center items-center w-full h-ful'>
            <CircularProgressBox />
        </div>;
    }
    console.log(maintenanceMode, "maintenanceMode")

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
            <Heading level={1} title="Global Site Settings" className="mb-6 text-center" />
            <ConfirmModal
                title={"Are you really want to remove site logo?"}
                isOpen={removeModal}
                onRequestClose={() => setRemoveModal(false)}
                onConfirm={handleRemoveLogo}

            />
            {isError && (
                <Box sx={{ p: 2, mb: 3, textAlign: 'center', border: '1px dashed red', borderRadius: '4px' }}>
                    <AlertTriangle className="inline-block mr-2 text-red-500" />
                    <Typography color="error" display="inline">
                        {error.data?.message || error.message || "An error occurred loading settings."}
                    </Typography>
                    <Button onClick={() => refetch()} className="mt-2" variant="outline" size="sm">Try Again</Button>
                </Box>
            )}

            <Card>
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">
                    <Input
                        label="Site Title"
                        name="siteTitle"
                        value={siteTitle}
                        onChange={(e) => setSiteTitle(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Enter the title for your website"
                        required
                        disabled={isUpdatingSettings} // Disable during update
                    />

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Site Logo</label>
                        <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 2, mb: 1 }}>
                            {siteLogoPreview ? (
                                <img src={siteLogoPreview} alt="Site Logo Preview" className="h-20 w-auto border p-1 rounded-md object-contain" />
                            ) : (
                                <Box className="h-20 w-20 border p-1 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-50">
                                    <ImageIconPlaceholder size={32} className="text-gray-400" />
                                </Box>
                            )}
                            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} icon={<UploadCloud size={16} />} disabled={isUpdatingSettings}>
                                Upload Logo
                            </Button>
                            {siteLogoPreview && (
                                <Button type="button" variant="destructiveOutline" size="sm" onClick={() => setRemoveModal(true)} disabled={isUpdatingSettings}>
                                    Remove Logo
                                </Button>
                            )}
                        </Box>
                        <input
                            type="file"
                            ref={fileInputRef}
                            onChange={handleLogoChange}
                            accept="image/png, image/jpeg, image/gif, image/webp, image/svg+xml"
                            className="hidden"
                            disabled={isUpdatingSettings}
                        />
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Recommended: PNG, JPG, SVG. Max 2MB.</p>
                    </div>

                    <FormControlLabel
                        control={
                            <Switch
                                checked={maintenanceMode.isActive}
                                onChange={(e) => setMaintenanceMode((prev) => ({ ...prev, isActive: e.target.checked }))}
                                name="maintenanceMode"
                                color="primary"
                                disabled={isUpdatingSettings} // Disable during update
                            />
                        }
                        label={<Typography>Maintenance Mode <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">(Site will be temporarily unavailable to public)</span></Typography>}
                    />
                    <Input
                        label="Message"
                        name="message"
                        value={maintenanceMode.message}
                        onChange={(e) => setMaintenanceMode((prev) => ({ ...prev, message: e.target.value }))}
                        placeholder="Enter maintenance mode message"
                        disabled={isUpdatingSettings} // Disable during update
                    />

                    <div className="flex justify-end pt-4">
                        <Button type="submit" isLoading={isUpdatingSettings} disabled={isUpdatingSettings || isLoadingSettings}>
                            {isUpdatingSettings ? 'Saving Settings...' : 'Save Settings'}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </Card>
        </div>
    );
};

export default GlobalSettings;

// src/Pages/LiveBlogs/LiveBlogForm.jsx
// --- COMMIT: feat(live-blogs): Replace textarea with WordEditor for rich text description ---

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { useCreateLiveBlogMutation, useUpdateLiveBlogMutation, useGetLiveBlogByIdQuery } from '../../_core/Slices/liveBlogSlice';

// **FIX**: Import WordEditor instead of Textarea
import {
    Heading,
    Button,
    Card,
    Input,
    Select,
} from '../../components/components';

import WordEditor from '../../components/WordEditor/WordEditor';
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import { ArrowLeft, UploadCloud, X } from 'lucide-react';

const LiveBlogForm = ({ isEditMode = false }) => {
    const { blogId } = useParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    // State
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ title: '', description: '', status: 'draft' });
    const [imageFile, setImageFile] = useState(null);
    const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState(null);
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null);

    // RTK Query Hooks
    const { data: existingData, isLoading: isLoadingExisting } = useGetLiveBlogByIdQuery(blogId, { skip: !isEditMode });
    const [createLiveBlog, { isLoading: isCreating }] = useCreateLiveBlogMutation();
    const [updateLiveBlog, { isLoading: isUpdating }] = useUpdateLiveBlogMutation();
    const isLoading = isCreating || isUpdating;

    // Effect to populate form in edit mode
    useEffect(() => {
        if (isEditMode && existingData) {
            setFormData({
                title: existingData.title || '',
                description: existingData.description || '', // This will be passed to WordEditor
                status: existingData.status || 'draft',
            });
            setImagePreview(existingData.featuredImage?.url);
        }
    }, [isEditMode, existingData]);

    // --- Handlers ---
    const handleChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    };

    // **FIX**: Add a specific handler for the WordEditor component
    const handleEditorChange = (htmlContent) => {
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, description: htmlContent }));
    };

    const handleFileChange = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (file) {
            if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
                toast.error("Image file size should not exceed 5MB.");
                return;
            }
            setImageFile(file);
            setImagePreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
        }
    };

    const removeImage = () => {
        setImageFile(null);
        setImagePreview(null);
        if (fileInputRef.current) {
            fileInputRef.current.value = "";
        }
    };

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!formData.title.trim()) {
            toast.error("Title is required.");
            return;
        }

        const data = new FormData();
        data.append('title', formData.title);
        data.append('description', formData.description); // The HTML from WordEditor will be sent
        data.append('status', formData.status);
        if (imageFile) {
            data.append('featuredImage', imageFile);
        }

        try {
            if (isEditMode) {
                await updateLiveBlog({ id: blogId, data }).unwrap();
                toast.success('Live Blog updated successfully!');
            } else {
                await createLiveBlog(data).unwrap();
                toast.success('Live Blog created successfully!');
            }
            navigate('/dashboard/live-blogs');
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || 'An error occurred.');
        }
    };

    if (isLoadingExisting) {
        return <CircularProgressBox text="Loading blog details..." />;
    }

    const statusOptions = [
        { value: 'draft', label: 'Draft' },
        { value: 'live', label: 'Live' },
        { value: 'archived', label: 'Archived' },
    ];

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title={isEditMode ? "Edit Live Blog" : "Create New Live Blog"}
                actions={<Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/live-blogs')} icon={<ArrowLeft size={16} />}>Back to List</Button>}
            />
            <Card>
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-6">
                    <Input
                        name="title"
                        label="Title"
                        value={formData.title}
                        onChange={handleChange}
                        required
                        placeholder="e.g., Israel-Gaza War Live Updates"
                    />

                    {/* **THE FIX IS HERE:** Replacing Textarea with WordEditor */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Description</label>
                        <WordEditor
                            content={formData.description}
                            onChange={handleEditorChange}
                            placeholder="Enter a detailed description for the live event..."
                        />
                    </div>
                    {/* End of the fix */}

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-2">Featured Image</label>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                            {imagePreview && (
                                <div className="relative w-40 h-40">
                                    <img src={imagePreview} alt="Preview" className="rounded-lg w-full h-full object-cover" />
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={removeImage}
                                        className="absolute top-1 right-1 bg-red-500 text-white rounded-full p-1 shadow-md hover:bg-red-600 transition-colors"
                                    >
                                        <X size={14} />
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            )}
                            <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} icon={<UploadCloud size={16} />}>
                                {imagePreview ? "Change Image" : "Upload Image"}
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                        <input type="file" ref={fileInputRef} onChange={handleFileChange} className="hidden" accept="image/*" />
                    </div>

                    <Select
                        name="status"
                        label="Status"
                        value={formData.status}
                        onChange={handleChange}
                        options={statusOptions}
                    />

                    <div className="flex justify-end pt-4 border-t border-gray-200">
                        <Button type="submit" isLoading={isLoading} disabled={isLoading}>
                            {isEditMode ? 'Save Changes' : 'Create Live Blog'}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </Card>
        </div>
    );
};

export default LiveBlogForm;

// src/Pages/LiveBlogs/LiveBlogList.jsx
// --- COMMIT: feat(live-blogs): Create page to list all live blog events ---

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Heading, Button, MuiTable, CircularProgressBox } from '../../components/components';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
import { useGetLiveBlogsQuery, useDeleteLiveBlogMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/liveBlogSlice';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission';
import { formatDate } from '../../utils/formatters';
import { Box, Chip, IconButton, Tooltip, Typography } from '@mui/material';
import { Edit, Trash2, Plus, Radio } from 'lucide-react';

const LiveBlogList = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { data, isLoading, isError, error } = useGetLiveBlogsQuery();
    const [deleteLiveBlog, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteLiveBlogMutation();

    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [blogToDelete, setBlogToDelete] = useState(null);

    const openDeleteModal = (blog) => {
        setBlogToDelete(blog);
        setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
    };

    const confirmDelete = async () => {
        if (!blogToDelete) return;
        try {
            await deleteLiveBlog(blogToDelete._id).unwrap();
            toast.success(`Live Blog "${blogToDelete.title}" deleted.`);
            setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || 'Failed to delete live blog.');
        }
    };

    const userCanManage = hasPermission('editor');

    const columns = [
        { header: "Title", field: "title", render: (title) => <Typography variant="body2" fontWeight="bold">{title}</Typography> },
        { header: "Status", field: "status", align: "center", render: (status) => <Chip label={status} color={status === 'live' ? 'success' : 'default'} size="small" sx={{ textTransform: 'capitalize' }} /> },
        { header: "Author", field: "author", render: (author) => author?.username || 'N/A' },
        { header: "Last Update", field: "lastUpdatedAt", render: (date) => formatDate(date) },
        {
            header: "Actions", field: "actions", align: "right", render: (value, row) => (
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 0.5, justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                    <Button size="sm" onClick={() => navigate(`/dashboard/live-blogs/manage/${row._id}`)} icon={<Radio size={14} />}>Manage</Button>
                    <Tooltip title="Edit"><IconButton size="small" onClick={() => navigate(`/dashboard/live-blogs/edit/${row._id}`)}><Edit size={16} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                    <Tooltip title="Delete"><IconButton size="small" color="error" onClick={() => openDeleteModal(row)}><Trash2 size={16} /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                </Box>
            )
        },
    ];

    if (isLoading) return <CircularProgressBox text="Loading Live Blogs..." />;
    if (isError) return <Typography color="error">Error: {error.data?.message}</Typography>;

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={confirmDelete}
                isLoading={isDeleting}
                title="Delete Live Blog"
                variant="danger"
            >
                Are you sure you want to delete "<strong>{blogToDelete?.title}</strong>"? All associated updates will be permanently removed.
            </ConfirmModal>

            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Live Blogs"
                description="Create and manage real-time news events."
                actions={userCanManage && <Button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/live-blogs/create')} icon={<Plus size={16} />}>New Live Blog</Button>}
            />

            <MuiTable
                title="All Live Blog Events"
                columns={columns}
                rows={data?.blogs || []}
            />
        </div>
    );
};

export default LiveBlogList;

// src/Pages/LiveBlogs/LiveUpdatesManager.jsx
// --- COMMIT: feat(live-blogs): Adapt live updates page to manage a specific blog ---

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { useCreateLiveUpdateMutation, useGetLiveUpdatesQuery } from '../../_core/Slices/liveBlogSlice';
import { useGetLiveBlogByIdQuery } from '../../_core/Slices/liveBlogSlice';
import { Heading, Button, Card, Select, Textarea } from '../../components/components';
import LiveUpdatesFeed from '../../components/LiveUpdatesFeed/LiveUpdatesFeed';
import { Send, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission';

const LiveUpdatesManager = () => {
    const { blogId } = useParams();
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    // --- State for the form ---
    const [updateType, setUpdateType] = useState('text');
    const [updateText, setUpdateText] = useState('');
    const userCanManage = hasPermission('editor');
    const { data: blogData, isLoading: isLoadingBlog } = useGetLiveBlogByIdQuery(blogId);
    const [createLiveUpdate, { isLoading: isCreating }] = useCreateLiveUpdateMutation();
    // --- RTK Query Hooks ---


    const handleSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!updateText.trim()) {
            toast.error("Update content cannot be empty.");
            return;
        }

        const payload = {
            liveBlogId: blogId, // <-- IMPORTANT: Add the blogId to the payload
            content: {
                type: updateType,
                message: updateText, // Simplified to always use 'message' key
            }
        };

        createLiveUpdate(payload)
            .unwrap()
            .then(() => {
                setUpdateText('');
                toast.success('Update posted!');
            })
            .catch((err) => toast.error(err.data?.message || 'Failed to post update.'));
    };

    const updateTypeOptions = [
        { value: 'text', label: 'Standard Text Update' },
        { value: 'breaking', label: 'Breaking News' },
    ];


    if (!userCanManage) {
        return (
            <div className="p-6 text-center">
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-red-600">Access Denied</h1>
                <p>You do not have permission to manage live updates.</p>
                <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard')}>Go to Dashboard</Button>
            </div>
        );
    }

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}

                title={`Manage: ${blogData?.title || 'Live Blog'}`}
                description="Post real-time updates for this event."
                actions={<Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/live-blogs')} icon={<ArrowLeft size={16} />}>Back to List</Button>}
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                <div className="lg:col-span-1">
                    <Card>
                        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4">
                            <Heading level={3} title="Post a New Update" />
                            <Select
                                label="Update Type" name="updateType" value={updateType}
                                onChange={(e) => setUpdateType(e.target.value)}
                                searchable={false}
                                options={updateTypeOptions} disabled={isCreating}
                            />
                            <Textarea
                                label="Content" name="content" value={updateText}
                                onChange={(e) => setUpdateText(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder={`Enter content for a '${updateType}' update...`}
                                rows={5} required disabled={isCreating}
                            />
                            <div className="flex justify-end">
                                <Button type="submit" isLoading={isCreating} disabled={isCreating} icon={<Send size={16} />}>
                                    Post Update
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </Card>
                </div>
                <div className="lg:col-span-2">
                    <Card>
                        <div className="p-6">
                            <Heading level={3} title="Live Feed" />
                            <LiveUpdatesFeed liveBlogId={blogId} />
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default LiveUpdatesManager;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\LiveUpdates\LiveUpdates.jsx ---

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { useGetLiveUpdatesQuery, useCreateLiveUpdateMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/liveBlogSlice';
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission';

// UI Components
import { Heading, Button, Card, Select, Textarea } from '../../components/components';
import LiveUpdatesFeed from '../../components/LiveUpdatesFeed/LiveUpdatesFeed';
import { Send } from 'lucide-react';

const LiveUpdates = () => {

    // Form State
    const [updateType, setUpdateType] = useState('text');
    const [updateText, setUpdateText] = useState('');

    // RTK Query Mutation
    const [createLiveUpdate, { isLoading: isCreating }] = useCreateLiveUpdateMutation();

    const handleSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!updateText.trim()) {
            toast.error("Update content cannot be empty.");
            return;
        }

        const payload = {
            content: {
                type: updateType,
                // The key inside content can vary, so we handle it dynamically
                [updateType === 'text' ? 'message' : 'text']: updateText,
            }
        };

        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: createLiveUpdate,
            data: payload,
            toast: toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                // Clear the form after successful submission
                setUpdateText('');
            }
        });
    };

    // Permission check
    const userCanCreate = hasPermission('reader');

    if (!userCanCreate) {
        return (
            <div className="p-6 text-center">
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-red-600">Access Denied</h1>
                <p>You do not have permission to post live updates.</p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    const updateTypeOptions = [
        { value: 'text', label: 'Standard Text Update' },
        { value: 'breaking', label: 'Breaking News' },
    ];

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Live Updates Control Panel"
                description="Post real-time updates for live events or breaking news."
            />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                {/* --- Creation Form --- */}
                <div className="lg:col-span-1">
                    <Card>
                        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4">
                            <Heading level={3} title="Post a New Update" />
                            <Select
                                label="Update Type"
                                name="updateType"
                                searchable={false}
                                value={updateType}
                                onChange={(e) => setUpdateType(e.target.value)}
                                options={updateTypeOptions}
                                disabled={isCreating}
                            />
                            <Textarea
                                label="Content"
                                name="content"
                                value={updateText}
                                onChange={(e) => setUpdateText(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder={`Enter content for a '${updateType}' update...`}
                                rows={5}
                                required
                                disabled={isCreating}
                            />
                            <div className="flex justify-end">
                                <Button type="submit" isLoading={isCreating} disabled={isCreating} icon={<Send size={16} />}>
                                    Post Update
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </Card>
                </div>

                {/* --- Live Feed Preview --- */}
                <div className="lg:col-span-2">
                    <Card>
                        <div className="p-6">
                            <Heading level={3} title="Live Feed" />
                            {/* We can reuse the public component and pass a prop to show it's an admin view if needed */}
                            <LiveUpdatesFeed isAdminView={true} />
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default LiveUpdates;
