--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ManageRoles\ManageRoles.jsx ---

// Pages/ManageRoles/ManageRoles.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useGetAllRoleRequestsQuery, useProcessRoleRequestMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/userSlice';
import { MuiTable, Heading, Button } from '../../components/components';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Box, Avatar, Typography, Chip, Tooltip, IconButton } from '@mui/material';
import { Check, X } from 'lucide-react';

const ManageRoles = () => {
    const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    const [limit, setLimit] = useState(10);

    const { data: response, isLoading, isFetching } = useGetAllRoleRequestsQuery({ page, limit });
    const [processRequest, { isLoading: isProcessing }] = useProcessRoleRequestMutation();

    const handleProcess = async (requestId, status) => {
        try {
            await processRequest({ requestId, data: { status } }).unwrap();
            toast.success(`Request has been ${status}.`);
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || "Failed to process request.");
        }
    };

    const columns = [
        {
            header: "User", field: "user", render: (user) => (
                <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 2 }}>
                    <Avatar src={user.avatar?.url} />
                    <div>
                        <Typography variant="body2" fontWeight="bold">{user.fullName}</Typography>
                        <Typography variant="caption" color="text.secondary">{user.email}</Typography>
                    </div>
                </Box>
            )
        },
        { header: "Current Role", field: "currentRole", render: (role) => <Chip label={role} size="small" /> },
        { header: "Requested Role", field: "requestedRole", render: (role) => <Chip label={role} size="small" color="primary" /> },
        { header: "Reason", field: "reason" },
        {
            header: "Actions", field: "actions", align: "right", render: (_, row) => {
                return (
                    row.status === "pending" ? <Box>
                        <Tooltip title="Approve"><IconButton color="success" onClick={() => handleProcess(row._id, 'approved')} disabled={isProcessing}><Check /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                        <Tooltip title="Reject"><IconButton color="error" onClick={() => handleProcess(row._id, 'rejected')} disabled={isProcessing}><X /></IconButton></Tooltip>
                    </Box> : <Chip label={row.status} size="small" />
                )
            }
        },
    ];

    return (
        <div>
            <Heading title="Manage Role Requests" description="Review and process user role upgrade requests." />
            <MuiTable
                title="Pending Requests"
                columns={columns}
                rows={response?.requests || []}
                isLoading={isLoading || isFetching || isProcessing}
                totalRows={response?.totalRequests || 0}
                page={page - 1}
                rowsPerPage={limit}
                onPageChange={(e, newPage) => setPage(newPage + 1)}
                onRowsPerPageChange={(e) => setLimit(parseInt(e.target.value, 10))}
            />
        </div>
    );
};
export default ManageRoles;

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\ManageRoles\ManageRoles.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\MyArticles\MyArticles.jsx ---

"use client"

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { toast } from "react-toastify";

// RTK Query and other imports
import { useGetOwnArticlesQuery } from "../../_core/Slices/articleSlice.js"; // Using the new hook
import { ARTICLE_STATUS_OPTIONS, ARTICLE_SORT_OPTIONS } from '../../constants/articleConstants.js';
import { Select, Heading, Button, Card, Input } from "../../components/components";
import ArticlesCard from "../../components/ArticlesCard/ArticlesCard";
import ArticlesItem from "../../components/ArticlesItem/ArticlesItem";
import ConfirmModal from "../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal";
import { Search, Plus, Grid, List, SortAsc, SortDesc, FileText, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import Pagination from "../../components/Pagination/Pagination.jsx";

const ARTICLES_PER_PAGE = 9;

const MyArticles = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    // --- State for Filtering, Sorting, and Pagination ---
    const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState("grid");
    const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
    const [filters, setFilters] = useState({ status: "", category: "" });
    const [sortConfig, setSortConfig] = useState({ sortBy: "createdAt", sortOrder: "desc" });
    const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

    // --- RTK Query Hook ---
    const {
        data: apiResponse,
        isLoading,
        isError,
        error,
        refetch, // Get refetch to pass down to child components
    } = useGetOwnArticlesQuery({
        status: filters.status || undefined,
        // The API for 'my-articles' might also accept these params
        page: currentPage,
        limit: ARTICLES_PER_PAGE,
        sortBy: sortConfig.sortBy,
        sortOrder: sortConfig.sortOrder,
    });

    // --- Client-Side Search (since API might not support it on this endpoint) ---
    const filteredArticles = useMemo(() => {
        let articles = apiResponse?.articles || [];
        if (searchQuery) {
            const lowercasedQuery = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
            articles = articles.filter(article =>
                article.title.toLowerCase().includes(lowercasedQuery)
            );
        }
        // Category filtering would also be client-side if API doesn't support it here
        if (filters.category) {
            articles = articles.filter(article => article.category?._id === filters.category);
        }
        return articles;
    }, [apiResponse, searchQuery, filters.category]);

    const uniqueCategoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
        if (!apiResponse?.articles) return [];
        const unique = {};
        apiResponse.articles.forEach(article => {
            if (article.category?._id) unique[article.category._id] = { value: article.category._id, label: article.category.name };
        });
        return [{ value: "", label: "All Categories" }, ...Object.values(unique)];
    }, [apiResponse]);

    // --- Handlers ---
    const handleFilterChange = (e) => { setFilters(prev => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })); setCurrentPage(1); };
    const handleSortChange = (e) => { setSortConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, [e.target.name]: e.target.value })); setCurrentPage(1); };
    const handleSortOrderToggle = () => { setSortConfig(prev => ({ ...prev, sortOrder: prev.sortOrder === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc' })); setCurrentPage(1); };
    const handleSearchChange = (e) => { setSearchQuery(e.target.value); setCurrentPage(1); };

    useEffect(() => { if (isError) toast.error(error?.data?.message || "Failed to fetch your articles.") }, [isError, error]);

    if (isLoading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="animate-pulse">
                    <div className="h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/4 mb-4"></div>
                    <div className="h-4 bg-gray-200 rounded w-1/2 mb-8"></div>
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                    {[...Array(6)].map((_, i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="animate-pulse bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm"><div className="bg-gray-200 h-40 rounded"></div><div className="h-4 mt-4 bg-gray-200 rounded"></div><div className="h-3 mt-2 w-3/4 bg-gray-200 rounded"></div></div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="My Authored Articles"
                description="View, manage, and track the status of all the articles you have written."
                actions={
                    <Button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard/create-article")} icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}>
                        Create New Article
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            <Card>
                <div className="p-4">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-4">
                        <div className="lg:col-span-2">
                            <Input placeholder="Search your articles..." value={searchQuery} onChange={handleSearchChange} leftIcon={<Search className="size-4" />} />
                        </div>
                        <Select name="category" value={filters.category} onChange={handleFilterChange} options={uniqueCategoryOptions} placeholder="Filter by Category" />
                        <Select name="status" value={filters.status} onChange={handleFilterChange} options={ARTICLE_STATUS_OPTIONS} placeholder="Filter by Status" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-end">
                        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                            <Button variant={viewMode === "grid" ? "primary" : "outline"} size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode("grid")}><Grid className="size-4" /></Button>
                            <Button variant={viewMode === "list" ? "primary" : "outline"} size="sm" onClick={() => setViewMode("list")}><List className="size-4" /></Button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </Card>

            {filteredArticles.length === 0 ? (
                <Card>
                    <div className="text-center py-12">
                        <FileText className="size-12 text-gray-400 mx-auto mb-4" />
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-medium">You haven't written any articles yet.</h3>
                        <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6">Let's get started!</p>
                        <Button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard/create-article")} icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}>Create Your First Article</Button>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            ) : (
                <div className={viewMode === "grid" ? "grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6" : "space-y-4"}>
                    {filteredArticles.map((article) =>
                        viewMode === "grid" ? (
                            <ArticlesCard key={article._id} article={article} onActionComplete={refetch} />
                        ) : (
                            <ArticlesItem key={article._id} article={article} onActionComplete={refetch} />
                        ),
                    )}
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Note: Pagination would need to be client-side if API doesn't support it for this route, or enabled if it does. */}
            {apiResponse?.totalPages > 1 && (
                <Card>
                    <div className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                            Showing {apiResponse?.articles.length} of {apiResponse.totalArticles} articles
                        </span>
                        <Pagination
                            currentPage={apiResponse.currentPage}
                            totalPages={apiResponse?.totalPages}
                            onPageChange={(page) => setCurrentPage(page)}
                        />
                    </div>
                </Card>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

export default MyArticles;

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\MyArticles\MyArticles.jsx ---

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\NewsArticle\NewsArticle.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useRef, useMemo, useEffect } from "react"
import { Plus, Eye, Globe, Tag, FileText, Save, Upload, X, Star, Zap, Award, ChevronDown, Clock, Image, Pin, LucideHighlighter, PenBox } from "lucide-react" // Added new icons

// Assuming these are your custom, reusable UI components
import { Select, Input, Heading, Card, Button, Textarea } from "../../components/components"
import WordEditor from "../../components/WordEditor/WordEditor";
import CircularProgressBox from "../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox";
// You will likely need a Switch component for a better UI, but we'll use standard checkboxes for now
// import { Switch } from '../../components/components';


import { ARTICLE_STATUS_OPTIONS, ARTICLE_LAYOUT_OPTIONS } from "../../constants/articleConstants.js";

// RTK Query Hooks for data fetching and mutation
import { useCreateArticleMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/articleSlice"
import { useGetCategoriesQuery } from "../../_core/Slices/categorySlice"
import { useSelector } from "react-redux"
import { hasPermission } from "../../Helper/hasPermission"; // Assuming helper exists
import { toast } from "react-toastify"
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from "react-router"

const NewsArticle = () => {

    // --- Component State ---
    const defaultFormData = {
        title: "",
        content: "",
        category: "",
        excerpt: "",
        tags: [],
        slug: "",
        status: "draft",
        featuredImage: null,
        imageCaption: "",
        isOpinion: false,
        publishedAt: "",
        isFeatured: false,
        isTopHeadline: false,
        isEditorChoice: false,
        isPinned: false,
        isHighlighted: false,
        layoutType: "normal",
        metaTitle: "",
        metaDescription: ""
    }
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState(defaultFormData)
    const [currentTag, setCurrentTag] = useState("")
     const [factBoxContent, setFactBoxContent] = useState("");

      const handleFactBoxEditorChange = (html) => {
        setFactBoxContent(html);
    };


    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({})
    const [rootCategory, setRootCategory] = useState(null)
    const [isSeoOpen, setIsSeoOpen] = useState(false)
    const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState(null)
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null)

    // --- RTK Query Hooks (Unchanged) ---
    const [createArticle, { isLoading: isSubmitting }] = useCreateArticleMutation()
    const { data: categoriesData, isLoading: isLoadingCategories, isError, error: categoryFetchError } = useGetCategoriesQuery()

    // --- Memoized Data Transformation (Unchanged) ---
    const categoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
        if (!categoriesData) return []
        return categoriesData.map(cat => ({
            value: cat._id,
            label: cat.name,
            description: cat.description,
        }))
    }, [categoriesData])
    const subCategoryOptions = useMemo(() => {
        if (!rootCategory) return []
        const parentCategory = categoriesData.filter((cat) => cat._id === rootCategory)[0]
        console.log(parentCategory, "parent category")
        return parentCategory.subcategories?.map(cat => ({
            value: cat._id,
            label: cat.name,
            description: cat.description,
        }))
    }, [categoriesData, rootCategory])

    // --- Static Options (Unchanged) ---
    const statusOptions = [
        { value: "draft", label: "Draft" },
        { value: "published", label: "Published" },
        // { value: "pending-review", label: "Pending Review" },
        { value: "scheduled", label: "Schedule" },
        { value: "archived", label: "Archived" },
    ]

    // --- Helper & Event Handler Functions ---
    const generateSlug = (title) =>
        title
            .replace(/\b(a|an|the|in|on|at|to|for|with|from|by|about|as|of|into|over|under|can|could|may|might|must|shall|should|will|would|is|are|was|were|be|been|being|do|does|did|has|have|had)\b/gi, '')
            .toLowerCase()
            .replace(/[^a-z0-9 -]/g, '')
            .replace(/\s+/g, '-')
            .replace(/-+/g, '-')
            .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');


    // **MODIFIED**: Updated handleChange to handle checkboxes
    const handleChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value, type, checked } = e.target;
        const newValue = type === 'checkbox' ? checked : value;
        setFormData((prev) => {
            const newData = { ...prev, [name]: newValue }
            if (name === "title") newData.slug = generateSlug(value)
            return newData
        })
        if (errors[name]) setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: undefined }))
    }

    const handleEditorChange = (html) => {
        setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, content: html }))
        if (errors.content) setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, content: undefined }))
    }

    const handleFileUpload = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0]
        if (!file) return
        if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
            setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: "Please select a valid image file." }))
            return
        }
        if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
            setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: "Image size cannot exceed 5MB." }))
            return
        }
        setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: file }))
        setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: undefined }))
        const reader = new FileReader()
        reader.onloadend = () => setImagePreview(reader.result)
        reader.readAsDataURL(file)
    }

    const removeFeaturedImage = () => {
        setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, featuredImage: null }))
        setImagePreview(null)
        if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = ""
    }

    const handleTagKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key !== "Enter" || !currentTag.trim()) return
        e.preventDefault()
        const newTag = currentTag.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\b\w/g, char => char.toUpperCase())
        if (!formData.tags.includes(newTag)) {
            setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, tags: [...prev.tags, newTag] }))
        }
        setCurrentTag("")
    }
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const removeTag = (tagToRemove) => setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, tags: prev.tags.filter((tag) => tag !== tagToRemove) }))

    const validateForm = () => {
        const newErrors = {}
        if (!formData.title.trim()) newErrors.title = "Title is required"
        if (!formData.content.trim()) newErrors.content = "Content is required"
        if (!rootCategory) newErrors.category = "Category is required"
        if (formData.status === 'scheduled' && !formData.publishedAt) {
            newErrors.publishedAt = "Please select a publish date and time for scheduling.";
        }
        setErrors(newErrors)
        return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0
    }

    const handleSubmit = async (e, submissionStatus = formData.status) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!validateForm()) return

        const data = new FormData()
        data.append("title", formData.title)
        data.append("content", formData.content)
        data.append("category", formData.category || rootCategory)
        data.append("excerpt", formData.excerpt)
        data.append("imageCaption", formData.imageCaption)
        data.append("status", submissionStatus)
 data.append("factBoxContent", factBoxContent);
        // **NEW**: Append the boolean flags
        data.append("isFeatured", formData.isFeatured);
        data.append("isTopHeadline", formData.isTopHeadline);
        data.append("isEditorChoice", formData.isEditorChoice);
        data.append("isPinned", formData.isPinned);
        data.append("isHighlighted", formData.isHighlighted);
        data.append("isOpinion", formData.isOpinion);
        data.append("layoutType", formData.layoutType);
        data.append("metaDescription", formData.metaDescription);
        data.append("metaTitle", formData.metaTitle);

        if (submissionStatus === 'scheduled' && formData.publishedAt) {

            data.append("publishedAt", new Date(formData.publishedAt).toISOString());
        }
        if (formData.tags.length > 0) {
            formData.tags.forEach(tag => data.append('tags[]', tag))
        }
        if (formData.featuredImage) {
            data.append("featuredImage", formData.featuredImage)
        }

        try {
            await createArticle(data).unwrap()
            toast.success(`Article successfully saved as ${submissionStatus}!`)

            setFormData(defaultFormData)
            navigate(-1)
        } catch (err) {
            console.error("Failed to submit article:", err)
            toast.error(`Error: ${err.data?.message || "An unexpected error occurred."}`)
        }
    }

    const handleSaveAsDraft = (e) => handleSubmit(e, 'draft')
    const handleSaveAsPreview = (e) => handleSubmit(e, 'pending-review')

    const handlePreview = () => {
        const previewWindow = window.open("", "_blank");
        if (!previewWindow) {
            alert("Please allow popups to preview the article.");
            return;
        }

        const previewStyles = `
        body { 
            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif; 
            line-height: 1.6; 
            color: #333; 
            max-width: 800px; 
            margin: 40px auto; 
            padding: 20px; 
        }
        h1 { font-size: 2.5em; }
        .meta { color: #666; }
        .featured-image { max-width: 100%; height: auto; border-radius: 8px; margin: 1em 0; }
        .tag { display: inline-block; background-color: #f0f0f0; padding: 5px 10px; margin-right: 5px; border-radius: 15px; font-size: 0.8em; }
        
        
        .article-content {
            white-space: pre-wrap; 
            word-wrap: break-word; 
        }
    `;

        const categoryLabel = categoryOptions.find(c => c.value === formData.category)?.label || 'N/A';
        const tagsHtml = formData.tags.length > 0 ? `<div><strong>Tags:</strong> ${formData.tags.map(tag => `<span class="tag">${tag}</span>`).join(" ")}</div>` : '';

        // --- HTML MEIN CHANGE YAHAN HAI ---
        previewWindow.document.write(`
        <html>
        <head>
            <title>Preview: ${formData.title}</title>
            <style>${previewStyles}</style>
        </head>
        <body>
            <h1>${formData.title}</h1>
            <div class="meta">
                <span>Category: ${categoryLabel}</span> | <span>Status: ${formData.status}</span>
            </div>
            ${imagePreview ? `<img src="${imagePreview}" alt="Featured" class="featured-image" />` : ""}
            
            <!-- === YAHAN PAR CLASS LAGAI HAI === -->
            <div class="article-content">${formData.content}</div>
            
            ${tagsHtml}
        </body>
        </html>
    `);
        previewWindow.document.close();
    }

    // --- Conditional Rendering (Unchanged) ---
    if (isLoadingCategories) return <div className="h-screen w-full flex justify-center items-center">
        <CircularProgressBox />
    </div>;
    if (isError) return <div className="p-6 text-red-600">Error loading categories: {categoryFetchError.data?.message || 'Please try again later.'}</div>

    const canPublish = hasPermission('editor');
    console.log(rootCategory, "root")
    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={2}
                title="Create New Article"
                description="Write and publish engaging content for your audience"
                // --- ADDED: Action button for preview ---
                actions={
                    <Button variant="outline" type="button" onClick={handlePreview} icon={<Eye className="size-4" />}>
                        Preview Article
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            <>
                <form onSubmit={(e) => handleSubmit(e, canPublish ? formData.status : 'pending-review')} className="space-y-6">
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                        <Input
                            id="title"
                            name="title"
                            label="Article Title"
                            value={formData.title}
                            onChange={handleChange} required placeholder="Enter an engaging article title" error={errors.title} leftIcon={<FileText className="size-4" />} />

                        <Input id="slug" disabled name="slug" label="URL Slug" value={formData.slug} onChange={handleChange} placeholder="url-friendly-slug" readOnly leftIcon={<Globe className="size-4" />} helperText="Auto-generated from title." />

                    </div>
                    <Input
                        inputClassname={"w-full"}
                        id="excerpt"
                        name="excerpt"
                        label="Article Excerpt"
                        value={formData.excerpt}
                        onChange={handleChange} placeholder="Enter a short description of article" error={errors.title} leftIcon={<FileText className="size-4" />} />
                    {/* Featured Image Upload */}
                    <div className="">

                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 my-2 ">Featured Image</label>
                        <div className="space-y-3 mb-3">
                            {imagePreview ? (
                                <div className="relative inline-block">
                                    <img src={imagePreview} alt="Featured image preview" className="w-full max-w-md h-48 object-cover rounded-lg border" />
                                    <button type="button" onClick={removeFeaturedImage} className="absolute top-2 right-2 p-1 bg-red-500 text-white rounded-full"><X className="size-4" /></button>
                                </div>
                            ) : (
                                <div onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} className="border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg p-6 text-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <Upload className="size-8 text-gray-400 mx-auto mb-2" />
                                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Click to upload</p>
                                </div>
                            )}
                            <input ref={fileInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handleFileUpload} className="hidden" />
                            {errors.featuredImage && <p className="text-xs text-red-500">{errors.featuredImage}</p>}
                        </div>
                        <Input
                            inputClassname={"w-full my-3 "}
                            id="imageCaption"
                            name="imageCaption"
                            label="Image Caption"
                            value={formData.imageCaption}
                            onChange={handleChange} placeholder="Enter caption of image" leftIcon={<Image className="size-4" />} />
                    </div>

                    {/* Rich Text Editor Section */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Article Content <span className="text-red-500">*</span></label>
                        <WordEditor content={formData.content} onChange={handleEditorChange} />
                        {errors.content && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-red-500">{errors.content}</p>}
                    </div>
                             <Card className="mt-6">
                <Card.Header title="Fact Box / Summary (Optional)" />
                <Card.Body className="p-4">
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-2">
                        Is box mein likha gaya content article ke andar ek alag, styled box mein nazar aayega.
                    </p>
                    <WordEditor
                        content={factBoxContent}
                        onChange={handleFactBoxEditorChange}
                        placeholder="Key takeaways, summary, ya address yahan likhein..."
                    />
                </Card.Body>
            </Card>
            
                    {/* Category and Status Section */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                        <Select label="Category" value={rootCategory} onChange={(e) => setRootCategory(e.target.value)} options={categoryOptions} placeholder="Select a category" required error={errors.category} />
                        <Select disabled={!rootCategory} id="category" name="category" label="Sub Category" value={formData.category} onChange={handleChange} options={subCategoryOptions} placeholder="Select a category" />
                        <Select
                            id="layoutType"
                            name="layoutType"
                            label="Article Layout"
                            value={formData.layoutType}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            options={ARTICLE_LAYOUT_OPTIONS}
                            required
                        />
                        {canPublish && <Select id="status" name="status" label="Status" value={formData.status} onChange={handleChange} options={statusOptions} required />}
                    </div>
                    {canPublish && formData.status === 'scheduled' && (
                        <div className="p-4 bg-blue-50 border-l-4 border-blue-400 rounded-r-lg">
                            <Input
                                label="Publish Date and Time"
                                name="publishedAt"
                                type="datetime-local"
                                value={formData.publishedAt}
                                onChange={handleChange}
                                required={formData.status === 'scheduled'}
                                leftIcon={<Clock className="size-4" />}
                                helperText="The article will be automatically published at this time."
                                error={errors.publishedAt}
                            />
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Tags Section */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Tags</label>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2 py-3 border border-gray-400 shadow bg-white rounded-md">
                            {formData.tags.map((tag) => (
                                <span key={tag} className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-medium bg-blue-100 text-blue-800">
                                    {tag} <X onClick={() => removeTag(tag)} className="ml-1.5 size-3 cursor-pointer" />
                                </span>
                            ))}
                            <input type="text" value={currentTag} onChange={(e) => setCurrentTag(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={handleTagKeyDown} className="flex-1 outline-none border-none bg-transparent text-sm" placeholder="Add a tag and press Enter..." />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className=" pt-6">
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsSeoOpen(!isSeoOpen)} className="w-full flex justify-between items-center font-semibold text-lg text-gray-700">
                            <span>Advanced: SEO Settings</span>
                            <ChevronDown className={`transform transition-transform duration-200 ${isSeoOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
                        </button>
                        {isSeoOpen && (
                            <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
                                <Input name="metaTitle" label="Meta Title" value={formData.metaTitle} onChange={handleChange} placeholder="Custom title for search engine results" helperText="If empty, the article title will be used." />
                                <Textarea name="metaDescription" label="Meta Description" value={formData.metaDescription} onChange={handleChange} placeholder="Custom description for search engines" rows={3} helperText="A good meta description is around 155-160 characters." />
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {/* --- NEW SECTION for Article Flags --- */}

                    <div>
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-2">Article Flags</label>
                        <div className="p-4 border rounded-lg space-y-4">
                            {canPublish && <>

                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="checkbox" name="isFeatured" checked={formData.isFeatured} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Mark as Featured</span>
                                    <Star className="ml-2 size-4 text-yellow-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="checkbox" name="isTopHeadline" checked={formData.isTopHeadline} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Set as Top Headline</span>
                                    <Zap className="ml-2 size-4 text-orange-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="checkbox" name="isEditorChoice" checked={formData.isEditorChoice} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Editor's Choice</span>
                                    <Award className="ml-2 size-4 text-blue-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="checkbox" name="isPinned" checked={formData.isPinned} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Pin</span>
                                    <Pin className="ml-2 size-4 text-purple-500" />
                                </label>
                                <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                    <input type="checkbox" name="isHighlighted" checked={formData.isHighlighted} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                    <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Highlight</span>
                                    <LucideHighlighter className="ml-2 size-4 text-red-500" />
                                </label>
                            </>

                            }

                            <label className="flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                <input type="checkbox" name="isOpinion" checked={formData.isOpinion} onChange={handleChange} className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-gray-300 text-primary focus:ring-primary" />
                                <span className="ml-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Set as Opinion</span>
                                <PenBox className="ml-2 size-4 text-green-500" />
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    </div>


                    {/* Form Actions */}
                    <Card.Footer>
                        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-end gap-3">

                            {canPublish ? (
                                <>
                                    <Button variant="outline" type="button" onClick={handleSaveAsDraft} disabled={isSubmitting} icon={<Save className="size-4" />}>Save as Draft</Button>
                                    <Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} isLoading={isSubmitting} icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}>
                                        {isSubmitting ? "Saving..." : "Save Article"}
                                    </Button>
                                </>

                            ) : (
                                <Button type="button" onClick={handleSaveAsPreview} disabled={isSubmitting} isLoading={isSubmitting} icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}>
                                    {isSubmitting ? "Submitting..." : "Submit for Review"}
                                </Button>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </Card.Footer>
                </form>
            </>
        </div>
    )
}

export default NewsArticle;

        └── ProfilePage/
                ├── ProfilePage.jsx

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ProfilePage\ProfilePage.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { useSelector, useDispatch } from 'react-redux';
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import {
    Heading, Button, Input, Textarea, Card, Select,
} from "../../components/components";
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import { Box, Grid, Typography, Avatar, Chip, IconButton } from "@mui/material";
import { Camera, Save, KeyRound, Trash2, Link as LinkIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
    useGetProfileQuery,
    useUpdateProfileMutation,
    useUpdateAvatarMutation,
    useChangePasswordMutation,
} from "../../_core/Slices/userSlice";
import { setCredentials } from '../../_core/Slices/authSlice';
import { Country } from 'country-state-city';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission';

// Social media platforms
const socialMediaPlatforms = [
    { value: 'github', label: 'GitHub' },
    { value: 'linkedin', label: 'LinkedIn' },
    { value: 'twitter', label: 'Twitter' },
    { value: 'facebook', label: 'Facebook' },
    { value: 'instagram', label: 'Instagram' },
];

const ProfilePage = () => {
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
    const { userInfo: loggedInUser } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
    const dispatch = useDispatch();

    // Get all countries
    const countries = Country.getAllCountries();

    // State management
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        fullName: "",
        bio: "",
        role: "",
        country: { name: "", code: "" },
        socialMedia: []
    });
    const [passwordData, setPasswordData] = useState({
        oldPassword: "",
        newPassword: "",
        confirmPassword: ""
    });
    const [avatarFile, setAvatarFile] = useState(null);
    const [avatarPreview, setAvatarPreview] = useState("");
    const [originalData, setOriginalData] = useState(null);
    const [hasUnsavedChanges, setHasUnsavedChanges] = useState(false);
    const [newSocialLink, setNewSocialLink] = useState({ platform: "", url: "" });

    // RTK Query hooks
    const { data: profileDataResponse, isLoading: isLoadingProfile, isError: isProfileError, error: profileFetchError, refetch } = useGetProfileQuery();
    const [updateProfile, { isLoading: isUpdatingProfile }] = useUpdateProfileMutation();
    const [updateAvatar, { isLoading: isUpdatingAvatar }] = useUpdateAvatarMutation();
    const [changePassword, { isLoading: isChangingPassword }] = useChangePasswordMutation();

    // Effects
    useEffect(() => {
        if (profileDataResponse) {
            const { fullName, bio, role, country, socialMedia } = profileDataResponse;
            const initialData = {
                fullName: fullName || "",
                bio: bio || "",
                role: role || "",
                country: country || { name: "", code: "" },
                socialMedia: socialMedia || []
            };
            setFormData(initialData);
            setOriginalData(initialData);
            setAvatarPreview(profileDataResponse.avatar?.url || "");
            setHasUnsavedChanges(false);
        }
    }, [profileDataResponse]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (originalData) {
            const dataChanged = JSON.stringify(formData) !== JSON.stringify(originalData);
            setHasUnsavedChanges(dataChanged || !!avatarFile);
        }
    }, [formData, originalData, avatarFile]);

    // Handlers
    const handleProfileChange = (e) => {
        setFormData({ ...formData, [e.target.name]: e.target.value });
    };

    const handleCountryChange = (e) => {
        const countryCode = e.target.value;
        const selectedCountry = countries.find(c => c.isoCode === countryCode);

        setFormData({
            ...formData,
            country: {
                name: selectedCountry?.name || '',
                code: countryCode
            }
        });
    };

    const handleSocialMediaChange = (e) => {
        setNewSocialLink({ ...newSocialLink, [e.target.name]: e.target.value });
    };

    const handleAddSocialLink = () => {
        if (!newSocialLink.platform || !newSocialLink.url) {
            toast.error("Please select a platform and enter a URL");
            return;
        }

        // Validate URL format
        if (!newSocialLink.url.match(/^https?:\/\//)) {
            toast.error("Please enter a valid URL starting with http:// or https://");
            return;
        }

        setFormData({
            ...formData,
            socialMedia: [...formData.socialMedia, {
                platform: newSocialLink.platform.toLowerCase(),
                url: newSocialLink.url
            }]
        });

        setNewSocialLink({ platform: "", url: "" });
    };

    const handleRemoveSocialLink = (index) => {
        const updatedSocialMedia = [...formData.socialMedia];
        updatedSocialMedia.splice(index, 1);
        setFormData({ ...formData, socialMedia: updatedSocialMedia });
    };

    const handlePasswordChange = (e) => {
        setPasswordData({ ...passwordData, [e.target.name]: e.target.value });
    };

    const handleAvatarChange = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (file) {
            if (file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) {
                toast.error("Avatar must be less than 2MB.");
                return;
            }
            if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
                toast.error("Please select an image file (JPEG, PNG, etc.)");
                return;
            }
            setAvatarFile(file);
            setAvatarPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
        }
    };

    const handleResetProfileForm = () => {
        setFormData(originalData);
        setAvatarFile(null);
        setAvatarPreview(profileDataResponse.avatar?.url || "");
        setNewSocialLink({ platform: "", url: "" });
        toast.info("Changes have been discarded.");
    };

    const handleRemoveAvatar = async () => {
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append("removeAvatar", true);
        try {
            const response = await updateAvatar(formData).unwrap();
            const updatedUserInfo = { ...loggedInUser, user: response.data };
            dispatch(setCredentials(updatedUserInfo));
            setAvatarFile(null); // Reset file after successful upload
            toast.success("Avatar removed successfully");
            refetch()
        } catch (error) {
            toast.error(error?.data?.message || "Failed to remove avatar");
        }
    };
    const handleUpdateAvatar = async () => {
        if (!avatarFile) {
            toast.error("Please select an image first");
            return;
        }

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append("avatar", avatarFile);

        try {
            const response = await updateAvatar(formData).unwrap();
            const updatedUserInfo = { ...loggedInUser, user: response.data };
            dispatch(setCredentials(updatedUserInfo));
            setAvatarFile(null); // Reset file after successful upload
            toast.success("Avatar updated successfully");
            refetch()
        } catch (error) {
            toast.error(error?.data?.message || "Failed to update avatar");
        }
    };

    const handleProfileSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        try {
            const response = await updateProfile({
                fullName: formData.fullName,
                bio: formData.bio,
                role: formData.role,
                country: formData.country,
                socialMedia: formData.socialMedia
            }).unwrap();

            const updatedUserInfo = { ...loggedInUser, user: response.data };
            dispatch(setCredentials(updatedUserInfo));
            setOriginalData(formData); // Update original data to current state
            toast.success("Profile updated successfully");
            refetch()
        } catch (error) {
            toast.error(error?.data?.message || "Failed to update profile");
        }
    };

    const handlePasswordSubmit = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();

        // Validate passwords
        if (passwordData.newPassword !== passwordData.confirmPassword) {
            toast.error("New passwords do not match.");
            return;
        }
        if (passwordData.newPassword.length < 6) {
            toast.error("Password must be at least 6 characters.");
            return;
        }

        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: changePassword,
            data: {
                oldPassword: passwordData.oldPassword,
                newPassword: passwordData.newPassword
            },
            toast: toast,
            onSuccess: () => {
                setPasswordData({ oldPassword: "", newPassword: "", confirmPassword: "" });
                refetch()
            }

        });
    };

    if (isLoadingProfile) return <CircularProgressBox text="Loading Your Profile..." />;

    return (
        <div className="space-y-8 p-4 md:p-0">
            <Heading level={1} title="My Profile" description="Manage your personal information and security settings." />

            <Grid container spacing={4}>
                {/* Avatar Section */}
                <Grid item xs={12} md={4}>
                    <Card padding='sm'>
                        <Card.Body sx={{ display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", p: 4 }}>
                            <Box sx={{ position: "relative" }}>
                                <Avatar
                                    src={avatarPreview || profileDataResponse?.avatar?.url}
                                    sx={{ width: 120, height: 120, mb: 2, fontSize: '3rem' }}
                                >
                                    {formData.fullName?.[0]}
                                </Avatar>
                                <IconButton
                                    onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                                    size="small"
                                    sx={{
                                        position: "absolute",
                                        bottom: 10,
                                        right: 10,
                                        bgcolor: "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)",
                                        '&:hover': { bgcolor: 'white' }
                                    }}
                                    disabled={isUpdatingAvatar}
                                >
                                    <Camera size={18} />
                                </IconButton>
                                <input
                                    type="file"
                                    ref={fileInputRef}
                                    hidden
                                    accept="image/*"
                                    onChange={handleAvatarChange}
                                />
                            </Box>
                            <div className='flex flex-col'>

                                <Button
                                    onClick={handleUpdateAvatar}
                                    disabled={!avatarFile || isUpdatingAvatar}
                                    isLoading={isUpdatingAvatar}
                                    className="mt-4"
                                >
                                    Update Avatar
                                </Button>
                                <Button
                                    onClick={handleRemoveAvatar}
                                    disabled={isUpdatingAvatar}
                                    className="mt-4"
                                    variant="secondary"
                                >
                                    Remove Avatar
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                        </Card.Body>
                    </Card>
                </Grid>

                {/* Profile Form Section */}
                <Grid item xs={12} md={8}>
                    <form onSubmit={handleProfileSubmit}>
                        <Card padding='sm'>
                            <Card.Header title={"Profile Information"} />
                            <Card.Body className="p-6 space-y-4">
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                                    <Input
                                        label="Full Name"
                                        name="fullName"
                                        value={formData.fullName}
                                        onChange={handleProfileChange}
                                        disabled={isUpdatingProfile}
                                        required
                                    />
                                    {/* {hasPermission("superadmin")&& <Input
                                        label="Position/Role"
                                        name="role"
                                        value={formData.role}
                                        onChange={handleProfileChange}
                                        disabled={isUpdatingProfile}
                                        placeholder="E.g. Senior Developer"
                                    />} */}
                                </div>

                                <Textarea
                                    label="Bio"
                                    name="bio"
                                    placeholder="Tell us a little about yourself..."
                                    value={formData.bio}
                                    onChange={handleProfileChange}
                                    rows={4}
                                    disabled={isUpdatingProfile}
                                />

                                <Select
                                    label="Country"
                                    value={formData.country.code}
                                    onChange={handleCountryChange}
                                    options={countries.map(country => ({
                                        value: country.isoCode,
                                        label: country.name
                                    }))}
                                    disabled={isUpdatingProfile}
                                    placeholder="Select your country"
                                />

                                <div className="space-y-2">
                                    <Typography variant="subtitle2" fontWeight={600}>Social Media Links</Typography>
                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-5 gap-2">
                                        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                                            <Select
                                                label="Platform"
                                                name="platform"
                                                value={newSocialLink.platform}
                                                onChange={handleSocialMediaChange}
                                                options={socialMediaPlatforms}
                                                disabled={isUpdatingProfile}
                                                placeholder="Select platform"
                                            />
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                                            <Input
                                                label="URL"
                                                name="url"
                                                value={newSocialLink.url}
                                                onChange={handleSocialMediaChange}
                                                disabled={isUpdatingProfile}
                                                placeholder="https://example.com/username"
                                            />
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="flex items-end">
                                            <Button
                                                type="button"
                                                onClick={handleAddSocialLink}
                                                disabled={isUpdatingProfile || !newSocialLink.platform || !newSocialLink.url}
                                                className="h-10 w-full"
                                            >
                                                Add
                                            </Button>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    {formData.socialMedia.length > 0 && (
                                        <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                                            {formData.socialMedia.map((link, index) => (
                                                <div key={index} className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded">
                                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                        <LinkIcon size={16} className="text-gray-500" />
                                                        <span className="font-medium capitalize">{link.platform}:</span>
                                                        <a
                                                            href={link.url}
                                                            target="_blank"
                                                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                                                            className="text-blue-600 hover:underline truncate max-w-xs"
                                                        >
                                                            {link.url}
                                                        </a>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <IconButton
                                                        size="small"
                                                        onClick={() => handleRemoveSocialLink(index)}
                                                        disabled={isUpdatingProfile}
                                                        aria-label={`Remove ${link.platform} link`}
                                                    >
                                                        <Trash2 size={16} className="text-red-500" />
                                                    </IconButton>
                                                </div>
                                            ))}
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            </Card.Body>
                            <Card.Footer sx={{ justifyContent: "flex-end", p: 2 }}>
                                <Button
                                    type="button"
                                    variant="ghost"
                                    onClick={handleResetProfileForm}
                                    disabled={!hasUnsavedChanges || isUpdatingProfile}
                                >
                                    Reset
                                </Button>
                                <Button
                                    type="submit"
                                    disabled={!hasUnsavedChanges || isUpdatingProfile}
                                    isLoading={isUpdatingProfile}
                                    icon={<Save size={16} />}
                                >
                                    Save Profile
                                </Button>
                            </Card.Footer>
                        </Card>
                    </form>
                    <Card padding='sm' className="mt-6">
                        <Card.Header title={"Change Password"} />
                        <Card.Body className="p-6 space-y-4">
                            <Input
                                label="Current Password"
                                name="oldPassword"
                                type="password"
                                value={passwordData.oldPassword}
                                onChange={handlePasswordChange}
                                disabled={isChangingPassword}
                                showPasswordToggle={true}
                            />
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                                <Input
                                    label="New Password"
                                    name="newPassword"
                                    type="password"
                                    value={passwordData.newPassword}
                                    onChange={handlePasswordChange}
                                    disabled={isChangingPassword}
                                    showPasswordToggle={true}
                                />
                                <Input
                                    label="Confirm New Password"
                                    name="confirmPassword"
                                    type="password"
                                    value={passwordData.confirmPassword}
                                    onChange={handlePasswordChange}
                                    disabled={isChangingPassword}
                                    showPasswordToggle={true}
                                />
                            </div>
                        </Card.Body>
                        <Card.Footer sx={{ justifyContent: "flex-end", p: 2 }}>
                            <Button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={handlePasswordSubmit}
                                disabled={!passwordData.oldPassword || !passwordData.newPassword || !passwordData.confirmPassword}
                                isLoading={isChangingPassword}
                                icon={<KeyRound size={16} />}
                            >
                                Update Password
                            </Button>
                        </Card.Footer>
                    </Card>
                </Grid>
            </Grid>
        </div>
    );
};

export default ProfilePage;

// Pages/RequestRole/RequestRole.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useCreateRoleRequestMutation, useGetMyRoleRequestsQuery } from '../../_core/Slices/userSlice';
import { Heading, Button, Card, Select, Textarea } from '../../components/components';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { Shield, Send, Clock, CheckCircle, XCircle } from 'lucide-react';

const RequestRole = () => {
    const [requestedRole, setRequestedRole] = useState('author');
    const [reason, setReason] = useState('');

    const [createRequest, { isLoading }] = useCreateRoleRequestMutation();
    const { data: myRequests, isLoading: isLoadingHistory } = useGetMyRoleRequestsQuery();

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        try {
            await createRequest({ requestedRole, reason }).unwrap();
            toast.success("Your role upgrade request has been submitted.");
            setReason(''); // Clear form
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || "Failed to submit request.");
        }
    };

    const roleOptions = [
        { value: 'author', label: 'Author' },
        { value: 'editor', label: 'Editor' },
        { value: 'admin', label: 'Admin' },
    ];

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
            <Heading title="Request Role Upgrade" description="Submit a request to gain more permissions on the platform." />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                <Card>
                    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4">
                        <Select label="Requested Role" options={roleOptions} value={requestedRole} onChange={(e) => setRequestedRole(e.target.value)} />
                        <Textarea label="Reason for Request" value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} placeholder="Explain why you need this role..." rows={5} />
                        <div className="flex justify-end">
                            <Button type="submit" isLoading={isLoading} icon={<Send size={16} />}>Submit Request</Button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </Card>
                <Card>
                    <div className="p-6">
                        <Heading level={3} title="My Request History" />
                        <div className="mt-4 space-y-3 max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
                            {isLoadingHistory && <p>Loading history...</p>}
                            {myRequests?.map(req => (
                                <div key={req._id} className="p-3 border rounded-md">
                                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                                        <p>Request for: <span className="font-bold capitalize">{req.requestedRole}</span></p>
                                        <span className={`capitalize text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full ${req.status === 'pending' ? 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800' :
                                            req.status === 'approved' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-800'
                                            }`}>
                                            {req.status}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Submitted on: {new Date(req.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};
export default RequestRole;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\ResetPassword\ResetPassword.jsx ---

"use client"

import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { Lock, ArrowRight, ArrowLeft, Shield, CheckCircle } from "lucide-react"
import { Button, Input, } from "../../components/components"
import { useResetPasswordMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/authApiSlice.js"; // Changed path
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom" // Changed to react-router-dom
import { toast } from "react-toastify"
// import { makeRequest } from "../../Helper/ApiHelper" // Removed makeRequest

const ResetPassword = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        password: "",
        confirmPassword: "",
    })
    // const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false) // Not directly used by logic, Input component handles its own toggle
    // const [showConfirmPassword, setShowConfirmPassword] = useState(false) // Not directly used by logic
    // const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false) // To be replaced by isResettingPassword
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState({})
    const [passwordStrength, setPasswordStrength] = useState(0)

    const [resetPassword, { isLoading: isResettingPassword }] = useResetPasswordMutation(); // Updated hook

    useEffect(() => {
        // const { makeRequest } = require("../../Helper/ApiHelper"); // Removed makeRequest import from here
        const otpVerified = sessionStorage.getItem("otpVerified")
        // console.log(otpVerified) // Keep console.log for now if needed for debugging, or remove
        if (!otpVerified) {
            toast.error("OTP not verified. Redirecting...");
            navigate("/send-otp");
        }
    }, [navigate]);

    const params = useParams()
    const { email } = params

    // Password strength calculation
    const calculatePasswordStrength = (password) => {
        let strength = 0
        const checks = [
            password.length >= 8,
            /[A-Z]/.test(password),
            /[a-z]/.test(password),
            /[0-9]/.test(password),
            /[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(password),
        ]
        strength = checks.filter(Boolean).length
        return strength
    }

    // Password requirements
    const passwordRequirements = [
        { text: "At least 8 characters", test: (pwd) => pwd.length >= 8 },
        { text: "One uppercase letter", test: (pwd) => /[A-Z]/.test(pwd) },
        { text: "One lowercase letter", test: (pwd) => /[a-z]/.test(pwd) },
        { text: "One number", test: (pwd) => /[0-9]/.test(pwd) },
        { text: "One special character", test: (pwd) => /[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(pwd) },
    ]

    const handleChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target
        setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }))

        // Clear errors
        if (errors[name]) {
            setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: undefined }))
        }

        // Calculate password strength
        if (name === "password") {
            setPasswordStrength(calculatePasswordStrength(value))
        }

        // Check confirm password match
        if (name === "confirmPassword" || (name === "password" && formData.confirmPassword)) {
            const newPassword = name === "password" ? value : formData.password
            const newConfirmPassword = name === "confirmPassword" ? value : formData.confirmPassword

            if (newConfirmPassword && newPassword !== newConfirmPassword) {
                setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, confirmPassword: "Passwords don't match" }))
            } else {
                setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, confirmPassword: undefined }))
            }
        }
    }

    const validateForm = () => {
        const newErrors = {}

        if (!formData.password) {
            newErrors.password = "Password is required"
        } else if (passwordStrength < 3) {
            newErrors.password = "Password is too weak"
        }

        if (!formData.confirmPassword) {
            newErrors.confirmPassword = "Please confirm your password"
        } else if (formData.password !== formData.confirmPassword) {
            newErrors.confirmPassword = "Passwords don't match"
        }

        setErrors(newErrors)
        return Object.keys(newErrors).length === 0
    }
    // const [resetPass] = useResetPassMutation() // Replaced by resetPassword hook instance

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!validateForm()) return;

        try {
            const payload = { email, password: formData.password };
            // If your backend's /auth/reset-password needs the OTP or a verification token again,
            // it must be included in the payload. Assuming for now it's handled by backend session/context
            // established after OTP verification.
            const res = await resetPassword(payload).unwrap();

            toast.success(res?.message || "Password reset successfully! Please login with your new password.");
            sessionStorage.removeItem("otpVerified"); // Clear the OTP flag specifically
            navigate("/"); // Navigate to login page

        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || err.message || "Failed to reset password. Please try again.");
            setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, submit: err.data?.message || "Password reset failed." }));
        }
    };

    const getPasswordStrengthColor = () => {
        if (passwordStrength <= 2) return "bg-red-500"
        if (passwordStrength <= 3) return "bg-yellow-500"
        return "bg-green-500"
    }

    const getPasswordStrengthText = () => {
        if (passwordStrength <= 2) return "Weak"
        if (passwordStrength <= 3) return "Medium"
        return "Strong"
    }

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-50 to-gray-100 py-12 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="max-w-md w-full space-y-8">
                <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-8">
                    {/* Header */}
                    <div className="text-center mb-8">
                        <div className="mx-auto h-16 w-16 rounded-xl bg-[#3D72FA]/10 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                            <Lock className="h-8 w-8 text-[#3D72FA]" />
                        </div>
                        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900 font-inter">Reset Password</h2>
                        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">Create a new secure password for your account</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                            Resetting password for: <span className="font-medium">{email}</span>
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    {/* Form */}
                    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
                        {/* New Password */}
                        <Input
                            id="password"
                            name="password"
                            type="password"
                            label="New Password"
                            value={formData.password}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            placeholder="Enter your new password"
                            leftIcon={<Lock className="size-4" />}
                            showPasswordToggle
                            required
                            error={errors.password}
                        />

                        {/* Password Strength Indicator */}
                        {formData.password && (
                            <div className="space-y-3">
                                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                                    <div className="flex-1 bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2">
                                        <div
                                            className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${getPasswordStrengthColor()}`}
                                            style={{ width: `${(passwordStrength / 5) * 100}%` }}
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                    <span className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-600 min-w-[60px]">{getPasswordStrengthText()}</span>
                                </div>

                                {/* Password Requirements */}
                                <div className="space-y-2">
                                    <p className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">Password must contain:</p>
                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-1">
                                        {passwordRequirements.map((req, index) => {
                                            const isValid = req.test(formData.password)
                                            return (
                                                <div key={index} className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                                                    <CheckCircle className={`size-3 ${isValid ? "text-green-500" : "text-gray-300"}`} />
                                                    <span className={`text-xs ${isValid ? "text-green-600" : "text-gray-500"}`}>{req.text}</span>
                                                </div>
                                            )
                                        })}
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {/* Confirm Password */}
                        <Input
                            id="confirmPassword"
                            name="confirmPassword"
                            type="password"
                            label="Confirm New Password"
                            value={formData.confirmPassword}
                            onChange={handleChange}
                            placeholder="Confirm your new password"
                            leftIcon={<Lock className="size-4" />}
                            showPasswordToggle
                            required
                            error={errors.confirmPassword}
                        />

                        {/* Submit Error */}
                        {errors.submit && (
                            <div className="text-sm text-red-600 text-center bg-red-50 p-3 rounded-lg">{errors.submit}</div>
                        )}

                        {/* Action Buttons */}
                        <div className="space-y-4">
                            <Button
                                type="submit"
                                disabled={isResettingPassword || passwordStrength < 3 || formData.password !== formData.confirmPassword}
                                isLoading={isResettingPassword}
                                fullWidth
                                icon={<ArrowRight className="size-4" />}
                                iconPosition="right"
                            >
                                {isResettingPassword ? "Resetting Password..." : "Reset Password"}
                            </Button>

                            <Button onClick={() => { sessionStorage.removeItem("otpVerified"); navigate("/"); }} variant="secondary" fullWidth icon={<ArrowLeft className="size-4" />}>
                                Back to Login
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </form>

                    {/* Security Notice */}
                    <div className="mt-6 p-4 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg">
                        <div className="flex items-start space-x-3">
                            <Shield className="size-5 text-blue-600 mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
                            <div>
                                <h4 className="text-sm font-medium text-blue-900">Security Tips</h4>
                                <ul className="mt-1 text-xs text-blue-700 space-y-1">
                                    <li>• Use a unique password you haven't used before</li>
                                    <li>• Consider using a password manager</li>
                                    <li>• Don't share your password with anyone</li>
                                </ul>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}

export default ResetPassword

import React, { useState } from 'react'
import { Button, Card, Input } from '../../components/components'
import { Mail } from 'lucide-react'
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'; // Changed to react-router-dom
import { useSendOtpMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/authApiSlice.js'; // Changed path to authSlice.js
// import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper'; // Removed makeRequest


export default function SendOtp() {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState("")
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const [sendOtp, { isLoading }] = useSendOtpMutation(); // Destructure isLoading

    const handleSendOtp = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (email.trim() === '' || !email) { // Simplified condition
            toast.error("Enter your email"); // Changed to toast.error for errors
            return;
        }
        try {
            const res = await sendOtp({ email }).unwrap();
            toast.success(res?.message || "OTP sent successfully! Please check your email.");
            navigate(`/forgot-password/verify-otp/${email}`);
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || err.message || "Failed to send OTP. Please try again.");
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-50 to-gray-100 py-12 px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            <div className="max-w-md w-full space-y-8">
                <form onSubmit={handleSendOtp} className=''>
                    <Card className='w-full'>
                        <Input
                            label={"Enter Your Email"}
                            type="email"
                            placeholder={"xyz@gmail.com"}
                            leftIcon={<Mail />}
                            required
                            onChange={(e) => { setEmail(e.target.value) }}
                            value={email}
                        />
                        <Button type={"submit"} className={"w-full mt-5"} isLoading={isLoading} disabled={isLoading}>
                            {isLoading ? "Sending..." : "Send Otp"}
                        </Button>
                    </Card>

                </form>

            </div>
        </div>
    )
}
