import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { Card, Button, Select } from '../../components/components';
import ModalWrapper from '../../components/ModalWrapper/ModalWrapper';
import { Plus, Eye, Trash2, Clock, X, User } from 'lucide-react';
import {
    useGetStoriesQuery,
    useCreateStoryMutation,
    useViewStoryMutation,
    useDeleteStoryMutation
} from '../../_core/Slices/storySlice';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission';

const Stories = () => {
    const { userInfo } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
    const [duration, setDuration] = useState(7);
    const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
    const [showUploadModal, setShowUploadModal] = useState(false);
    const [viewedStory, setViewedStory] = useState(null);
    const [showViewModal, setShowViewModal] = useState(false);

    // RTK Query hooks
    const { data: stories = [], refetch, isLoading } = useGetStoriesQuery();
    const [createStory, { isLoading: isCreatingStory }] = useCreateStoryMutation();
    const [viewStory] = useViewStoryMutation();
    const [deleteStory, { isLoading: isDeletingStory }] = useDeleteStoryMutation();


    const getVideoDuration = (file) => {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const video = document.createElement('video');

            video.preload = 'metadata';
            video.src = URL.createObjectURL(file);

            video.onloadedmetadata = () => {
                URL.revokeObjectURL(video.src); // Clean up
                resolve(video.duration); // Duration in seconds
            };

            video.onerror = () => {
                reject('Invalid video file');
            };
        });
    };
    // Handle file selection

    const handleFileChange = async (e) => {
        setFile(e.target.files?.[0]);
        if (e.target.files?.[0]) {
            const fileType = e.target.files[0].type;
            if (fileType.startsWith('image/')) {
                setDuration(7); // Default duration for images
            } else if (fileType.startsWith('video/')) {
                const videoDuration = await getVideoDuration(e.target.files[0]);
                setDuration(videoDuration); // Default duration for videos
            }
        }
    };
    console.log(duration)
    // Handle form submission
    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!file) return;

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('mediaFile', file);
        if (file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            formData.append('duration', duration);

        } else {
            formData.append("duration", await getVideoDuration(file))
        }
        formData.append('type', file.type.startsWith('image/') ? 'image' : 'video');

        try {
            await createStory(formData).unwrap();
            refetch();
            setShowUploadModal(false);
            setFile(null);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error uploading story:', error);
        }
    };
    console.log(file)
    // Handle view story
    const handleViewStory = async (storyItem) => {
        try {
            const response = await viewStory(storyItem._id).unwrap();
            setViewedStory(response.story);
            setShowViewModal(true);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error viewing story:', error);
        }
    };

    // Handle delete story
    const handleDelete = async (itemId) => {
        try {
            await deleteStory(itemId).unwrap();
            refetch();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error deleting story:', error);
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="p-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto">
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-start md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
                <div>
                    <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-800">Stories Dashboard</h1>
                    <p className="text-gray-500 mt-1">Manage and view all user stories</p>
                </div>
                <Button
                    onClick={() => setShowUploadModal(true)}
                    icon={<Plus size={18} />}
                    className="w-full md:w-auto"
                >
                    Create New Story
                </Button>
            </div>

            {/* Stories Grid */}
            <div className="space-y-6">
                {stories.map((story) => (
                    <Card key={story._id} className="overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                        <div className="p-5 flex items-center space-x-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-50 to-white">
                            <div className="relative">
                                <img
                                    src={story.userId.avatar?.url || '/default-avatar.jpg'}
                                    alt={story.userId.fullName}
                                    className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full border-2 border-white shadow"
                                />
                                <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3 h-3 bg-green-500 rounded-full border-2 border-white"></span>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <h3 className="font-semibold text-gray-800">{story.userId.fullName}</h3>
                                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">@{story.userId.username}</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="p-5 border-t border-text-light">
                            <div className="flex space-x-4 pb-3 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide">
                                {story.items.map((item) => (
                                    <div
                                        key={item._id}
                                        className="relative flex-shrink-0 group"
                                    >
                                        <div className="relative w-32 h-48 rounded-lg overflow-hidden shadow">
                                            {item.type === 'image' ? (
                                                <img
                                                    src={item.media.url}
                                                    alt="Story"
                                                    className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform group-hover:scale-105"
                                                />
                                            ) : (
                                                <video
                                                    src={item.media.url}
                                                    className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                                                />
                                            )}
                                            <div className={`absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/60 to-transparent flex flex-col justify-between p-3 ${isDeletingStory && "opacity-70"}`}>
                                                <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                                                    <span className="bg-black/50 text-white text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
                                                        {item.duration.toFixed(0)}s
                                                    </span>
                                                    {hasPermission("admin") && (
                                                        <button
                                                            disabled={isDeletingStory}
                                                            onClick={(e) => {
                                                                e.stopPropagation();
                                                                handleDelete(item._id);
                                                            }}
                                                            className="bg-red-500 text-white p-1 rounded-full hover:bg-red-600 transition-colors"
                                                        >
                                                            <Trash2 size={14} />
                                                        </button>
                                                    )}
                                                </div>
                                                <Button
                                                    variant="outline"
                                                    size="sm"
                                                    onClick={() => handleViewStory(item)}
                                                    icon={<Eye size={14} />}
                                                    className="w-full bg-white/90 hover:bg-white truncate"
                                                >
                                                    View Story
                                                </Button>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                ))}
                            </div>

                            <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-3 border-t border-text-light">
                                <span className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                                    {story.items.length} {story.items.length === 1 ? 'story' : 'stories'}
                                </span>
                                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                                    <User size={16} className="text-gray-400" />
                                    <span className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                                        {story.items.reduce((acc, item) => acc + (item.viewers?.length || 0), 0)} views
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                ))}
            </div>

            {/* Upload Story Modal */}
            <ModalWrapper
                isOpen={showUploadModal}
                onRequestClose={() => setShowUploadModal(false)}
                title="Create New Story"
                size="lg"
            >
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
                    <div
                        className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-8 text-center cursor-pointer transition-colors ${file ? 'border-primary bg-primary/5' : 'border-gray-300 hover:border-gray-400'
                            }`}
                        onClick={() => fileInputRef.current.click()}
                    >
                        {file ? (
                            <div className="space-y-2">
                                {file.type.startsWith('image/') ? (
                                    <img
                                        src={URL.createObjectURL(file)}
                                        alt="Preview"
                                        className="mx-auto max-h-40 rounded-lg shadow"
                                    />
                                ) : (
                                    <video
                                        src={URL.createObjectURL(file)}
                                        controls
                                        className="mx-auto max-h-40 rounded-lg shadow"
                                    />
                                )}
                                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mt-2">{file.name}</p>
                            </div>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="space-y-3">
                                <div className="mx-auto w-12 h-12 bg-gray-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                                    <Plus className="text-gray-400" />
                                </div>
                                <p className="text-gray-600 font-medium">Click to upload media</p>
                                <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Supports images and videos</p>
                            </div>
                        )}
                        <input
                            type="file"
                            ref={fileInputRef}
                            onChange={handleFileChange}
                            accept="image/*,video/*"
                            className="hidden"
                            required
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                        <Select
                            label="Story Duration"
                            disabled={!file?.type.startsWith('image/')}
                            value={duration}

                            onChange={(e) => setDuration(e.target.value)}
                            options={[
                                { value: 5, label: '5 seconds' },
                                { value: 7, label: '7 seconds' },
                                { value: 10, label: '10 seconds' },
                                !file?.type.startsWith('image/') ? { value: duration, label: `${duration?.toFixed()} seconds` } : { value: 15, label: '15 seconds' }
                                ,
                            ]}
                            leftIcon={<Clock size={16} />}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-end space-x-3 pt-2">
                        {/* <Button
                            variant="outline"
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => {
                                setShowUploadModal(false);
                                setFile(null);
                            }}
                        >
                            Cancel
                        </Button> */}
                        <Button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={!file || isCreatingStory}
                            icon={<Plus size={16} />}
                            className="min-w-[120px]"
                        >
                            {file ? (isCreatingStory ? 'uploading...' : "Upload Story") : 'Select File'}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </ModalWrapper>

            {/* View Story Modal */}
            {showViewModal && viewedStory && (
                <ModalWrapper
                    isOpen={showViewModal}
                    onRequestClose={() => setShowViewModal(false)}
                    title="Story Details"
                    size="xl"
                    noPadding
                >
                    <div className="relative">
                        {viewedStory.type === 'image' ? (
                            <img
                                src={viewedStory.media.url}
                                alt="Story"
                                className="w-full max-h-[70vh] object-contain rounded-t-lg"
                            />
                        ) : (
                            <video
                                src={viewedStory.media.url}
                                controls
                                autoPlay
                                className="w-full max-h-[70vh] object-contain rounded-t-lg"
                            />
                        )}

                        <div className="p-6 space-y-4">
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                                <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-lg">
                                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 mb-1">Duration</p>
                                    <p className="text-lg font-semibold ">{viewedStory.duration} seconds</p>
                                </div>
                                <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-lg">
                                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500 mb-1">Viewers</p>
                                    <p className="text-lg font-semibold">{viewedStory.viewers?.length || 0}</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </ModalWrapper>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

export default Stories;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\UploadVideo\UploadVideo.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import {
    useGenerateVideoSignatureMutation,
    useUploadVideoToCloudinaryMutation,
    useSaveVideoDetailsMutation,
    useCreateVideoFromUrlMutation
} from '../../_core/Slices/videoSlice';
import {
    Heading,
    Button,
    Card,
    Input,
    Select,
    Textarea,
} from '../../components/components';
import { ArrowLeft, UploadCloud, Film, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { Box, Typography } from '@mui/material';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission';
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';



const UploadVideo = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();


    // --- State Management ---
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
        title: '',
        description: '',
        status: 'published',
        tags: [],
    });
    const [currentTag, setCurrentTag] = useState(''); // **NEW**: State for the tag input field
    const [uploadMethod, setUploadMethod] = useState(null); // **NEW**: State for the tag input field
    const [videoFile, setVideoFile] = useState(null);
    const [videoUrl, setVideoUrl] = useState(null);

    const [generateSignature, { isLoading: isGeneratingSignature }] = useGenerateVideoSignatureMutation();
    const [uploadToCloudinary, { isLoading: isUploading }] = useUploadVideoToCloudinaryMutation();
    const [saveDetails, { isLoading: isSaving }] = useSaveVideoDetailsMutation();

    const [createVideoFromUrl, { isLoading: isUrlUploading }] = useCreateVideoFromUrlMutation()

    const [videoPreviewUrl, setVideoPreviewUrl] = useState(null);
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null);
    const isLoading = isGeneratingSignature || isUploading || isSaving;
    // --- Event Handlers ---
    const handleInputChange = (e) => {
        const { name, value } = e.target;
        setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    };

    const handleFileChange = (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (file) {
            if (!file.type.startsWith('video/')) {
                toast.error('Please select a valid video file.');
                setVideoFile(null);
                setVideoPreviewUrl(null);
                if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = "";
                return;
            }
            setVideoFile(file);
            // Create a temporary URL for instant local preview
            const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
            setVideoPreviewUrl(objectUrl);
        } else {
            setVideoFile(null);
            setVideoPreviewUrl(null);
        }
    };

    // **NEW**: Handler for adding a tag
    const handleTagKeyDown = (e) => {
        if (e.key !== 'Enter' || !currentTag.trim()) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        const newTag = currentTag.trim();
        if (newTag && !formData.tags.includes(newTag)) {
            setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, tags: [...prev.tags, newTag] }));
        }
        setCurrentTag(''); // Reset the input field
    };

    // **NEW**: Handler for removing a tag
    const removeTag = (tagToRemove) => {
        setFormData(prev => ({
            ...prev,
            tags: prev.tags.filter(tag => tag !== tagToRemove),
        }));
    };

    // --- Submission Logic (Kept as provided, with tags array handling) ---
    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!videoFile) {
            toast.error("Please select a video file to upload.");
            return;
        }
        if (!formData.title.trim()) {
            toast.error("Title is required.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            // Step 1: Get signature from our backend
            toast.info('Step 1/3: Getting upload permissions...');
            const signatureData = await generateSignature().unwrap();

            // Step 2: Upload directly to Cloudinary
            toast.info('Step 2/3: Uploading video... This may take a while.');
            const cloudinaryResponse = await uploadToCloudinary({
                file: videoFile,
                signatureData
            }).unwrap();

            // Step 3: Save details to our database
            toast.info('Step 3/3: Finalizing and saving video details...');
            await saveDetails({
                title: formData.title,
                description: formData.description,
                tags: formData.tags,
                cloudinaryResponse, // Cloudinary se mila poora response bhej dein
            }).unwrap();

            toast.success('Video uploaded successfully!');
            navigate('/dashboard/videos');

        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Upload failed:', error);
            const errorMessage = error?.data?.message || 'An unexpected error occurred during upload.';
            toast.error(`Upload Failed: ${errorMessage}`);
        }
    };

    const handleSubmitUrl = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!formData.title.trim()) {
            toast.error("Title is required.");
            return;
        }
        const payload = {
            title: formData.title,
            description: formData.description,
            tags: formData.tags,
            videoUrl
        }
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: createVideoFromUrl,
            data: payload,
            onSuccess: () => {
                toast.success('Video URL uploaded successfully!');
                navigate('/dashboard/videos');
            }
        })

    }


    // --- Render Logic ---
    const userCanUpload = hasPermission('editor');

    if (!userCanUpload) {
        return (
            <Box sx={{ p: 3, textAlign: 'center' }}>
                <Typography variant="h6" color="error">Access Denied</Typography>
                <Typography>You do not have permission to upload videos.</Typography>
                <Button onClick={() => navigate("/dashboard")} className="mt-4">Back to Dashboard</Button>
            </Box>
        );
    }

    const statusOptions = [
        { value: 'published', label: 'Published' },
        { value: 'private', label: 'Private' },
    ];

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Upload New Video"
                actions={<Button variant="outline" onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/videos')} icon={<ArrowLeft className="size-4" />}>Back to Videos</Button>}
            />

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                <div className="lg:col-span-2">
                    <Card>
                        <form onSubmit={uploadMethod == "file" ? handleSubmit : handleSubmitUrl} className="p-6 space-y-6">
                            <Input name="title" label="Video Title" value={formData.title} onChange={handleInputChange} placeholder="Enter a descriptive title for your video" required disabled={isUploading} />
                            <Textarea name="description" label="Description" value={formData.description} onChange={handleInputChange} placeholder="Briefly describe the video's content" rows={4} disabled={isUploading} />

                            {/* **CHANGED**: Tag Input UI */}
                            <div>
                                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Tags (Optional)</label>
                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg min-h-[44px]">
                                    {formData.tags.map((tag, index) => (
                                        <span key={index} className="flex items-center bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 text-sm font-medium pl-2.5 pr-1 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                                            {tag}
                                            <button type="button" onClick={() => removeTag(tag)} className="ml-1.5 p-0.5 rounded-full hover:bg-blue-200" disabled={isUploading}>
                                                <X className="size-3" />
                                            </button>
                                        </span>
                                    ))}
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={currentTag}
                                        onChange={(e) => setCurrentTag(e.target.value)}
                                        onKeyDown={handleTagKeyDown}
                                        className="flex-1 min-w-[120px] p-1 outline-none border-none bg-transparent placeholder-gray-500 text-sm"
                                        placeholder="Add tag and press Enter"
                                        disabled={isUploading}
                                    />
                                </div>
                            </div>


                            <Select name="status" label="Initial Status" value={formData.status} onChange={handleInputChange} options={statusOptions} searchable={false} disabled={isUploading} />

                            <div className="flex justify-end pt-4 border-t">
                                {uploadMethod == "file" ?
                                    <Button type="submit" disabled={isLoading || !videoFile} isLoading={isLoading}>
                                        {isLoading ? 'Uploading...' : 'Upload & Process Video'}
                                    </Button> :
                                    <Button type="submit" disabled={isLoading || !videoUrl} isLoading={isUrlUploading}>
                                        {isLoading ? 'Uploading...' : 'Upload & Process Video'}
                                    </Button>}
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </Card>
                </div>

                <div className="lg:col-span-1 space-y-6">
                    <Select
                        name="method" label="Upload method" value={uploadMethod} onChange={(e) => setUploadMethod(e.target.value)} options={[{ value: "file", label: "File" }, { value: "url", label: "Url" }]} disabled={isUploading}
                    />
                    {uploadMethod &&
                        <>
                            {uploadMethod == "file" ? <Card>
                                <div className="p-6">
                                    <Heading level={3} title="Video File" />
                                    <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center px-6 pt-5 pb-6 border-2 border-gray-300 border-dashed rounded-md">
                                        <div className="space-y-1 text-center">
                                            <UploadCloud className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-gray-400" />
                                            <div className="flex text-sm text-gray-600">
                                                <label htmlFor="file-upload" className="relative cursor-pointer bg-white rounded-md font-medium text-primary hover:text-primary-focus focus-within:outline-none">
                                                    <span>Select a file</span>
                                                    <input id="file-upload" name="videoFile" type="file" className="sr-only" ref={fileInputRef} onChange={handleFileChange} accept="video/*" required />
                                                </label>
                                                <p className="pl-1">or drag and drop</p>
                                            </div>
                                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">MP4, WebM, OGG up to 50MB</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    {videoPreviewUrl ? (
                                        <div className="mt-4">
                                            <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-2">Preview:</p>
                                            <video key={videoPreviewUrl} controls src={videoPreviewUrl} className="w-full rounded-md shadow-md"></video>
                                            {videoFile && <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">File: {videoFile.name}</p>}
                                        </div>
                                    ) : (
                                        <div className="mt-4 text-center text-gray-500">
                                            <Film className="mx-auto h-12 w-12" />
                                            <p className="mt-2 text-sm">No video selected</p>
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            </Card> :
                                <Input name="url" label="Video Url" value={videoUrl} onChange={(e) => setVideoUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="Paste a Video Url" disabled={isUploading} />
                            }
                        </>
                    }
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default UploadVideo;

"use client";
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { toast } from "react-toastify";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";
import debounce from 'lodash.debounce';

// --- UI Component Imports ---
import { Heading, Button, Card, Input, Select, MuiTable } from "../../components/components.js";
import ModalWrapper from "../../components/ModalWrapper/ModalWrapper";
import ConfirmModal from "../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal";
import CircularProgressBox from "../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox";
import { Box, Typography, Chip, Avatar, Tooltip, IconButton, CircularProgress, Alert } from "@mui/material";
import { Search, Plus, Edit, Trash2, User as UserIcon, BookOpen } from "lucide-react";

// --- RTK Query & Helper Imports ---
import { useGetUsersQuery, useCreateUserMutation, useDeleteUserMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/userSlice.js";
import { hasPermission } from "../../Helper/hasPermission.js";
import { formatDate } from "../../utils/formatters.js";
import { USER_ROLE_OPTIONS } from "../../constants/userConstants.js";
import { makeRequest } from "../../Helper/ApiHelper.js";
import getRole from "../../Helper/getRole.js";

// =================================================================================
// == Create User Form Component (Self-contained and unchanged)
// =================================================================================
const CreateUserForm = ({ onClose, onSuccess, createUserMutation, isCreatingUser }) => {
  // This component remains the same.
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ fullName: "", email: "", username: "", password: "", role: "reader" });
  const { userInfo: currentUser } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
  const assignableRoles = [{ value: "reader", label: "Reader" }, { value: "author", label: "Author" }, { value: "editor", label: "Editor" }, ...(hasPermission(currentUser?.user?.role, 'superadmin') ? [{ value: "admin", label: "Admin" }] : [])];
  const handleChange = ({ target: { name, value } }) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
  const handleSubmit = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); makeRequest({ apiFunc: createUserMutation, data: formData, toast, onSuccess }) };
  return (
    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-4">
      <Heading level={2} title="Create New User" />
      <Input name="fullName" label="Full Name" value={formData.fullName} onChange={handleChange} required />
      <Input name="email" type="email" label="Email Address" value={formData.email} onChange={handleChange} required />
      <Input name="username" label="Username" value={formData.username} onChange={handleChange} required />
      <Input name="password" type="password" label="Password" value={formData.password} onChange={handleChange} required />
      <Select name="role" label="Role" value={formData.role} onChange={handleChange} options={assignableRoles} required />
      <Box className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-4 border-t border-gray-200"><Button variant="outline" onClick={onClose} type="button" disabled={isCreatingUser}>Cancel</Button><Button type="submit" isLoading={isCreatingUser} disabled={isCreatingUser}>Create User</Button></Box>
    </form>
  );
};

// =================================================================================
// == Main User Management Page Component
// =================================================================================
const UserManagement = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { userInfo: currentUser } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
  const currentUserRole = getRole()
  // --- State for Filters and Pagination ---
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(10);
  const [role, setRole] = useState("");
  const [sortBy] = useState("createdAt"); // sortBy and sortOrder can be added back if needed
  const [sortOrder] = useState("desc");

  // **MODIFIED**: State for debounced search
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  const [debouncedSearchTerm, setDebouncedSearchTerm] = useState("");

  // --- Modal State ---
  const [isCreateModalOpen, setIsCreateModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isDeleteConfirmOpen, setIsDeleteConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
  const [userToDelete, setUserToDelete] = useState(null);

  // --- RTK Query Hooks ---
  const { data: usersData, isLoading, isFetching, isError, error } = useGetUsersQuery(
    // The API call uses the debounced term
    { page, limit, searchQuery: debouncedSearchTerm, sortBy, sortOrder }
  );
  const [createUser, { isLoading: isCreatingUser }] = useCreateUserMutation();
  const [deleteUser, { isLoading: isDeletingUser }] = useDeleteUserMutation();

  // --- Debounce search term ---
  const debouncedSearch = useMemo(
    () => debounce((value) => {
      setDebouncedSearchTerm(value);
      setPage(1); // Reset to first page on new search
    }, 500), // 500ms delay
    []
  );
  useEffect(() => {
    debouncedSearch(searchTerm);
    return () => debouncedSearch.cancel();
  }, [searchTerm, debouncedSearch]);

  // --- Handlers ---
  const handleRoleChange = (e) => { setRole(e.target.value); setPage(1); };
  const handleMuiPageChange = (event, newPage) => setPage(newPage + 1);
  const handleMuiLimitChange = (event) => { setLimit(parseInt(event.target.value, 10)); setPage(1); };
  const handleEditUser = (userId) => navigate(`/dashboard/users/edit/${userId}`);
  const handleDeleteUserClick = (user) => {
    if (currentUser?.user._id === user._id) { toast.error("You cannot delete yourself."); return; }
    setUserToDelete(user);
    setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(true);
  };
  const confirmUserDelete = () => {
    if (!userToDelete) return;
    makeRequest({ apiFunc: deleteUser, data: userToDelete._id, toast, onSuccess: () => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false) });
  };

  const columns = [
    { header: "User", field: "fullName", render: (v, row) => <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 2 }}><Avatar src={row.avatar?.url} /><div><Typography variant="body2" fontWeight="bold">{row.fullName}</Typography><Typography variant="caption" color="text.secondary">{row.email}</Typography></div></Box> },
    { header: "Role", field: "role", render: (role) => <Chip label={role} size="small" sx={{ textTransform: 'capitalize' }} /> },
    { header: "Joined Date", field: "createdAt", render: (date) => formatDate(date) },
    {
      header: "Actions", field: "actions", align: "right", render: (v, row) => (
        hasPermission('admin') && row.role !== currentUserRole && row.role !== "superadmin" && (
          <Box>
            {row.role !== "reader" && <Tooltip title="View Articles">
              <IconButton size="small">
                <Link to={`/dashboard/articles-by-author/${row._id}`}>
                  <BookOpen size={16} />
                </Link>
              </IconButton>
            </Tooltip>}
            <Tooltip title="Edit">
              <IconButton onClick={() => handleEditUser(row._id)} size="small">
                <Edit size={16} />
              </IconButton>
            </Tooltip>
            <Tooltip title="Delete">
              <IconButton onClick={() => handleDeleteUserClick(row)} size="small" color="error">
                <Trash2 size={16} />
              </IconButton>
            </Tooltip>
          </Box>
        )
      )
    }
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <ModalWrapper isOpen={isCreateModalOpen} onRequestClose={() => setIsCreateModalOpen(false)} title="Create New User">
        <CreateUserForm onClose={() => setIsCreateModalOpen(false)} onSuccess={() => setIsCreateModalOpen(false)} createUserMutation={createUser} isCreatingUser={isCreatingUser} />
      </ModalWrapper>
      <ConfirmModal isOpen={isDeleteConfirmOpen} onClose={() => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false)} onConfirm={confirmUserDelete} title="Confirm Deletion" isConfirmLoading={isDeletingUser} confirmButtonVariant="danger">
        <p>Are you sure you want to delete <strong>{userToDelete?.fullName}</strong>? This action is permanent.</p>
      </ConfirmModal>

      <Heading level={1} title="User Management" description={`Manage all ${usersData?.totalUsers || 0} users in the system`} actions={
        hasPermission('admin') && <Button onClick={() => setIsCreateModalOpen(true)} icon={<Plus className="size-4" />}>New User</Button>
      } />

      <Card>
        <div className="p-4 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <Input placeholder="Search by name, username or email..." value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)} leftIcon={<Search className="size-4" />} />
          </div>
          {/* <Select value={role} onChange={handleRoleChange} options={USER_ROLE_OPTIONS} placeholder="Filter by role" /> */}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {isLoading ? <CircularProgressBox text="Loading users..." /> : isError ? (
        <Alert severity="error">{getUsersError?.data?.message || 'Failed to load users.'}</Alert>
      ) : (
        <MuiTable
          title="All Users"
          columns={columns}
          rows={usersData?.users || []}
          isLoading={isFetching}
          pagination
          page={page - 1}
          rowsPerPage={limit}
          totalRows={usersData?.totalUsers || 0}
          onPageChange={handleMuiPageChange}
          onRowsPerPageChange={handleMuiLimitChange}
          showSearch={false}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default UserManagement;

--- END OF FILE: src\Pages\UserManagement\UserManagement.jsx ---

"use client";
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams, Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { toast } from "react-toastify";
import { useSelector } from "react-redux";
import debounce from 'lodash.debounce';

// --- UI Component Imports ---
import { Heading, Button, Card, Input, Select, MuiTable } from "../../components/components.js";
import ModalWrapper from "../../components/ModalWrapper/ModalWrapper";
import ConfirmModal from "../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal";
import { Box, Typography, Chip, Avatar, Tooltip, IconButton, CircularProgress, Alert } from "@mui/material";
import { Search, Plus, Edit, Trash2, UserCheck, Shield, User as UserIcon, Eye, BookOpen } from "lucide-react";

// --- RTK Query & Helper Imports ---
import { useGetUsersQuery, useCreateUserMutation, useDeleteUserMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/userSlice.js";
import { hasPermission } from "../../Helper/hasPermission.js";
import { formatDate } from "../../utils/formatters.js";
import { USER_ROLE_OPTIONS } from "../../constants/userConstants.js";
import { makeRequest } from "../../Helper/ApiHelper.js";
import getRole from "../../Helper/getRole.js";


// =================================================================================
// == Create User Form Component (No changes from previous version)
// =================================================================================
const CreateUserForm = ({ onClose, onSuccess, createUserMutation, isCreatingUser }) => {
    // This component remains exactly the same
    const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ fullName: "", email: "", username: "", password: "", role: "reader" });
    const { userInfo: currentUser } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
    const assignableRoles = [
        { value: "reader", label: "Reader" }, { value: "author", label: "Author" }, { value: "editor", label: "Editor" },
        ...(hasPermission('superadmin') ? [{ value: "admin", label: "Admin" }] : [])
    ];
    const handleChange = ({ target: { name, value } }) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
    const handleSubmit = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); makeRequest({ apiFunc: createUserMutation, data: formData, toast, onSuccess }) };

    return (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-4">
            <Heading level={2} title="Create a New User" description="Fill in the details to add a new user." />
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <Input name="fullName" label="Full Name" value={formData.fullName} onChange={handleChange} required />
                <Input name="username" label="Username" value={formData.username} onChange={handleChange} required />
            </div>
            <Input name="email" type="email" label="Email Address" value={formData.email} onChange={handleChange} required />
            <Input name="password" type="password" label="Password" value={formData.password} onChange={handleChange} required />
            <Select name="role" label="Role" value={formData.role} onChange={handleChange} options={assignableRoles} required />
            <Box className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-4 border-t">
                <Button variant="outline" onClick={onClose} type="button" disabled={isCreatingUser}>Cancel</Button>
                <Button type="submit" isLoading={isCreatingUser} disabled={isCreatingUser}>Create User</Button>
            </Box>
        </form>
    );
};

// =================================================================================
// == Main User Management Page Component
// =================================================================================
const UserPage = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { role: roleFromParams } = useParams(); // Get role from URL
    const { userInfo: currentUser } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);
    if (!hasPermission("superadmin") && roleFromParams == "admin") {
        navigate(-1)
    }
    // --- State Management for Filtering & Pagination ---
    const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
    const [limit, setLimit] = useState(10);
    const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
    const [debouncedSearchTerm, setDebouncedSearchTerm] = useState("");

    // --- Modal State ---
    const [isCreateModalOpen, setIsCreateModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [isDeleteConfirmOpen, setIsDeleteConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
    const [userToDelete, setUserToDelete] = useState(null);

    // --- Debounce search input ---
    const debouncedSearch = useMemo(
        () => debounce((value) => {
            setDebouncedSearchTerm(value);
            setPage(1); // Reset to first page on new search
        }, 500), // 500ms delay
        []
    );
    useEffect(() => {
        debouncedSearch(searchTerm);
        return () => debouncedSearch.cancel();
    }, [searchTerm, debouncedSearch]);


    // --- RTK Query Hooks ---
    const {
        data: usersData,
        isLoading: isLoadingUsers,
        isFetching,
        isError: isGetUsersError,
        error: getUsersError,
    } = useGetUsersQuery(
        // Pass all params to the API
        { page, limit, role: roleFromParams, searchQuery: debouncedSearchTerm },
        { refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true }
    );
    const [createUser, { isLoading: isCreatingUser }] = useCreateUserMutation();
    const [deleteUser, { isLoading: isDeletingUser }] = useDeleteUserMutation();

    const usersToRender = usersData?.users || [];
    const totalUsers = usersData?.totalUsers || 0;

    // --- Handlers ---
    const handleMuiPageChange = (event, newPage) => setPage(newPage + 1);
    const handleMuiLimitChange = (event) => { setLimit(parseInt(event.target.value, 10)); setPage(1); };
    const handleEditUser = (userId) => navigate(`/dashboard/users/edit/${userId}`);
    const handleDeleteUserClick = (user) => {
        if (currentUser.user._id === user._id) { toast.error("You cannot delete yourself."); return; }
        setUserToDelete(user);
        setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(true);
    };
    const confirmUserDelete = () => {
        if (!userToDelete) return;
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: deleteUser,
            data: userToDelete._id,
            toast: toast,
            onSuccess: () => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false),
            onError: () => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false)
        });
    };

    const getRoleChipProps = (role) => {
        switch (role) {
            case "superadmin": return { color: "error", icon: <Shield sx={{ fontSize: 16 }} /> };
            case "admin": return { color: "warning", icon: <UserCheck sx={{ fontSize: 16 }} /> };
            case "editor": return { color: "primary", icon: <Edit sx={{ fontSize: 16 }} /> };
            default: return { color: "default", icon: <UserIcon sx={{ fontSize: 16 }} /> };
        }
    };

    const columns = [
        { header: "User", field: "fullName", render: (v, row) => (<Box sx={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 2 }}><Avatar src={row.avatar?.url} alt={row.fullName} /><div><Typography variant="body2" fontWeight="bold">{row.fullName}</Typography><Typography variant="caption" color="text.secondary">{row.email}</Typography></div></Box>) },
        {
            header: "Role", field: "role",
            render: (role) => <Chip className="p-2" {...getRoleChipProps(role)} label={role} size="small" sx={{ textTransform: 'capitalize' }} />
        },
        { header: "Joined Date", field: "createdAt", render: (date) => formatDate(date) },
        {
            header: "Actions", field: "actions", align: "right", render: (v, row) => (
                <Box>
                    {row.role !== "reader" && <Tooltip Tooltip title="view articles">
                        <IconButton size="small">
                            <Link to={`/dashboard/articles-by-author/${row._id}`}>
                                <BookOpen size={16} />
                            </Link>
                        </IconButton>
                    </Tooltip>}
                    {hasPermission("admin") && (
                        <>

                            <Tooltip title="Edit User">
                                <IconButton onClick={() => handleEditUser(row._id)} size="small">
                                    <Edit size={16} />
                                </IconButton>
                            </Tooltip>
                            <Tooltip title="Delete User">
                                <IconButton onClick={() => handleDeleteUserClick(row)} size="small" color="error">
                                    <Trash2 size={16} />
                                </IconButton>
                            </Tooltip>
                        </>
                    )}
                </Box >

            )
        },
    ];

    return (
        <div className=" space-y-6">
            <ModalWrapper isOpen={isCreateModalOpen} onRequestClose={() => setIsCreateModalOpen(false)} contentLabel="Create New User Modal">
                <CreateUserForm onClose={() => setIsCreateModalOpen(false)} onSuccess={() => setIsCreateModalOpen(false)} createUserMutation={createUser} isCreatingUser={isCreatingUser} />
            </ModalWrapper>
            <ConfirmModal isOpen={isDeleteConfirmOpen} onRequestClose={() => setIsDeleteConfirmOpen(false)} onConfirm={confirmUserDelete} title="Confirm Deletion" confirmText="Delete" isConfirmLoading={isDeletingUser} variant="danger">
                <p>Are you sure you want to delete the user <strong>{userToDelete?.fullName}</strong>? This action cannot be undone.</p>
            </ConfirmModal>

            <Heading
                level={1} title="User Management"
                description={`Viewing ${roleFromParams ? `${roleFromParams}s` : 'all users'}`}
                actions={<Button onClick={() => setIsCreateModalOpen(true)} icon={<Plus size={16} />} disabled={isLoadingUsers}>New User</Button>}
            />

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                <Card>
                    <div className=" grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                        <div className="md:col-span-2">
                            <Input placeholder="Search by name, username or email..." value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)} leftIcon={<Search className="size-4" />} />
                        </div>
                        {/* <Select value={role} onChange={handleRoleChange} options={USER_ROLE_OPTIONS} placeholder="Filter by role" /> */}
                    </div>
                </Card>

                {isLoadingUsers || isFetching ? (
                    <Box sx={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "center", p: 10 }}><CircularProgress /></Box>
                ) : isGetUsersError ? (
                    <Alert severity="error" sx={{ m: 2 }}>Error: {getUsersError.data?.message || 'Could not load users.'}</Alert>
                ) : (

                    <MuiTable
                        title="All Users"
                        columns={columns}
                        rows={usersToRender}
                        isLoading={isFetching}
                        pagination
                        page={page - 1}
                        rowsPerPage={limit}
                        totalRows={totalUsers || 0}
                        onPageChange={handleMuiPageChange}
                        onRowsPerPageChange={handleMuiLimitChange}
                        showSearch={false}
                    />
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default UserPage;

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\VerifyOtp\VerifyOtp.jsx ---

"use client"

import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from "react"
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom"
import { ArrowRight, ArrowLeft, RefreshCw, Shield, Mail } from "lucide-react"
import { toast } from "react-toastify"
import { useSendOtpMutation, useVerifyOtpMutation } from "../../_core/Slices/authApiSlice.js"; // Changed path
// import { makeRequest } from "../../Helper/ApiHelper" // Removed makeRequest

import { Button } from "../../components/components"
import { makeRequest } from "../../Helper/ApiHelper.js"

const VerifyOtp = () => {
    const [otp, setOtp] = useState(["", "", "", "", "", ""])
    // const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false) // To be replaced by isVerifyingOtp
    // const [isResending, setIsResending] = useState(false) // To be replaced by isResendingOtp
    const [error, setError] = useState("")
    const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(60) // Changed initial time to 60s
    const [canResend, setCanResend] = useState(false)

    const inputRefs = useRef([])
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const { email } = useParams()
    const [sendOtp, { isLoading: isResendingOtp, refetch }] = useSendOtpMutation(); // Use specific loading state
    const [verifyOtp, { isLoading: isVerifyingOtp }] = useVerifyOtpMutation(); // Use specific loading state
    useEffect(() => {
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: sendOtp,
            data: { email },
            toast
        })
    }, [email])

    // Auto-send OTP on mount - Removed as it's typically part of SendOtp page logic or handled by navigation flow

    // Timer countdown
    useEffect(() => {
        if (timeLeft > 0) {
            const timer = setTimeout(() => setTimeLeft((t) => t - 1), 1000)
            return () => clearTimeout(timer)
        } else {
            setCanResend(true)
        }
    }, [timeLeft])

    const formatTime = (seconds) => {
        const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60)
        const secs = seconds % 60
        return `${mins}:${secs.toString().padStart(2, "0")}`
    }

    const handleOtpChange = (index, value) => {
        if (!/^\d*$/.test(value)) return

        const newOtp = [...otp]
        newOtp[index] = value
        setOtp(newOtp)
        setError("")

        if (value && index < 5) inputRefs.current[index + 1]?.focus()

        if (newOtp.every((digit) => digit !== "")) handleVerify(newOtp.join(""))
    }

    const handleKeyDown = (index, e) => {
        if (e.key === "Backspace" && !otp[index] && index > 0) {
            inputRefs.current[index - 1]?.focus()
        }
    }

    const handlePaste = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData("text").replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 6)
        if (pasted.length === 6) {
            const newOtp = pasted.split("")
            setOtp(newOtp)
            setError("")
            handleVerify(pasted)
        }
    }

    const handleVerify = async (otpCode = otp.join("")) => {
        if (otpCode.length !== 6) {
            setError("Please enter all 6 digits")
            return
        }

        // setIsLoading(true) // Replaced by isVerifyingOtp
        setError(""); // Clear local error

        try {
            const res = await verifyOtp({ email, otp: otpCode }).unwrap();
            toast.success(res?.message || "OTP verified successfully!");
            sessionStorage.setItem("otpVerified", "true"); // Ensure value is a string
            navigate(`/`);
        } catch (err) {
            toast.error(err.data?.message || err.message || "OTP verification failed. Please try again.");
            setError(err.data?.message || "Invalid OTP or server error."); // Set local error for display
        }
        // setIsLoading(false) // Replaced by isVerifyingOtp
    }

    const handleResendOtp = async () => {
        // try {
        //     await sendOtp({ email }).unwrap();
        //     toast.success("OTP resent successfully");
        //     setTimeLeft(60); // Reset timer to a more standard 60 seconds
        //     setCanResend(false);
        //     setOtp(["", "", "", "", "", ""]);
        //     if (inputRefs.current[0]) inputRefs.current[0].focus();
        // } catch (err) {
        //     toast.error(err.data?.message || err.message || "Failed to resend OTP.");
        // }
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: sendOtp,
            data: { email },
            toast
        })

    }

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-50 to-gray-100 py-12 px-4">
            <div className="max-w-md w-full space-y-8">
                <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-8">
                    <div className="text-center mb-8">
                        <div className="mx-auto h-16 w-16 rounded-xl bg-[#3D72FA]/10 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
                            <Shield className="h-8 w-8 text-[#3D72FA]" />
                        </div>
                        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">Verify Your Email</h2>
                        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">We've sent a 6-digit verification code to</p>
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center mt-2">
                            <Mail className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-400 mr-2" />
                            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">{email}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mb-6">
                        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-4 text-center">Enter verification code</label>
                        <div className="flex justify-center space-x-3" onPaste={handlePaste}>
                            {otp.map((digit, index) => (
                                <input
                                    key={index}
                                    ref={(el) => (inputRefs.current[index] = el)}
                                    type="text"
                                    inputMode="numeric"
                                    maxLength={1}
                                    value={digit}
                                    onChange={(e) => handleOtpChange(index, e.target.value)}
                                    onKeyDown={(e) => handleKeyDown(index, e)}
                                    className={`w-12 h-12 text-center text-lg font-semibold border-2 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3D72FA] transition-all duration-200 ${error ? "border-red-500 ring-red-500" : "border-gray-300"
                                        } ${digit ? "border-[#3D72FA] bg-[#3D72FA]/5" : ""}`}
                                    disabled={isVerifyingOtp} // Use isVerifyingOtp
                                />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                        {error && <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-red-600 text-center">{error}</p>}
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-center mb-6">
                        {!canResend ? (
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                                Resend Otp in <span className="font-medium text-[#3D72FA]">{formatTime(timeLeft)}</span>
                            </p>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="space-y-2">
                                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">Didn't receive the code?</p>
                                <Button
                                    variant="link"
                                    onClick={handleResendOtp}
                                    disabled={isResendingOtp || !canResend} // Use isResendingOtp
                                    icon={
                                        isResendingOtp ? ( // Use isResendingOtp
                                            <RefreshCw className="size-4 animate-spin" />
                                        ) : (
                                            <RefreshCw className="size-4" />
                                        )
                                    }
                                    className="text-[#3D72FA]"
                                >
                                    {isResendingOtp ? "Sending..." : "Resend Code"} {/* Use isResendingOtp */}
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="space-y-4">
                        <Button
                            onClick={() => handleVerify()}
                            disabled={isVerifyingOtp || otp.some((d) => !d)} // Use isVerifyingOtp
                            isLoading={isVerifyingOtp} // Use isVerifyingOtp
                            fullWidth
                            icon={<ArrowRight className="size-4" />}
                            iconPosition="right"
                        >
                            {isVerifyingOtp ? "Verifying..." : "Verify Code"} {/* Use isVerifyingOtp */}
                        </Button>

                        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => navigate("/")} fullWidth icon={<ArrowLeft className="size-4" />} disabled={isVerifyingOtp || isResendingOtp}>
                            Back to Login
                        </Button>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-6 text-center">
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Having trouble? Check your spam folder or contact support.</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}

export default VerifyOtp

--- START OF FILE: src\Pages\VideoList\VideoList.jsx ---

import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useSelector } from 'react-redux';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';

// --- All necessary imports ---
import { useGetVideosQuery, useDeleteVideoMutation, useToggleVideoHeadlineMutation } from '../../_core/Slices/videoSlice';
import { useVideoFilters } from '../../hooks/useVideoFilters.js';
import { hasPermission } from '../../Helper/hasPermission.js';
import { makeRequest } from '../../Helper/ApiHelper';
import { formatDate } from '../../utils/formatters.js';
import { Heading, Button, Card } from '../../components/components';
import ConfirmModal from '../../components/ConfirmModal/ConfirmModal';
import CircularProgressBox from '../../components/CircularProgressBox/CircularProgressBox';
import CustomTooltip from '../../components/CustomTooltip/CustomTooltip';
import { Edit, Trash2, Plus, Maximize, Eye as EyeIcon, Calendar } from 'lucide-react';

// --- Helper function to parse YouTube URLs ---
const getYouTubeVideoId = (url) => {
    if (!url) return null;
    // This regex handles various YouTube URL formats (watch, youtu.be, embed, etc.)
    const regExp = /^.*(youtu.be\/|v\/|u\/\w\/|embed\/|watch\?v=|&v=)([^#&?]*).*/;
    const match = url.match(regExp);
    console.log(match, "match")
    return (match && match[2].length === 11) ? match[2] : null;
};

// ==========================================================
// == Reusable VideoCard Component for the Admin Dashboard
// ==========================================================
const VideoCard = ({ video, onEdit, onDelete, onActionComplete }) => {
    const videoRef = useRef(null);
    const [toggleHeadline, { isLoading: isToggling }] = useToggleVideoHeadlineMutation();

    const handleToggleHeadline = () => {
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: toggleHeadline,
            data: video._id,
            toast: toast,
            onSuccess: onActionComplete, // Refetch the list on success
            message: `Headline status updated!`
        });
    };

    const handleFullscreen = () => {
        if (videoRef.current?.requestFullscreen) {
            videoRef.current.requestFullscreen();
        } else if (videoRef.current?.mozRequestFullScreen) { // Firefox
            videoRef.current.mozRequestFullScreen();
        } else if (videoRef.current?.webkitRequestFullscreen) { // Chrome, Safari
            videoRef.current.webkitRequestFullscreen();
        }
    };

    const VideoPlayer = () => {
        if (video.sourceType === 'url' && video.videoUrl) {
            const videoId = getYouTubeVideoId(video.videoUrl);
            if (!videoId) {
                return <div className="w-full h-96 bg-black text-white flex items-center justify-center  text-center text-sm">Invalid Video URL</div>;
            }
            return (
                <iframe
                    src={`https://www.youtube.com/embed/${videoId}`}
                    title={video.title}
                    frameBorder="0"
                    allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture"
                    allowFullScreen
                    className="w-full h-full"
                ></iframe>
            );
        }

        if (video.sourceType === 'upload' && video.video?.url) {
            return (
                <video ref={videoRef} className="w-full h-full object-contain" src={video?.video.url} poster={video.thumbnail?.url} controls>
                    Your browser does not support the video tag.
                </video>
            );
        }

        return <div className="w-full h-full bg-gray-200 flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500">No Video Source</div>;
    };

    return (
        <Card padding='none' className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden group">
            <div className="relative bg-black aspect-video h-50">
                <VideoPlayer />
                {video.sourceType === 'upload' && (
                    <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                        <CustomTooltip content="Fullscreen">
                            <Button size="icon" variant="secondary" className="bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/70" onClick={handleFullscreen}><Maximize size={16} /></Button>
                        </CustomTooltip>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
            <div className="p-4 flex-grow flex flex-col">
                <h3 className="font-semibold text-base mb-2 break-words leading-tight  line-clamp-2">{video.title}</h3>
                <div className="mt-auto space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-gray-500">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><EyeIcon size={14} /><span>{video.viewCount || 0} views</span></div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1"><Calendar size={14} /><span>{formatDate(video.createdAt)}</span></div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="pt-3 border-t flex justify-end gap-2">
                        <CustomTooltip content={video.isTopHeadline ? "Remove from Top Headlines" : "Set as Top Headline"}>
                            {/* <Button size="sm" variant={video.isTopHeadline ? 'primary' : 'outline'} onClick={handleToggleHeadline} disabled={isToggling}>
                                <Zap size={14} />
                            </Button> */}
                        </CustomTooltip>
                        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={onEdit} icon={<Edit size={14} />}>Edit</Button>
                        <Button size="sm" variant="dangerOutline" onClick={onDelete} icon={<Trash2 size={14} />}>Delete</Button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </Card>
    );
};

// ===========================================
// == Main VideoList Dashboard Page Component
// ===========================================
const VideoList = () => {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { userInfo } = useSelector((state) => state.auth);

    const { page, limit, handlers, queryParams } = useVideoFilters(9);
    const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [videoToDelete, setVideoToDelete] = useState(null);

    const {
        data: videosData,
        isLoading: isLoadingVideos,
        isError,
        error,
        refetch,
    } = useGetVideosQuery(queryParams, { refetchOnMountOrArgChange: true });

    const [deleteVideo, { isLoading: isDeleting }] = useDeleteVideoMutation();

    const userCanManage = hasPermission('editor');

    const handleDeleteClick = (video) => {
        setVideoToDelete(video);
        setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
    };

    const confirmDelete = () => {
        if (!videoToDelete) return;
        makeRequest({
            apiFunc: deleteVideo,
            data: videoToDelete._id,
            toast: toast,
            onSuccess: () => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)
        });
    };

    if (isLoadingVideos) return <CircularProgressBox text="Loading Videos..." />;

    return (
        <div className="p-4 md:p-6 space-y-6">
            <Heading
                level={1}
                title="Video Management"
                description={`Manage all ${videosData?.totalVideos || 0} uploaded videos`}
                actions={userCanManage && (<Button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/videos/upload')} icon={<Plus size={16} />}>Upload Video</Button>)}
            />

            {isError ? (
                <Card className="text-center p-8"><p className="text-red-500">{error?.data?.message || "An error occurred."}</p></Card>
            ) : videosData?.videos?.length > 0 ? (
                <>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
                        {videosData.videos.map((video) => (
                            <VideoCard
                                key={video._id}
                                video={video}
                                onEdit={() => navigate(`/dashboard/videos/edit/${video.slug}`)}
                                onDelete={() => handleDeleteClick(video)}
                                onActionComplete={refetch}
                            />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                    {videosData.totalPages > 1 && (
                        <Card>
                            <div className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span>Page {page} of {videosData.totalPages}</span>
                                <div className="space-x-2">
                                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handlers.handlePageChange(page - 1)} disabled={page === 1}>Previous</Button>
                                    <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => handlers.handlePageChange(page + 1)} disabled={page === videosData.totalPages}>Next</Button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </Card>
                    )}
                </>
            ) : (
                <Card className="text-center p-12">
                    <video autoPlay loop muted playsInline className="w-32 h-32 mx-auto"><source src="/assets/videos/no-data.webm" type="video/webm" /></video>
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-medium mt-4">No Videos Found</h3>
                    <p className="text-gray-500 mt-2">Get started by uploading your first video.</p>
                    <Button onClick={() => navigate('/dashboard/videos/upload')} className="mt-6">Upload Video</Button>
                </Card>
            )}

            <ConfirmModal
                isOpen={isDeleteModalOpen}
                onClose={() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false)}
                title="Confirm Delete Video"
                onConfirm={confirmDelete}
                isLoading={isDeleting}
                confirmButtonVariant="danger"
            >
                Are you sure you want to permanently delete "<strong>{videoToDelete?.title}</strong>"?
            </ConfirmModal>
        </div>
    );
};

export default VideoList;

// File: src/utils/formatters.js
export const formatDuration = (seconds) => {
    if (isNaN(seconds) || seconds < 0) return "N/A";
    const h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
    const m = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
    const s = Math.floor(seconds % 60);
    if (h > 0) return `${h}:${m.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${s.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
    return `${m}:${s.toString().padStart(2, '0')}`;
};
export const formatDate = (dateInput) => {
    if (!dateInput) return "N/A";
    try {
        const date = new Date(dateInput);
        if (isNaN(date.getTime())) return "Invalid Date";
        return date.toLocaleDateString("en-US", { year: "numeric", month: "short", day: "numeric" });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Error formatting date:", error); // Keep console.error for actual errors
        return "Invalid Date";
    }
};

// File: src/_core/Slices/advertisementSlice.js
import { apiSlice } from './apiSlice';

export const advertisementApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
    endpoints: (builder) => ({
        // Get active ads (public)
        getActiveAds: builder.query({
            query: (location) => ({
                url: '/ads',
                params: location ? { location } : undefined
            }),
            providesTags: (result = [], error, arg) => [
                'Advertisement',
                ...result.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Advertisement', id: _id }))
            ],
        }),

        // Get all ads (admin only)
        getAllAds: builder.query({
            query: () => '/ads/all',
            providesTags: (result = [], error, arg) => [
                'Advertisement',
                ...result.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Advertisement', id: _id }))
            ],
        }),

        // Create new ad (admin only)
        createAd: builder.mutation({
            query: (adData) => ({
                url: '/ads',
                method: 'POST',
                body: adData,
            }),
            invalidatesTags: ['Advertisement'],
        }),

        // Update ad (admin only)
        updateAd: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ id, adData }) => ({
                url: `/ads/${id}`,
                method: 'PUT',
                body: adData,
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, arg) => [
                { type: 'Advertisement', id: arg.id }
            ],
        }),

        // Delete ad (admin only)
        deleteAd: builder.mutation({
            query: (id) => ({
                url: `/ads/${id}`,
                method: 'DELETE',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, id) => [
                { type: 'Advertisement', id }
            ],
        }),

        // Toggle ad status (admin only)
        toggleAdStatus: builder.mutation({
            query: (id) => ({
                url: `/ads/${id}/toggle-status`,
                method: 'PATCH',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, id) => [
                { type: 'Advertisement', id }
            ],
        }),
    }),
});

export const {
    useGetActiveAdsQuery,
    useGetAllAdsQuery,
    useCreateAdMutation,
    useUpdateAdMutation,
    useDeleteAdMutation,
    useToggleAdStatusMutation,
} = advertisementApiSlice;

--- START OF FILE: src\_core\Slices\apiSlice.js ---

// File: src/_core/Slices/apiSlice.js
import { createApi, fetchBaseQuery } from '@reduxjs/toolkit/query/react';
import { logout } from './authSlice';
import { toast } from 'react-toastify';
import { BASE_URL } from '../../utils/ApiBaseUrl';
// Fallback to local URL if not set
const baseQuery = fetchBaseQuery({
    baseUrl: BASE_URL,

    prepareHeaders: (headers, { getState }) => {
        const token = getState().auth.userInfo?.token;
        if (token) {
            headers.set('authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
        }
        return headers;
    },
});
export const baseQueryWithReauth = async (args, api, extraOptions) => {
    const result = await baseQuery(args, api, extraOptions);

    if (result?.error?.status === 401) {
        // Auto logout logic
        toast.error('Session expired. Please log in again.');
        api.dispatch(logout());
        // Optionally redirect to login page
        window.location.href = '/';
    }

    return result;
};

export const apiSlice = createApi({
    reducerPath: 'api',
    baseQuery: baseQueryWithReauth,
    tagTypes: ['Article', 'Category', 'User', 'DashboardStats', 'Comment', 'Video', 'Gallery', 'Setting', 'Profile', 'LiveUpdate', 'LiveBlog'],
    endpoints: (builder) => ({}),
});
