// File: src/_core/Slices/articleSlice.js
import { apiSlice } from './apiSlice';

export const articleApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
    endpoints: (builder) => ({
        // Query to get all articles with filters
        getArticles: builder.query({
            query: (params) => ({ url: '/articles/admin/all', params }), // params will include page, limit, category, status, sortBy, sortOrder, searchQuery
            providesTags: (result) =>
                result && result.articles // Ensure result and result.articles exist
                    ? [...result.articles.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Article', id: _id })), { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }]
                    : [{ type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        getAdminArticlesBySlug: builder.query({
            query: (slug) => ({ url: `/articles/admin/${slug}` }), // params will include page, limit, category, status, sortBy, sortOrder, searchQuery
            providesTags: (result) =>
                result && result.articles // Ensure result and result.articles exist
                    ? [...result.articles.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Article', id: _id })), { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }]
                    : [{ type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        // Query to get a single article by ID
        getArticleBySlug: builder.query({
            query: (slug) => `/articles/${slug}`, // Using existing admin details path
            providesTags: (result, error, slug) => [{ type: 'Article', slug }],
        }),
        // Mutation to create a new article
        createArticle: builder.mutation({
            query: (articleData) => ({
                url: '/articles',
                method: 'POST',
                body: articleData, // This should be FormData if image uploads are involved
            }),
            invalidatesTags: [{ type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        // Mutation to update an article
        updateArticle: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ id, data }) => ({ // Changed articleId to id, articleData to data for consistency with example
                url: `/articles/${id}`,
                method: 'PUT',
                body: data, // This can be FormData or JSON
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { id }) => [{ type: 'Article', id }, { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        // Mutation to delete an article
        deleteArticle: builder.mutation({
            query: (id) => ({ // Changed articleId to id
                url: `/articles/${id}`,
                method: 'DELETE',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, id) => [{ type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }, { type: 'Article', id }], // Also invalidate the specific article
        }),
        // Query to get article history
        getArticleHistory: builder.query({
            query: (id) => `/articles/${id}/history`,
            providesTags: (result, error, id) => [{ type: 'Article', id: `${id}_HISTORY` }], // More specific tag
        }),
        // Mutation to revert an article to a specific history version
        revertArticle: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ articleId, historyId }) => ({
                url: `/articles/${articleId}/revert`,
                method: 'POST', // Assuming POST, adjust if backend uses PUT
                body: { historyId },
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { articleId }) => [
                { type: 'Article', id: articleId },
                { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' },
                { type: 'Article', id: `${articleId}_HISTORY` },
            ],
        }),
        // Mutation to add images to an article (assuming gallery or content images)
        addArticleImages: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ articleId, formData }) => ({
                url: `/articles/${articleId}/images`,
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData, // Expects FormData
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { articleId }) => [{ type: 'Article', id: articleId }],
        }),
        // Mutation to delete an image from an article
        deleteArticleImage: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ articleId, imageId }) => ({
                url: `/articles/${articleId}/images/${imageId}`,
                method: 'DELETE',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { articleId }) => [{ type: 'Article', id: articleId }],
        }),
        // Mutation to pin an article
        pinArticle: builder.mutation({
            query: (id) => ({ // Changed articleId to id
                url: `/articles/${id}/pin`,
                method: 'PUT', // Or POST, based on API
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, id) => [{ type: 'Article', id }, { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        toggleFeaturedStatus: builder.mutation({
            query: (articleId) => ({
                url: `/articles/${articleId}/toggle-featured`,
                method: 'PUT',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, articleId) => [{ type: 'Article', id: articleId }, { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        toggleHighlightStatus: builder.mutation({
            query: (articleId) => ({
                url: `/articles/${articleId}/toggle-highlight`,
                method: 'PUT',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, articleId) => [{ type: 'Article', id: articleId }, { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        toggleOpinion: builder.mutation({
            query: (articleId) => ({
                url: `/articles/${articleId}/toggle-opinion`,
                method: 'PUT',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, articleId) => [{ type: 'Article', id: articleId }, { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),

        toggleTopHeadlineStatus: builder.mutation({
            query: (articleId) => ({

                method: 'PUT',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, articleId) => [{ type: 'Article', id: articleId }, { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),

        toggleEditorChoiceStatus: builder.mutation({
            query: (articleId) => ({
                url: `/articles/${articleId}/toggle-editor-choice`,
                method: 'PUT',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, articleId) => [{ type: 'Article', id: articleId }, { type: 'Article', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        getOwnArticles: builder.query({
            query: (params) => ({
                url: '/articles/my-articles', // This is the protected route for the logged-in user
                params: params, // Will accept status, category, etc. for filtering
            }),
            // Use a specific tag for this list so it can be managed independently
            providesTags: (result) =>
                result?.articles
                    ? [...result.articles.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Article', id: _id })), { type: 'Article', id: 'OWN_LIST' }]
                    : [{ type: 'Article', id: 'OWN_LIST' }],
        }),
        updateArticleStatus: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ articleId, status }) => ({
                url: `/articles/${articleId}/status`,
                method: 'PUT',
                body: status,
            }),
            // providesTags: (result) =>
            //     result?.articles

            //         : [{ type: 'Article', id: 'OWN_LIST' }],
            invalidatesTags: ["Article"]
        }),

    }),
});

--- START OF FILE: src\_core\Slices\authApiSlice.js ---

import { toast } from "react-toastify";
import { apiSlice } from "./apiSlice";
import { setCredentials } from './authSlice';
export const authApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
    endpoints: (builder) => ({
        login: builder.mutation({
            query: (credentials) => ({
                url: '/auth/login', // Adjust if your backend route is different
                method: 'POST',
                body: credentials,
                invalidatesTags: (result, error, id) => [
                    { type: "Profile", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
                    { type: "DashboardStats", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
                    { type: "User", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
                ],// e.g., { email, password }
            }),
            async onQueryStarted(arg, { dispatch, queryFulfilled }) {
                try {
                    const { data } = await queryFulfilled;

                    // Pehle ki tarah user aur token nikalein
                    let userToStore = data.user || data.data?.user;
                    let tokenToStore = data.token || data.data?.token;

                    if (userToStore && tokenToStore) {
                        // **YAHAN HAI FIX**
                        // Ek naya object banayein jo 'user' aur 'token' keys ke saath ho
                        const finalPayload = {
                            user: userToStore,
                            token: tokenToStore,
                        };
                        // Ab is sahi structure wale object ko dispatch karein
                        dispatch(setCredentials(finalPayload));
                    } else {
                        toast("Login response missing user or token:", data);
                    }
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('Login API error in onQueryStarted:', error);
                }
            },
            // invalidatesTags: ['User', 'Profile'], // Optional: if login should refetch user/profile
        }),
        signup: builder.mutation({
            query: (userData) => ({
                url: '/auth/register', // Adjust if your backend route is different
                method: 'POST',
                body: userData, // e.g., { fullName, email, password, username }
            }),
            async onQueryStarted(arg, { dispatch, queryFulfilled }) {
                try {
                    const { data } = await queryFulfilled;
                    // If backend returns user and token upon successful signup (auto-login)
                    let userToStore = data.user || data.data?.user;
                    let tokenToStore = data.token || data.data?.token;

                    if (userToStore && tokenToStore) {
                        const finalUserPayload = { ...userToStore, token: tokenToStore };
                        dispatch(setCredentials(finalUserPayload));
                    }
                    // If no auto-login, this onQueryStarted might just log success
                    // or do nothing specific for auth state. Component handles navigation.
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('Signup API error in onQueryStarted:', error);
                }
            },
        }),
        sendOtp: builder.mutation({
            query: (data) => ({ // data: { email }
                url: '/auth/send-otp',
                method: 'POST',
                body: data,
            }),
        }),
        verifyOtp: builder.mutation({
            query: (data) => ({ // data: { email, otp }
                url: '/auth/verify-otp',
                method: 'POST',
                body: data,
            }),
        }),
        resetPassword: builder.mutation({
            query: (data) => ({ // data: { email, password, (otp might be implicit via session/token or needed here) }
                url: '/auth/reset-password',
                method: 'POST',
                body: data,
            }),
        }),
        forgotPassword: builder.mutation({ // For logged-in users
            query: (credentials) => ({ // credentials: { oldPassword, newPassword }
                url: '/auth/forgot-password', // This matches ProfilePage's usage
                method: 'POST', // Or PUT, consistent with userSlice.js if it was defined there
                body: credentials,
            }),
        }),
    }),
});

export const {
    useLoginMutation,
    useSignupMutation,
    useSendOtpMutation,
    useVerifyOtpMutation,
    useResetPasswordMutation,
    useForgotPasswordMutation,
} = authApiSlice;

--- START OF FILE: src\_core\Slices\authSlice.js ---

import { createSlice } from '@reduxjs/toolkit';
import { apiSlice } from './apiSlice'; // Import apiSlice

const initialState = {
    userInfo: localStorage.getItem('userInfo')
        ? JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('userInfo'))
        : null,
};

// Existing slice for synchronous actions
const authSlice = createSlice({
    name: 'auth',
    initialState,
    reducers: {
        setCredentials: (state, action) => {
            state.userInfo = action.payload;
            localStorage.setItem('userInfo', JSON.stringify(action.payload));
            // Potentially set a cookie here as well if needed for SSR or other purposes
        },
        logout: (state) => {
            state.userInfo = null;
            localStorage.removeItem('userInfo');
            // Potentially remove cookie here
            // Also, you might want to dispatch apiSlice.util.resetApiState() here
            // to clear out all cached data from RTK Query upon logout.
            // Example: dispatch(apiSlice.util.resetApiState()); (would need dispatch)
        },
    },
});

export const { setCredentials, logout } = authSlice.actions;
export default authSlice.reducer;

// New section for API endpoints related to auth

import { apiSlice } from './apiSlice';

export const categoriesApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
    endpoints: (builder) => ({
        // Get all categories
        getCategories: builder.query({
            query: (params) => ({ url: '/categories', params }),
            providesTags: (result) =>
                result?.categories
                    ? [
                        ...result.categories.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Category', id: _id })),
                        { type: 'Category', id: 'LIST' }, // Global invalidation tag
                    ]
                    : [{ type: 'Category', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),

        // Get a single category by ID
        getCategoryById: builder.query({
            query: (id) => `/categories/${id}`,
            providesTags: (result, error, id) => [{ type: 'Category', id }],
        }),

        // Create a new category
        createCategory: builder.mutation({
            query: (categoryData) => ({
                url: '/categories',
                method: 'POST',
                body: categoryData,
            }),
            invalidatesTags: [
                { type: 'Category', id: 'LIST' }, // Forces refetch of ALL categories
            ],
        }),

        // Update a category
        updateCategory: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ id, data }) => ({
                url: `/categories/${id}`,
                method: 'PUT',
                body: data,
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { id }) => [
                { type: 'Category', id }, // Invalidates the specific category
                { type: 'Category', id: 'LIST' }, // Invalidates the full list
            ],
        }),

        // Delete a category
        deleteCategory: builder.mutation({
            query: (id) => ({
                url: `/categories/${id}`,
                method: 'DELETE',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, id) => [
                { type: 'Category', id }, // Invalidates the deleted category
                { type: 'Category', id: 'LIST' }, // Invalidates the full list
            ],
        }),
    }),
});

export const {
    useGetCategoriesQuery,
    useGetCategoryByIdQuery,
    useCreateCategoryMutation,
    useUpdateCategoryMutation,

--- START OF FILE: src\_core\Slices\commentSlice.js ---

import { apiSlice } from './apiSlice';

export const commentApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
    endpoints: (builder) => ({
        // Query to get all comments for the moderation page
        // Params can include status (e.g., 'pending-review', 'approved')
        getComments: builder.query({
            query: (params) => ({
                url: '/comments/all',
                params: params, // e.g., { status: 'pending-review', page: 1, limit: 10 }
            }),
            // Provides tags for caching. 'LIST' is for the overall list, individual IDs for specific comments.
            providesTags: (result) =>
                result?.comments
                    ? [...result.comments.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Comment', id: _id })), { type: 'Comment', id: 'LIST' }]
                    : [{ type: 'Comment', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),

        // Mutation to update a comment's status (for approving/unapproving)
        updateCommentStatus: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ commentId, isApproved }) => ({
                url: `/comments/${commentId}/status`,
                method: 'PUT',
                body: { isApproved },
            }),
            // Invalidates the specific comment's cache and the entire list to force a refetch.
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { commentId }) => [{ type: 'Comment', id: commentId }, { type: 'Comment', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),

        // Mutation to delete a comment (by a moderator)
        deleteComment: builder.mutation({
            query: (commentId) => ({
                url: `/comments/${commentId}`,
                method: 'DELETE',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, commentId) => [{ type: 'Comment', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),

        // Query to get comments for a public article page (not used on this page, but good to have in the slice)
        getPublicArticleComments: builder.query({
            query: (articleId) => `/comments/article/${articleId}`,
            providesTags: (result, error, articleId) => [{ type: 'Comment', id: `ARTICLE_${articleId}_LIST` }],
        }),
    }),
});

// Export the auto-generated hooks for use in components
export const {
    useGetCommentsQuery,
    useUpdateCommentStatusMutation,
    useDeleteCommentMutation,
    useGetPublicArticleCommentsQuery, // Not used here, but available for the frontend
} = commentApiSlice;

import { apiSlice } from './apiSlice';

export const liveBlogApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
    endpoints: (builder) => ({
        // === ADMIN DASHBOARD ENDPOINTS ===
        getLiveBlogs: builder.query({
            query: (params) => ({ url: '/live-blogs', params }),
            providesTags: (result) =>
                result?.blogs
                    ? [...result.blogs.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'LiveBlog', id: _id })), { type: 'LiveBlog', id: 'LIST' }]
                    : [{ type: 'LiveBlog', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        getLiveBlogById: builder.query({
            query: (id) => `/live-blogs/${id}`,
            providesTags: (result, error, id) => [{ type: 'LiveBlog', id }],
        }),
        createLiveBlog: builder.mutation({
            query: (newBlogData) => ({ url: '/live-blogs', method: 'POST', body: newBlogData }),
            invalidatesTags: [{ type: 'LiveBlog', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        updateLiveBlog: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ id, data }) => ({ url: `/live-blogs/${id}`, method: 'PUT', body: data }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { id }) => [{ type: 'LiveBlog', id }, { type: 'LiveBlog', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        deleteLiveBlog: builder.mutation({
            query: (id) => ({ url: `/live-blogs/${id}`, method: 'DELETE' }),
            invalidatesTags: [{ type: 'LiveBlog', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),

        // === PUBLIC-FACING ENDPOINTS ===
        getPublicLiveBlogs: builder.query({
            query: (params) => ({
                url: '/live-blogs/public', // Assuming a public route like this exists on the backend
                params: params, // e.g., { status: 'live' }
            }),
            providesTags: [{ type: 'LiveBlog', id: 'PUBLIC_LIST' }],
        }),
        getLiveBlogBySlug: builder.query({
            query: (slug) => `/live-blogs/view/${slug}`, // Public route to view by slug
            providesTags: (result, error, slug) => [{ type: 'LiveBlog', slug }],
        }),

        // === LIVE UPDATE ENDPOINTS (used by both dashboard and public pages) ===
        getLiveUpdates: builder.query({
            query: ({ liveBlogId, ...params }) => ({
                url: '/live-updates',
                params: { liveBlogId, ...params },
            }),
            providesTags: (result, error, { liveBlogId }) => [{ type: 'LiveUpdate', id: liveBlogId }],
        }),
        createLiveUpdate: builder.mutation({
            query: (newUpdateContent) => ({
                url: '/live-updates',
                method: 'POST',
                body: newUpdateContent,
            }),
            // Invalidation is mainly handled by Pusher, but this is a good fallback
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { liveBlogId }) => [{ type: 'LiveUpdate', id: liveBlogId }],
        }),

        updateLiveUpdate: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ id, ...content }) => ({
                url: `/live-updates/${id}`,
                method: 'PUT',
                body: content
            }),

            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { liveBlogId }) => [{ type: 'LiveUpdate', id: liveBlogId }],
        }),

        deleteLiveUpdate: builder.mutation({
            query: (id) => ({
                url: `/live-updates/${id}`,
                method: 'DELETE',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { liveBlogId }) => [{ type: 'LiveUpdate', id: liveBlogId }],
        }),
    }),
});

// Export all hooks
export const {
    useGetLiveBlogsQuery,
    useGetLiveBlogByIdQuery,
    useCreateLiveBlogMutation,
    useUpdateLiveBlogMutation,
    useDeleteLiveBlogMutation,
    useGetLiveUpdatesQuery,
    useCreateLiveUpdateMutation,
    useGetPublicLiveBlogsQuery,
    useGetLiveBlogBySlugQuery,
    useUpdateLiveUpdateMutation,
    useDeleteLiveUpdateMutation
} = liveBlogApiSlice;

// File: src/_core/Slices/storySlice.js
import { apiSlice } from './apiSlice';

export const storyApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
    endpoints: (builder) => ({
        // Get all active stories
        getStories: builder.query({
            query: () => '/stories',
            providesTags: (result = [], error, arg) => [
                'Story',
                ...result.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Story', id: _id }))
            ],
        }),

        // Get stories by user ID
        getUserStories: builder.query({
            query: (userId) => `/stories/user/${userId}`,
            providesTags: (result = [], error, arg) => [
                'Story',
                ...result.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Story', id: _id }))
            ],
        }),

        // Create new story
        createStory: builder.mutation({
            query: (storyData) => ({
                url: '/stories',
                method: 'POST',
                body: storyData,
                credentials: 'include',
                formData: true, // Important for file uploads
            }),
            invalidatesTags: ['Story'],
        }),

        // Mark story as viewed
        viewStory: builder.mutation({
            query: (itemId) => ({
                url: `/stories/${itemId}`,
                method: 'POST',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, arg) => [
                { type: 'Story', id: arg }
            ],
        }),

        // Delete story
        deleteStory: builder.mutation({
            query: (itemId) => ({
                url: `/stories/${itemId}`,
                method: 'DELETE',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, arg) => [
                { type: 'Story', id: arg }
            ],
        }),

        // Get story views analytics (admin only)
        getStoryAnalytics: builder.query({
            query: ({ startDate, endDate }) => ({
                url: '/stories/analytics',
                params: { startDate, endDate },
            }),
            providesTags: ['StoryAnalytics'],
        }),
    }),
});

export const {
    useGetStoriesQuery,
    useGetUserStoriesQuery,
    useCreateStoryMutation,
    useViewStoryMutation,
    useDeleteStoryMutation,
    useGetStoryAnalyticsQuery,
} = storyApiSlice;

// File: src/_core/Slices/userSlice.js
import { apiSlice } from "./apiSlice";

export const userApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
  endpoints: (builder) => ({
    // Query to get all users
    getUsers: builder.query({
      query: (params) => ({ url: "/users", params }), // Example: /users/admin/all, adjust as per backend
      providesTags: (result) =>
        result && result.users // Assuming the response has a 'users' array
          ? [
            ...result.users.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: "User", id: _id })),
            { type: "User", id: "LIST" },
          ]
          : [{ type: "User", id: "LIST" }],
    }),
    // Query to get a single user by ID
    getUserById: builder.query({
      query: (id) => `/users/${id}`, // Example: /users/admin/:id, adjust as per backend
      providesTags: (result, error, id) => [{ type: "User", id }],
    }),
    // Mutation to create a new user (e.g., by admin)
    createUser: builder.mutation({
      query: (userData) => ({
        url: "/users", // Example: /users/admin/create, adjust as per backend
        method: "POST",
        body: userData,
      }),
      invalidatesTags: [{ type: "User", id: "LIST" }],
    }),
    // Mutation to update a user (e.g., by admin or user themselves)
    updateUser: builder.mutation({
      query: ({ id, data }) => ({
        url: `/users/${id}`, // Example: /users/admin/:id, adjust as per backend
        method: "PUT",
        body: data,
      }),
      invalidatesTags: (result, error, { id }) => [
        { type: "User", id },
        { type: "User", id: "LIST" },
      ],
    }),
    // Mutation to delete a user (e.g., by admin)
    deleteUser: builder.mutation({
      query: (id) => ({
        url: `/users/${id}`, // Example: /users/admin/:id, adjust as per backend
        method: "DELETE",
      }),
      invalidatesTags: (result, error, id) => [
        { type: "User", id },
        { type: "User", id: "LIST" },
      ],
    }),

    // Profile related endpoints (to be used in ProfilePage)
    getProfile: builder.query({
      query: () => "/users/profile", // Adjust as per your backend route for fetching logged-in user's profile
      providesTags: (result, error, id) => [
        { type: "Profile", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
        { type: "User", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
      ],
    }),
    updateProfile: builder.mutation({
      query: (profileData) => ({
        url: "/users/profile", // Adjust as per your backend route
        method: "PUT",
        body: profileData, // Can be FormData if avatar upload is included
      }),
      invalidatesTags: (result, error, id) => [
        { type: "Profile", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
        { type: "User", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
      ],
    }),
    updateAvatar: builder.mutation({
      query: (formData) => ({
        url: "/users/profile",
        method: "PUT",
        body: formData,
      }),
      invalidatesTags: (result) => [
        { type: "Profile", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
        { type: "User", id: result?._id || "CURRENT" },
      ],
    }),

    // Bookmarks, Followed Categories, Followed Authors (placeholders, specific endpoints TBD based on backend)
    getBookmarks: builder.query({
      query: (params) => ({ url: "/users/profile/bookmarks", params }), // Example endpoint
      providesTags: (result) =>
        result
          ? [{ type: "Profile", id: "BOOKMARKS_LIST" }]
          : [{ type: "Profile", id: "BOOKMARKS_LIST" }],
    }),
    unbookmarkArticle: builder.mutation({
      query: (articleId) => ({
        url: `/users/profile/bookmarks/${articleId}`, // Example endpoint
        method: "DELETE",
      }),
      invalidatesTags: [{ type: "Profile", id: "BOOKMARKS_LIST" }],
    }),

    getFollowedCategories: builder.query({
      query: (params) => ({
        url: "/users/profile/followed-categories",
        params,
      }), // Example endpoint
      providesTags: (result) =>
        result
          ? [{ type: "Profile", id: "FOLLOWED_CATEGORIES_LIST" }]
          : [{ type: "Profile", id: "FOLLOWED_CATEGORIES_LIST" }],
    }),
    unfollowCategory: builder.mutation({
      query: (categoryId) => ({
        url: `/users/profile/followed-categories/${categoryId}`, // Example endpoint
        method: "DELETE",
      }),
      invalidatesTags: [{ type: "Profile", id: "FOLLOWED_CATEGORIES_LIST" }],
    }),

    getFollowedAuthors: builder.query({
      query: (params) => ({ url: "/users/profile/followed-authors", params }), // Example endpoint
      providesTags: (result) =>
        result
          ? [{ type: "Profile", id: "FOLLOWED_AUTHORS_LIST" }]
          : [{ type: "Profile", id: "FOLLOWED_ AUTHORS_LIST" }],
    }),
    unfollowAuthor: builder.mutation({
      query: (authorId) => ({
        url: `/users/profile/followed-authors/${authorId}`, // Example endpoint
        method: "DELETE",
      }),
      invalidatesTags: [{ type: "Profile", id: "FOLLOWED_AUTHORS_LIST" }],
    }),

    // Mutation to change user password
    changePassword: builder.mutation({
      query: (credentials) => ({
        // credentials: { oldPassword, newPassword }
        url: "/auth/reset-password", // As specified in new plan
        method: "POST",
        body: credentials,
      }),
      // Consider invalidating 'User' or 'Profile' tags if a password change
      // should force re-fetch of user data, though often not required.
      // invalidatesTags: [{ type: 'User', id: 'PROFILE' }, { type: 'Profile', id: 'CURRENT' }],
    }),
    // Add other user-specific mutations if needed (e.g., role update)
    createRoleRequest: builder.mutation({
      query: (data) => ({
        url: '/role-requests',
        method: 'POST',
        body: data, // { requestedRole, reason }
      }),
      invalidatesTags: ['RoleRequests'],
    }),

    getAllRoleRequests: builder.query({
      query: (params) => ({ // { status, page, limit }
        url: '/role-requests/all',
        params,
      }),
      providesTags: ['RoleRequests'],
    }),

    processRoleRequest: builder.mutation({
      query: ({ requestId, data }) => ({
        url: `/role-requests/${requestId}/process`,
        method: 'PUT',
        body: data, // { status, adminNotes }
      }),
      invalidatesTags: ['RoleRequests'],
    }),

    getMyRoleRequests: builder.query({
      query: () => '/role-requests/my-requests',
      providesTags: ['RoleRequests'],
    }),
  }),
});

export const {
  useGetUsersQuery,
  useGetUserByIdQuery,
  useCreateUserMutation,
  useUpdateUserMutation,
  useUpdateAvatarMutation,
  useDeleteUserMutation,
  useGetProfileQuery,
  useUpdateProfileMutation,
  useGetBookmarksQuery,
  useUnbookmarkArticleMutation,
  useGetFollowedCategoriesQuery,
  useUnfollowCategoryMutation,
  useGetFollowedAuthorsQuery,
  useUnfollowAuthorMutation,
  useChangePasswordMutation, // Added new hook

  useCreateRoleRequestMutation,
  useGetAllRoleRequestsQuery,
  useProcessRoleRequestMutation,
  useGetMyRoleRequestsQuery,
} = userApiSlice;

--- START OF FILE: src\_core\Slices\videoSlice.js ---

import { apiSlice } from './apiSlice';

export const videoApiSlice = apiSlice.injectEndpoints({
    endpoints: (builder) => ({
        getVideos: builder.query({
            query: (params) => ({ url: '/videos', params }),
            providesTags: (result) =>
                result?.videos
                    ? [...result.videos.map(({ _id }) => ({ type: 'Video', id: _id })), { type: 'Video', id: 'LIST' }]
                    : [{ type: 'Video', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        getVideoById: builder.query({
            query: (slug) => `/videos/${slug}`,
            providesTags: (result, error, slug) => [{ type: 'Video', id: result?._id }],
        }),
        deleteVideo: builder.mutation({
            query: (id) => ({ url: `/videos/${id}`, method: 'DELETE' }),
            invalidatesTags: [{ type: 'Video', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        updateVideo: builder.mutation({
            query: ({ id, data }) => ({ url: `/videos/${id}`, method: 'PUT', body: data }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, { id }) => [{ type: 'Video', id }, { type: 'Video', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),


        generateVideoSignature: builder.mutation({
            query: () => ({
                url: '/videos/generate-signature',
                method: 'POST',
            }),
        }),

        uploadVideoToCloudinary: builder.mutation({
            queryFn: async ({ file, signatureData }) => {
                const formData = new FormData();
                formData.append('file', file);
                formData.append('api_key', signatureData.api_key);
                formData.append('timestamp', signatureData.timestamp);
                formData.append('signature', signatureData.signature);
                formData.append('upload_preset', 'newshub-videos');

                try {
                    const response = await fetch(`https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${signatureData.cloud_name}/video/upload`, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        body: formData,
                    });
                    const data = await response.json();
                    if (!response.ok) return { error: { data } };
                    return { data };
                } catch (error) {
                    return { error: { data: { message: error.message } } };
                }
            },
        }),


        saveVideoDetails: builder.mutation({
            query: (videoDetails) => ({
                url: '/videos/save-details',
                method: 'POST',
                body: videoDetails,
            }),
            invalidatesTags: [{ type: 'Video', id: 'LIST' }],
        }),
        createVideoFromUrl: builder.mutation({
            query: (videoData) => ({
                url: '/videos/create-from-url',
                method: 'POST',
                body: videoData, // e.g., { title, videoUrl, description, tags, ... }
            })
        }),
        toggleVideoHeadline: builder.mutation({
            query: (videoId) => ({
                url: `/videos/${videoId}/toggle-headline`,
                method: 'PUT',
            }),
            invalidatesTags: (result, error, videoId) => [{ type: 'Video', id: videoId }, { type: 'Video', id: 'LIST' }],
        })
    })
});

export const {
    useGetVideosQuery,
    useGetVideoByIdQuery,
    useDeleteVideoMutation,
    useUpdateVideoMutation,
    useGenerateVideoSignatureMutation,
    useUploadVideoToCloudinaryMutation,
    useSaveVideoDetailsMutation,
    useCreateVideoFromUrlMutation,
        useToggleVideoHeadlineMutation
} = videoApiSlice;
